# Spec 1 W6 (holds enforced, recycled numbers, date-of-birth correction and age signals, child safety) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Make account holds bite and close the recycled-number gap (W6a: migration 11 as three files, the Custom Access Token Hook `public.attune_access_token_hook` with counted and alerted fail-opens, the hold kind in `get_my_home_state`, the sealed dormancy challenge, DM1 and DM2, `start-fresh-account`, `purge-recovery-holds`, `identity-health-alert`, H1 from the sign-in verify), let a person fix a wrong date of birth once and read the platform's age signal at A2 (W6b: migration 12, Settings -> Date of birth, Apple Declared Age Range and Play Age Signals), and give everyone "Report a concern about a child" (W6c: migration 13, the report screen from Settings -> Safety and Support, the child-safety address).

**Architecture:** Three sub-waves, ten tasks. **W6a (Tasks 1-5).** Task 1 is migrations 11a (`holds_enforced`: `get_my_home_state` re-created from its live body with `hold_kind`; `authenticated` loses DELETE on `users`, W1 M-6) and 11b (`access_token_hook`: `account_activity` seeded from Auth's own times, `fresh_start_challenges`, `private.auth_hook_failures`, `users.parked_phone_hmac`, the AES-sealed nonce helpers, `private.access_token_refusal`, and the hook that fails open on any error and records it), proven by `dormancy_contracts.sql` sections A-C with eight mutants. Task 2 is migration 11c (`fresh_start`: `consume_fresh_start` writing the real number's HMAC before anything parks it, `mark_fresh_start_parked`, the purge list, the alerts, `get_identity_health` re-created as its W1 body `|| private.identity_health_w6a()`, the retention and the two edge crons), sections A2 and D-G, six mutants. Task 3 is the three edge functions with Deno tests and `config.toml`. Task 4 is the phone's hold kind (only an age hold, or one whose kind isn't known, blocks the device) and the hook-refusal parser in `passwordless_auth_service.dart`. Task 5 is the sign-in side: the coordinator's DM1/H1 outcomes and the fresh start, DM1 and DM2, H1 from the verify with the device block for an age hold, the copy, goldens. **W6b (Tasks 6-8).** Task 6 is migration 12 (`correct_my_birth_date`, `get_my_birth_date_status`) and section H. Task 7 is the platform age signal at A2 (Dart, Swift in `AppDelegate.swift`, Kotlin in `MainActivity.kt`, the weak link, the entitlement, Play Age Signals 0.0.4). Task 8 is Settings -> Date of birth. **W6c (Tasks 9-10).** Task 9 is migration 13 (`child_safety_reports`, `report_child_safety_concern`, the alert pair), `child_safety_contracts.sql` and the alert source in `identity-health-alert`. Task 10 is the report screen, both Settings rows, the address, then the W6 regression gate. Every SQL, Dart, Deno, Swift and Kotlin block below was run while writing this plan: the SQL on a scratch Postgres 17 cluster restored from `attune_spec3a`'s dump (each migration applied twice, `exit=0` both times; each contract red before its migration with the message quoted and green after; each SQL mutant red with the message quoted; the whole contract suite with no new FAIL against the baseline), the Dart in a scratch copy of the tree (every new test red before its code, green after; goldens generated and looked at; the analyzer clean on every touched file; the whole suite), the Deno tests and `deno check` of every new function, the Swift `AgeSignalReader` type-checked and linked against the iOS 26.1 SDK at deployment target 14.0 (strong `LC_LOAD_DYLIB` without the flag, `LC_LOAD_WEAK_DYLIB` with it), the AppDelegate channel code type-checked against Flutter-shaped stubs, the Play Age Signals 0.0.4 class signatures read with `javap` from the published AAR, and `flutter build apk --debug` built with the Kotlin reader and the new dependency (the iOS app build itself is Task 7 Step 6's).

**Tech Stack:** Flutter 3.29.1 (Dart 3.7), Riverpod 2.6, GoRouter, Supabase Postgres 17 (RLS, `SECURITY DEFINER` with `search_path = ''`, pg_cron, pgcrypto in `extensions`, Vault through `public.app_setting`), Supabase CLI 2.75, Deno 2, Swift 5 (Xcode 26.1, iOS 26.1 SDK), Kotlin (AGP from the repo), psql, Python 3 (edit and check scripts). One new dependency: `com.google.android.play:age-signals:0.0.4` (spec §5.1, W6b). No new Dart package.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-24-app-flow-identity-design.md` — §1.3-§1.5, §2 (copy, motion, §2.3 accessibility, §2.5 `dormancy_rule` and `platform_age_signals`), §3.3 A2 and A4 (hook outcomes), §3.5 (DM1, DM2, `dormancy_rule`), §3.6, §3.15 (H1, Settings -> Date of birth, "Report a concern about a child"), §4.2 (`users.parked_phone_hmac`, `account_holds`, `account_activity`, `fresh_start_challenges`, `private.auth_hook_failures`, `child_safety_reports`), §4.3 W6a, W6b, W6c, §4.4 `attune_access_token_hook`, §4.5 (`start-fresh-account`, `purge-recovery-holds`, `identity-health-alert`, the auth settings note), §4.6, §4.7 #11-#13, §4.8, §5.1-§5.8 rows tagged W6*, §6.1, §6.3, §7.1 (`dormancy_contracts.sql`, `child_safety_contracts.sql`), §7.4 ("Hook returns the event for a dormant OTP"), §7.5, §8 W6a-c and their gates, §8.1, §9, §11 (S-8, I-11, F-2, F-4), and the owner approval: **O3, no** (a parked account's live Dating restrictions do not follow a recycled number; Spec 1 changes nothing for it). Owner rulings: `docs/superpowers/research/2026-10-24-flow-dating-research/11-synthesis-decisions.md` §12. Format reference: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-02-spec1-w5-about-us.md`.

## Global Constraints

- Work in the main working tree on `main` (`/Users/user/attune`). W1 (360db8d1), W2 (8b3477ad), W3 (c211b262), W4 (6b8fc7ed) and W5 (b020e7d1) are committed; the owner may have uncommitted edits (`supabase/.temp/cli-latest`, `backup-before-planning-p2.sql` at the time of writing): never revert, stash, stage, checkout or reformat them. Every edit below is an exact-string replacement; if the text to replace has moved because a later fix changed it, apply the same change to the current text and say so in the task report.
- **NO commits.** No `git add`, `git commit`, `git stash`, `git checkout`, `git reset`, `git rm`. Tasks end with "Run tests", not "Commit".
- Never `dart format` a pre-existing file (the repo predates the 3.7 formatter). Format only the files this plan creates, by path (Task 10, Step 8 lists them).
- **SQL:** contracts run only on the local scratch database `attune_spec3a`, only as `psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f <file>`, inside the file's own `BEGIN … ROLLBACK`. Confirm the target first (`psql -X -d attune_spec3a -Atc 'select current_database()'` prints `attune_spec3a`). NEVER `attune_test`. NEVER run `scripts/local_pg_setup.sh`, `scripts/local_pg_grants.sql` or `scripts/local_pg_bootstrap.sql`; Tasks 1, 2, 6 and 9 **edit** `scripts/local_pg_grants.sql` and Task 1 **edits** `scripts/local_pg_bootstrap.sql` (the `supabase_auth_admin` stub, W2 P-30), never running either. The one cluster-level statement this plan runs is Task 1 Step 1's guarded `CREATE ROLE supabase_auth_admin` (P-32).
- **Migrations:** `20261103100000_holds_enforced.sql` + `…100010_…_grants.sql`, `20261103100100_access_token_hook.sql` + `…100110_…_grants.sql` (Task 1), `20261103100200_fresh_start.sql` + `…100210_…_grants.sql` (Task 2), `20261103110000_birth_date_correction.sql` + `…110010_…_grants.sql` (Task 6), `20261103120000_child_safety_reports.sql` + `…120010_…_grants.sql` (Task 9), later than every migration in the tree (the newest is W5's `20261102110110_couple_profiles_links_grants.sql`). If `ls supabase/migrations | sort | tail -1` shows anything at or after `20261103100000` when Task 1 starts, shift all ten to the next free hour, keeping their order, and change every header, `\i` line and command below to match. Each file is one `BEGIN; … COMMIT;`, idempotent (applied twice, `exit=0` both times), `SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s'`, every `SECURITY DEFINER` function has `SET search_path = ''` and schema-qualifies every name, server-only objects are revoked inside the migration itself and again in the twin, grants are explicit, nothing reaches `anon`; the hook is executable by `supabase_auth_admin` only (revoked from PUBLIC, `anon`, `authenticated` and `service_role`); `service_role` gets DML on every new public table (`account_activity`, `fresh_start_challenges`, `child_safety_reports`). No `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY`.
- **Re-created functions** start from their live latest bodies, found with `grep -l` and checked identical to `pg_get_functiondef`: `get_my_home_state` (`20261025100300`, Task 1 adds one key) and `get_identity_health` (`20261025100500`, Task 2 adds `|| private.identity_health_w6a()` and nothing else). Each task diffs the body.
- **Flags:** W6 adds no flag. `dormancy_rule` (read by the hook through `private.spec1_flag_on`) and `platform_age_signals` (read at A2 through the anon `get_public_app_config`) exist from W1c, inserted false, each with its production gate row; `supabase/sql/dev_enable_spec1_flags.sql` already turns both on in development and is not edited. Holds are security and are never behind a flag (§2.5): the hook refuses held accounts whatever the flags say. The DOB correction and the child-safety report are unflagged (§2.5 lists no flag for them).
- **Never touch:** `lib/features/games/**`, `ChatScreen`'s Games entry points, `UniversalBubble` / `ChatTextField` parameters, `lib/features/auth/utility/auth_exports.dart`, every Games route in `app_router.dart` (`test/architecture/games_untouched_test.dart` stays green), `lib/features/opinions/**` (Opinions stays as it is), `ATTUNE_SOUL.md`, `ATTUNE_THESIS.md`, every applied migration, `supabase/sql/dev_enable_spec1_flags.sql`, the legacy `OnboardingGate`, the existing SQL contracts (`identity_foundation_contracts.sql` included: P-11 keeps its I4 scan green without an edit). HomeScreen's latch block (from `if (isAuthenticated && !_gateData.isComplete) {` to `_redirectingToOnboarding = false;`) stays byte-identical (Task 1 saves it, Task 10 diffs it); `lib/home/home_screen.dart` is not edited at all.
- **Safety system rules:** nothing in W6 depends on an LLM (the hook, the challenge, the holds, the correction and the report are SQL and plain code); safety notifications go to the at-risk person only (W6 sends no push to anyone; the two webhook posts go to the team's `SAFETY_ALERT_WEBHOOK`, never to a user, and carry counts and times only); nothing tells the reported person (`child_safety_reports` has no column for them, contract A pins the column list); quick exit works everywhere (the app shell's `QuickExitZone` sits above every route W6 adds; W6 registers no quick-exit wipe and touches no protective key).
- **Shared phones (D8, D20; W2-W5 rules still apply):** the dormancy nonce lives in memory only, never in prefs (Task 5 test); H1 at the sign-in verify drops the draft before anything else; the device block follows an age hold only, so a safety or recovery hold never stops the next person on the phone; nothing about the old account of a recycled number is shown (DM1 test); the report screen keeps nothing on the phone; the date of birth is never shown, cached or returned (contract H, Task 8 test). No key or value names Dating; the report's "Dating" chip shows only to someone who uses Dating (P-27).
- **Copy:** every new string is an English key appended to `lib/i10n/app_en.arb` (`flow_*` for sign-in and Settings -> Date of birth, `support_*` for the report) by W3's `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-26-spec1-w3/arb_append.py`, then generated with `flutter gen-l10n --arb-dir=lib/i10n --template-arb-file=app_en.arb --output-dir=lib/i10n/generated --output-localization-file=app_localizations.dart --no-synthetic-package`; other locales fall back to English. The fragments here are pure ASCII (no en dash, no curly quote, no ellipsis), so no `\uXXXX` escape is needed; each task checks that with `grep -cP '[^\x00-\x7F]' <fragment>` (prints `0`). Should a later copy fix need a non-ASCII character, write it as a JSON `\uXXXX` escape by script and verify it with `grep -P`, never by hand. Never "they" for the partner (W6 copy names no partner), never "both of you". Dates shown to people use `MyDateFormat` only (Task 8's "until {date}" uses `MyDateFormat.toDate`).
- **UI:** Material 3 from the app theme and W2's flow widgets (`FlowFrame` wraps `AllowLargeText`; `FlowPrimaryButton`, `FlowSecondaryButton`, `FlowTextLink`, `FlowErrorLine`); every control labelled, targets at least 48 dp, text contrast at least 4.5:1, errors announced and shown with an icon, goldens at 1.0, 1.3, 2.0 and right to left (`expectFlowGoldens`), `androidTapTargetGuideline`, `labeledTapTargetGuideline` and `textContrastGuideline` (`expectFlowGuidelines`) on every new screen. DM1, DM2, H1, the date-of-birth screens and the report are never animated (§2.2).
- **No deploys.** `supabase db push`, function deploys, Vault secrets, the dashboard's auth hooks, the store consoles and the CSAE page are the owner's ("Owner deploy checklist"). Don't read `.env*`, credentials or the keychain. No `flutter pub get` (no Dart package changes); Task 7's Android build fetches the one Gradle dependency.
- **Regression gate at the end of each sub-wave (Tasks 5, 8, 10):** every SQL contract on `attune_spec3a` shows no new FAIL against the Task 1 baseline, and `dormancy_contracts.sql` (and from Task 9 `child_safety_contracts.sql`), `identity_foundation_contracts.sql`, `invite_v2_contracts.sql`, `couple_profile_contracts.sql`, `otp_delivery_contracts.sql` and `account_deletion_contracts.sql` are PASS; the Deno tests of the three new functions and `accept-invite` pass; the full `flutter test` passes; `flutter analyze --no-pub lib test` shows no `error` line that the Task 1 baseline didn't; the Games test passes; no Opinions, Games or `auth_exports.dart` change; the latch is unchanged.

## Review Focus

1. **A real owner comes back to their own number after 180 days without the app.** DM1 offers "I'm new to Attune" first; a person who taps it parks their own account. Expect "Used Attune before on this number? Contact support" to be right there (support-only recovery for 180 days), nothing about the old account on screen, and the old account never parked if it was used again since the challenge (another device refreshed). Tests: Task 2 contract section D (`an account in use again was parked`, mutant `park_while_active`), Task 5 `sign_up_dormancy_flow_test.dart` (DM1's support link; `nothing about any account is shown`).
2. **The hook misbehaves in production: a malformed event, a missing Vault secret, a slow database.** Expect the token to be issued (fail open, never a lock-out), one `auth_hook_failures` row and one `auth_hook_fail_open` count per failure, and a webhook post within 15 minutes, marked only after the webhook accepted it. Tests: Task 1 section C (four malformed events, the missing key, mutants `fail_closed`, `no_failure_row`), Task 3 `identity-health-alert/handler.test.ts` (a failed post or no webhook marks nothing).
3. **The hook's 403 at the code step reads as a wrong code.** Auth answers a refused sign-in with 403, which A4 used to read as "That code didn't work". Expect DM1 for `account_dormant:*`, H1 for `account_review*`, and a real wrong code still a wrong code. Tests: Task 5 coordinator test (`a wrong code is still a wrong code`), flow test (`a safety hold at sign-in: H1 … ` asserts no wrong-code text), Task 4 `passwordless_hook_refusal_test.dart`.
4. **A shared phone: Ama is under an age hold, Kofi under a safety hold, a stranger has a recycled number.** Expect the 30-day device block only after Ama's H1, never after Kofi's; the dormancy nonce never on disk; the draft gone at H1. Tests: Task 4 gate tests (`a safety_review hold: H1, and this phone is not blocked`, `a hold of a kind this build does not know blocks`), Task 5 (`an age hold at sign-in … blocked`, `a safety hold at sign-in … not blocked`, `the challenge never reaches the phone's storage`).
5. **The platform's age answer at A2 is slow, declined or contradicts the typed date.** Expect a minor or supervised answer to stop at X1 (before any date, or after an adult date: the stricter wins), a declined or unavailable answer to leave it to the date, an adult answer never to let an under-18 date through, and the read made once per sign-up. Tests: Task 7 `sign_up_age_signal_test.dart` (every case), `age_signals_test.dart` (failure and rubbish are unavailable or unknown).

## Decisions found while verifying the code (read before Task 1)

Each is a verified fact about today's code (HEAD b020e7d1), the database or Supabase Auth, with the smallest change that keeps the spec's intent. *Changes spec intent* marks the ones the owner should see.

- **P-1 (migration 11 as three files).** §4.7 #11 is one migration. It is built as `holds_enforced` (11a), `access_token_hook` (11b) and `fresh_start` (11c), each with its twin, so the server half of W6a reviews in two tasks. Same objects, same order of dependence (11c's `consume_fresh_start` uses 11b's sealing helpers).
- **P-2 (the dormancy challenge is sealed, not stored).** §4.4 says the hook "inserts a challenge and returns account_dormant:<nonce>". Supabase Auth runs a Postgres hook inside the token's own transaction (`internal/hooks/hookspgfunc`: "If the caller passes a transaction, the hook joins it"; `internal/tokens/service.go` calls `GenerateAccessToken(r, tx, …)` inside `db.Transaction` and returns the hook's error from it), so a refusal rolls back every row the hook wrote: a stored challenge would never exist. The nonce is therefore **sealed**: AES-256-CBC of (user id, issue time) under a key derived from the new Vault secret `fresh_start_key`, with a 16-byte HMAC-SHA-256 tag, 128 hex characters that reveal nothing about the old account. `consume_fresh_start` opens it, checks 15 minutes, and records the take in `fresh_start_challenges` (`used_at`), which is what makes it single-use. For the same reason the hook can't count `dormancy_challenge`; identity health counts taken challenges (`fresh_starts_7d`, the `dormancy_fresh_start` metric, the existing `dormancy_` prefix) instead. *Changes spec intent* (mechanism and one health count; owner question 1).
- **P-3 (the hold kind).** `get_my_home_state` gains `hold_kind` (`age_review` first, then `safety_review`, then `recovery_hold`; NULL when none) beside the unchanged `hold` boolean, so older builds keep working (W3 batch 5 carry, before W6a places `recovery_hold` and Spec 2 places `safety_review`).
- **P-4 (users DELETE).** `authenticated` still holds table DELETE on `public.users` (no policy lets it delete a row today). W6a revokes it in 11a and its twin (W1 deferred M-6); contract A pins it (mutant `users_delete`). `local_pg_table_privileges.py` replays the table-level REVOKE itself.
- **P-5 (`account_review:age`).** §4.4's hook message is `account_review` for every hold. At the sign-in verify there is no session, so the phone can't ask which hold it is, yet the W3 carry C-2 needs the age hold to block the device. The hook answers `account_review:age` for an age hold and `account_review` otherwise; H1 shows the same words for both. The person who typed an under-18 date learns nothing new; a safety hold stays unexplained. *Changes spec intent* (owner question 2). **Final review I-1 (ruled):** a code sign-in to an age-held account unused for 180 days answers plain `account_review` (H1, no device block), so the new owner of a recycled number is never device-blocked for a stranger's hold; a refresh, or an age hold in use, keeps `account_review:age`; the hold stays. Cost if wrong: a minor returning after 6+ months isn't device-blocked at sign-in (still on hold).
- **P-6 (fail open, including a timeout).** Auth runs the hook with a 2-second `statement_timeout`. A plain `WHEN OTHERS` doesn't catch `query_canceled`, which would fail closed (a slow database locking everyone out). The handler catches `query_canceled OR OTHERS`, records the SQLSTATE and returns the event unchanged. Deliberate refusals are returned values, never exceptions, so they can't be swallowed.
- **P-7 (the `fresh_start_key` secret).** Vault (or `app.settings.fresh_start_key` locally), at least 32 characters, set by the owner. Unset or short: the dormant sign-in fails open, counted and alerted (contract C), so a missing secret never locks anyone out and is never silent; identity health shows `fresh_start_key_present`.
- **P-8 (who the hook looks at).** An Auth user with no `public.users` row is a sign-up in progress: the hook returns the event and records nothing (an `account_activity` row would break its foreign key and fail open). Activity is written at most every 12 hours.
- **P-9 (seeding `account_activity`).** On the day the hook is switched on no account has an activity row, and `coalesce(activity, users.created_at)` would challenge every account older than 180 days at its next code sign-in. 11b seeds the table from the latest of `users.created_at`, `auth.users.last_sign_in_at`, `auth.users.updated_at` and the account's sessions' last refresh, never moving a value back.
- **P-10 (`consume_fresh_start`).** Signature `consume_fresh_start(p_nonce text, p_ip_hash text DEFAULT NULL) → uuid` (the spec's plus the IP hash for the per-IP limit of §4.5, kept in a new `private.fresh_start_attempts`, 1 hour). An unusable nonce returns NULL rather than raising, so the IP's try is kept (a raise would roll it back; mutant `invalid_raises`); the edge function answers it with one plain `fresh_start:invalid`. It refuses an account used again since the challenge, already parked, or under any hold. The same nonce returns the same id again only while the park isn't finished (`phone_released_at` unset), so a failed park can be retried; after `mark_fresh_start_parked` it takes nothing (mutant `reuse_after_park`). `mark_fresh_start_parked(p_user)` is new: it sets `phone_released_at` only once Auth's number is `+999` and 12 digits and a recovery hold is open.
- **P-11 (`get_identity_health`).** `identity_foundation_contracts.sql` I4 scans the live `get_identity_health` for W6 table names, so adding W6's counts inside it would turn that contract red. W6a puts them in `private.identity_health_w6a()` and re-creates `get_identity_health` as its W1 body `|| private.identity_health_w6a()`; the identity contract stays byte-identical and green (contract F pins the split, mutant `health_inline`).
- **P-12 (parking the number).** `start-fresh-account` derives `+999` and 12 digits from SHA-256(`<user id>:<try>`) (deterministic, so a retry tries the same numbers), up to 5 tries on `phone_exists`, with `phone_confirm: true` so W1a's `auth_users_phone_sync` mirrors it to `public.users`. A retry that finds the account already on `+999` only marks it.
- **P-13 (the purge).** `purge-recovery-holds` follows `purge-unfinished-signups`: up to 200 a run, `is_recovery_hold_due` re-checked just before each delete (support may have resolved the hold), then `prepare_account_deletion` and `auth.admin.deleteUser` (delete-account's order); a failed prepare never deletes; a 60-second budget.
- **P-14 (the alert).** `identity-health-alert` posts one message per kind (`{"text": …}`, a count and a time span, nothing else) to `SAFETY_ALERT_WEBHOOK`, and marks the rows sent only after a 2xx. No webhook configured: nothing is marked, so the rows stay due (and visible in identity health).
- **P-15 (`config.toml`).** `[functions.start-fresh-account] verify_jwt = false`. The Custom Access Token Hook stays switched on per project in the dashboard (the W2c precedent for the Send SMS Hook): a commented `[auth.hook.custom_access_token]` section shows how to try it locally.
- **P-16 (`parked_phone_hmac` and the users SELECT grant).** §4.2 says the column is "not granted to clients". W1 kept table-level SELECT on `users` (own row, RLS), so a client technically may read its own row's value; it is only ever set on a parked account, and the hook refuses every token for a parked account, so no client can read a value. Narrowing `users` to column grants would touch every W1-W5 reader; not done.
- **P-17 (the gate's device block).** `holdBlocksDevice(kind)`: an age hold blocks, and so does a hold whose kind isn't known (a server or cache from before W6a, the RI-3 refusal whose read failed, a kind a later spec adds), exactly as every hold did before; a safety or recovery hold shows H1 alone. The test helper `homeJson(hold: true)` reads as `age_review` unless a test names another kind, so every existing W3/W4 hold test keeps its meaning unedited.
- **P-18 (the sign-in flow's DM1, DM2 and H1).** `/signup` gains three steps: DM1 (`dormant_step.dart`), DM2 (`fresh_start_step.dart`) and H1, which reuses W3's `AccountHoldStep` (`catch_up_steps.dart`, documented "W6a reuses it"; §5.1's `account_hold_screen.dart` is not created). No session exists after a refused verify, so H1's "Sign out" goes to Welcome. After "I'm new to Attune" the way in becomes a new account (A1/A2 then A5, never N1); a spent challenge goes back to A3 with the number open and "That took too long. Enter your number again."; offline or too many tries stay on DM1 and retry the same challenge. None of the three is animated.
- **P-19 (`passwordless_auth_service.dart`).** One parser, `HookRefusal.of(error)` (`AccountDormant(nonce)`, `AccountUnderReview(age:)`), used by the sign-up coordinator and by `userSafeMessage`, which the legacy `LoginScreen` (flag off) shows: H1's sentence for `account_review*`, and for a dormant number "Welcome to Attune. Let's get you set up: update Attune to the latest version to continue." (that screen can't run the fresh start). Every other message is unchanged.
- **P-20 (age signals).** iOS: `AgeSignalReader` lives in `AppDelegate.swift` (a new Swift file would need hand-made `project.pbxproj` entries), asks Declared Age Range only on iOS 26+ and only when `Storefront.current?.countryCode` is in `age_signal_storefronts`, and is weak-linked through the Runner target's `OTHER_LDFLAGS` (`-weak_framework DeclaredAgeRange`): verified while writing this plan that without the flag the linker emits a strong `LC_LOAD_DYLIB`, which would crash below iOS 26. Android: Play Age Signals **0.0.4** (the current release; its result is `ageLower`, `ageUpper`, `ageRangeSource`, read with `javap`; 0.0.3's `userStatus` is gone): 18 and over is adult, a band ending under 18 is supervised when a parent set it (`TIER_B`) and minor otherwise, not shared is unknown, any failure unavailable. A2 asks once per sign-up, as soon as it shows, and Continue waits at most 30 seconds for an answer still out. "Under-18 block hardened" (§8 W6b) is this: a signal stop writes the same 30-day block as an under-18 date (the block itself already meets §3.6). Only a status crosses the channel; nothing is stored or sent.
- **P-21 (the correction rule).** `private.birth_date_correction_open(p_user)`: a declared DOB, `self_corrections = 0`, `declared_at` under 7 days ago (§4.3's anchor; §3.15 says "of sign-up", the same moment for a W2 account), and no `dating_enrollments` row in `state = 'active'`. It names Spec 2's table directly; Spec 2 re-creates it from its live body if Dating's state moves.
- **P-22 (`get_my_birth_date_status`).** A new authenticated RPC `{age, can_correct, correct_until}` for Settings (never the date). `correct_my_birth_date` with the same date again changes nothing and spends nothing; under 18 places the `age_review` hold and returns `{status:'not_eligible'}` without raising, so the hold commits (`set_my_birth_date`'s pattern; mutant `minor_raises`).
- **P-23 (Settings -> Date of birth).** A row under flow_signin_v2 (every v2 account has a DOB), subtitle "Only your age is shown, never the date"; the screen says "Your age: {age}" and either "If it's wrong, you can fix it once, until {date}." with "Fix a mistake" (A2 again, titled "Fix your date of birth"), or "To change this, contact support." with "Contact support". Under 18: block the phone, sign out, X1 (`/age-stop?from=account`), the gate's order.
- **P-24 (child-safety alerts).** §4.2's table plus one column, `alerted_at`, and two service functions, `child_safety_alerts_due` / `mark_child_safety_alerts_sent`; `identity-health-alert` posts "N new report(s) of a concern about a child, the first at …" (no words, no ids) so the named contact hears within 15 minutes rather than polling a table. *Changes spec intent* (owner question 3).
- **P-25 (no retention job for reports).** §4.8 keeps a report "2 years after closing, unless counsel places a legal hold"; the table has no closing time and counsel owes the rule (§10). W6c adds no purge; the owner checklist carries it.
- **P-26 (the emergency line).** §3.15's body ends "call {emergency number}". Until W7a's checked resource list exists, it reads "call your local emergency number" (no unchecked number is shown). *Changes spec intent* (owner question 4).
- **P-27 (the "Dating" chip).** Shown only when the live home state's path is Dating or the person has a Dating enrollment (D20: nothing about Dating on the Relationship path or on disk).
- **P-28 (where the report lives).** Settings -> Safety (after the Safety PIN) and the top of Settings -> Support, both signed in (the RPC needs an account); route `/settings/report-child-concern`. Batch 8 fix round 1 (review M-4): also a row on Support resources (`SafetyResourcesScreen`, the screen §5.4 names), signed in only, so §3.15's "also linked from the Support screen" holds as written.
- **P-29 (copy the spec doesn't give).** DM1: "We couldn't reach Attune. Check your connection and try again.", "Too many tries. Try again in an hour.", "That took too long. Enter your number again."; Settings: "Only your age is shown, never the date", "We don't have your date of birth yet.", "If it's wrong, you can fix it once, until {date}.", "Fix your date of birth", "Saved.", "We couldn't load this. Check your connection and try again."; the report: "A person on our team reads every report" (row subtitle), "What did you see?", "{count} of {max}", "You've sent 5 reports today. To tell us more, email {email}.", "We couldn't send this. Check your connection and try again.", "Done". DV specialist (DM1, DM2, H1) and the owner (the rest) read them at the gates.
- **P-30 (addresses).** `AppConstants.supportEmail` is already `support@barsopus.com` (W3 replaced the placeholder, D6). W6c adds `AppConstants.childSafetyEmail = 'childsafety@barsopus.com'` (§5.2's proposal); the owner confirms both before the W6c gate.
- **P-31 (the CSAE page).** §8.1's content is drafted in the owner checklist for barsopus.com; nothing in the repo publishes it.
- **P-32 (`supabase_auth_admin` on the scratch database).** `attune_spec3a`'s cluster has no `supabase_auth_admin` role, so 11b's twin can't grant to it. Task 1 Step 1 creates the stub once, exactly as the edited bootstrap does (`NOLOGIN NOINHERIT`, guarded), and runs nothing else from the bootstrap.
- **P-33 (fixtures).** `dormancy_contracts.sql` uses ids `5f6a0000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000NN` and phones `15558400NN`; `child_safety_contracts.sql` ids `5f6c…NN` and phones `15558401NN` (§7.1's 84xx range), on `auth.users` and `public.users`.

## Owner questions (open; the plan builds the recommendation)

1. **The sealed challenge (P-2), and identity health counting fresh starts instead of challenges.** *Recommendation:* accept; a stored challenge can't survive Auth's rollback, and the sealed one is single-use, 15-minute, opaque and keyed by a Vault secret.
2. **`account_review:age` (P-5).** *Recommendation:* accept; it is the only way the sign-in H1 can block the phone for an age hold (W3 carry C-2), it tells the person nothing they didn't type, and H1's words don't change.
3. **The child-safety webhook post (P-24).** *Recommendation:* accept; "a person on our team reads every report" needs that person to hear about one, and the post carries a count and a time only.
4. **"Call your local emergency number" until W7a (P-26).** *Recommendation:* accept; an unchecked number in a child-safety screen is worse than a generic line, and W7a's verified list can fill it in later.
5. **The child-safety address `childsafety@barsopus.com` and the named contact (P-30, W6c gate).** *Recommendation:* confirm the address before the W6c build reaches testers; it is in the limit message and on the CSAE page.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing contract**

Create `supabase/tests/dormancy_contracts.sql` with exactly:

```sql
-- supabase/tests/dormancy_contracts.sql
-- Spec 1 W6a and W6b (§3.5, §3.15, §4.3 W6a/W6b, §4.4, §4.5, §4.6, §7.1):
-- holds enforced by the Custom Access Token Hook (sections A-C), the hold
-- kind in the home state, the recycled-number challenge and the fresh
-- start, the purge list, the fail-open alerts and identity health (A2,
-- D-G); W6b adds the date-of-birth correction rules (section H).
-- One BEGIN ... ROLLBACK.
-- Run only on attune_spec3a:
--   psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f supabase/tests/dormancy_contracts.sql
-- Fixture ids 5f6a0000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000NN; phones +15558400NN
-- (§7.1's 84xx range), set on auth.users and public.users. Every refusal
-- is checked by its message.
BEGIN;

DO $$
BEGIN
  PERFORM set_config('app.settings.fresh_start_key', 'w6a-contract-key-0123456789abcdefghijkl', true);
  PERFORM set_config('app.settings.dating_exclusion_key', 'w6a-contract-dating-key', true);
END $$;

CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.id(p_n text) RETURNS uuid LANGUAGE sql AS $$
  SELECT ('5f6a0000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000' || p_n)::uuid;
$$;

CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.error_of(p_sql text) RETURNS text LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
  EXECUTE p_sql;
  RETURN NULL;
EXCEPTION WHEN raise_exception OR insufficient_privilege THEN
  RETURN SQLERRM;
END $$;

-- An account [p_n] last active [p_idle] ago (NULL: no activity row).
CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.person(p_n text, p_idle interval) RETURNS uuid LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO auth.users (id, phone, phone_confirmed_at, created_at)
  VALUES (pg_temp.id(p_n), '15558400' || p_n, now() - interval '400 days', now() - interval '400 days');
  INSERT INTO public.users (id, phone, display_name, path, created_at)
  VALUES (pg_temp.id(p_n), '15558400' || p_n, 'Person ' || p_n, 'just_me', now() - interval '400 days');
  IF p_idle IS NOT NULL THEN
    INSERT INTO public.account_activity (user_id, last_active_at)
    VALUES (pg_temp.id(p_n), now() - p_idle)
    ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE SET last_active_at = EXCLUDED.last_active_at;
  ELSE
    DELETE FROM public.account_activity WHERE user_id = pg_temp.id(p_n);
  END IF;
  RETURN pg_temp.id(p_n);
END $$;

CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.event(p_user uuid, p_method text) RETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE sql AS $$
  SELECT jsonb_build_object('user_id', p_user, 'authentication_method', p_method,
                            'claims', jsonb_build_object('sub', p_user, 'role', 'authenticated', 'aal', 'aal1'));
$$;

-- The hook as Supabase Auth calls it (supabase_auth_admin).
CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.hook(p_event jsonb) RETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE
  v jsonb;
BEGIN
  EXECUTE 'SET LOCAL ROLE supabase_auth_admin';
  v := public.attune_access_token_hook(p_event);
  RESET ROLE;
  RETURN v;
END $$;

-- The hook's answer, or what it raised (a fail-closed hook is a finding,
-- never an aborted contract).
CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.hook_safe(p_event jsonb) RETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
  RETURN pg_temp.hook(p_event);
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
  RESET ROLE;
  RETURN jsonb_build_object('raised', SQLERRM);
END $$;

CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.failures() RETURNS bigint LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
  RETURN (SELECT count(*) FROM private.auth_hook_failures);
END $$;

CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.flag(p_key text, p_on boolean) RETURNS void LANGUAGE sql AS $$
  UPDATE public.feature_flags SET enabled = p_on WHERE key = p_key;
$$;

CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.as_service(p_sql text) RETURNS text LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE
  v text;
BEGIN
  EXECUTE 'SET LOCAL ROLE service_role';
  BEGIN
    EXECUTE p_sql INTO v;
  EXCEPTION WHEN raise_exception OR insufficient_privilege THEN
    RESET ROLE;
    RETURN 'ERROR: ' || SQLERRM;
  END;
  RESET ROLE;
  RETURN v;
END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN PERFORM pg_temp.flag('dormancy_rule', true); END $$;


-- =====================================================================
-- A. Objects and grants (spec §4.2, §4.3 W6a; W1 M-6).
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  v_bad text;
BEGIN
  IF to_regclass('public.account_activity') IS NULL OR to_regclass('public.fresh_start_challenges') IS NULL
     OR to_regclass('private.auth_hook_failures') IS NULL
     OR to_regprocedure('public.attune_access_token_hook(jsonb)') IS NULL
     OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns
                     WHERE table_schema = 'public' AND table_name = 'users' AND column_name = 'parked_phone_hmac') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: W6a objects are missing';
  END IF;

  -- The hook: Supabase Auth only (spec §4.3 W6a).
  IF NOT has_function_privilege('supabase_auth_admin', 'public.attune_access_token_hook(jsonb)', 'EXECUTE')
     OR has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'public.attune_access_token_hook(jsonb)', 'EXECUTE')
     OR has_function_privilege('anon', 'public.attune_access_token_hook(jsonb)', 'EXECUTE')
     OR has_function_privilege('service_role', 'public.attune_access_token_hook(jsonb)', 'EXECUTE') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the access-token hook is executable by someone other than supabase_auth_admin';
  END IF;

  SELECT string_agg(f, ', ') INTO v_bad
    FROM unnest(ARRAY['private.fresh_start_key(text)', 'private.seal_fresh_start(uuid,timestamp with time zone)',
                      'private.open_fresh_start(text)', 'private.access_token_refusal(jsonb)']) AS f
   WHERE has_function_privilege('authenticated', f, 'EXECUTE') OR has_function_privilege('anon', f, 'EXECUTE')
      OR has_function_privilege('service_role', f, 'EXECUTE') OR has_function_privilege('supabase_auth_admin', f, 'EXECUTE');
  IF v_bad IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a server-only W6a helper is executable by a role: %', v_bad;
  END IF;

  IF has_table_privilege('authenticated', 'public.account_activity', 'SELECT')
     OR has_table_privilege('anon', 'public.account_activity', 'SELECT')
     OR has_table_privilege('authenticated', 'public.fresh_start_challenges', 'SELECT')
     OR has_table_privilege('anon', 'public.fresh_start_challenges', 'SELECT')
     OR has_table_privilege('authenticated', 'private.auth_hook_failures', 'SELECT')
     OR has_table_privilege('service_role', 'private.auth_hook_failures', 'SELECT')
     OR NOT has_table_privilege('service_role', 'public.account_activity', 'DELETE')
     OR NOT has_table_privilege('service_role', 'public.fresh_start_challenges', 'INSERT') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: W6a table grants are wrong';
  END IF;
  IF NOT (SELECT relrowsecurity FROM pg_class WHERE oid = 'public.account_activity'::regclass)
     OR NOT (SELECT relrowsecurity FROM pg_class WHERE oid = 'public.fresh_start_challenges'::regclass) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: RLS is off on a W6a table';
  END IF;

  -- W1 deferred M-6: no client deletes a users row.
  IF has_table_privilege('authenticated', 'public.users', 'DELETE')
     OR has_table_privilege('anon', 'public.users', 'DELETE') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a client may DELETE users (M-6)';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- B. The hold kind in the home state (W3 batch 5 carry): age_review first,
--    then safety_review, then recovery_hold; 'hold' unchanged.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  v_uid constant uuid := pg_temp.person('01', interval '1 day');
  v jsonb;
BEGIN
  PERFORM set_config('request.jwt.claims', json_build_object('sub', v_uid, 'role', 'authenticated')::text, true);
  PERFORM set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub', v_uid::text, true);
  EXECUTE 'SET LOCAL ROLE authenticated';
  v := public.get_my_home_state();
  RESET ROLE;
  IF (v -> 'hold') IS DISTINCT FROM 'false'::jsonb OR (v -> 'hold_kind') IS DISTINCT FROM 'null'::jsonb THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an account with no hold reads %', v;
  END IF;

  PERFORM public.place_account_hold(v_uid, 'recovery_hold');
  PERFORM public.place_account_hold(v_uid, 'safety_review');
  EXECUTE 'SET LOCAL ROLE authenticated';
  v := public.get_my_home_state();
  RESET ROLE;
  IF (v -> 'hold') IS DISTINCT FROM 'true'::jsonb OR v ->> 'hold_kind' IS DISTINCT FROM 'safety_review' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: safety_review must outrank recovery_hold, got %', v -> 'hold_kind';
  END IF;

  PERFORM public.place_account_hold(v_uid, 'age_review');
  EXECUTE 'SET LOCAL ROLE authenticated';
  v := public.get_my_home_state();
  RESET ROLE;
  IF v ->> 'hold_kind' IS DISTINCT FROM 'age_review' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an age hold must read age_review first, got %', v -> 'hold_kind';
  END IF;

  UPDATE public.account_holds SET resolved_at = now(), resolved_by = 'contract' WHERE user_id = v_uid;
  EXECUTE 'SET LOCAL ROLE authenticated';
  v := public.get_my_home_state();
  RESET ROLE;
  IF (v -> 'hold') IS DISTINCT FROM 'false'::jsonb OR (v -> 'hold_kind') IS DISTINCT FROM 'null'::jsonb THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: resolved holds still read as %', v;
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- C. The hook (spec §4.4, R12, S-8).
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  v_recent constant uuid := pg_temp.person('10', interval '1 hour');
  v_stale constant uuid := pg_temp.person('11', interval '13 hours');
  v_dormant constant uuid := pg_temp.person('12', interval '200 days');
  v_never constant uuid := pg_temp.person('13', NULL);
  v_age constant uuid := pg_temp.person('14', interval '1 hour');
  v_safety constant uuid := pg_temp.person('15', interval '1 hour');
  v_parked constant uuid := pg_temp.person('16', interval '1 hour');
  v_new constant uuid := pg_temp.id('17');
  v_before timestamptz;
  v_failures bigint;
  v_metric bigint;
  v jsonb;
  v_nonce text;
  v_open record;
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO auth.users (id, phone, phone_confirmed_at) VALUES (v_new, '1555840017', now());
  PERFORM public.place_account_hold(v_age, 'age_review');
  PERFORM public.place_account_hold(v_safety, 'safety_review');
  PERFORM public.place_account_hold(v_parked, 'recovery_hold');
  v_failures := pg_temp.failures();

  -- In use: the event comes back unchanged; activity under 12 h is left.
  SELECT last_active_at INTO v_before FROM public.account_activity WHERE user_id = v_recent;
  v := pg_temp.hook(pg_temp.event(v_recent, 'otp'));
  IF v IS DISTINCT FROM pg_temp.event(v_recent, 'otp') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a recent account''s sign-in was changed: %', v;
  END IF;
  IF (SELECT last_active_at FROM public.account_activity WHERE user_id = v_recent) IS DISTINCT FROM v_before THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: activity under 12 hours old was rewritten';
  END IF;

  -- A refresh after 13 hours moves the activity to now.
  v := pg_temp.hook(pg_temp.event(v_stale, 'token_refresh'));
  IF v IS DISTINCT FROM pg_temp.event(v_stale, 'token_refresh')
     OR (SELECT last_active_at FROM public.account_activity WHERE user_id = v_stale) IS DISTINCT FROM now() THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a refresh after 13 hours did not record activity';
  END IF;
  -- No activity row at all (before the seed reached it): written.
  v := pg_temp.hook(pg_temp.event(v_never, 'token_refresh'));
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.account_activity WHERE user_id = v_never AND last_active_at = now()) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a first token wrote no activity';
  END IF;

  -- A recycled number: a code sign-in to an account unused for 180 days.
  v := pg_temp.hook(pg_temp.event(v_dormant, 'otp'));
  IF v #>> '{error,http_code}' IS DISTINCT FROM '403'
     OR v #>> '{error,message}' !~ '^account_dormant:[0-9a-f]{128}$' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a dormant OTP sign-in was not challenged: %', v;
  END IF;
  v_nonce := substring(v #>> '{error,message}' FROM 17);
  SELECT * INTO v_open FROM private.open_fresh_start(v_nonce);
  IF v_open.user_id IS DISTINCT FROM v_dormant OR v_open.issued_at > now() OR v_open.issued_at < now() - interval '1 second' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the nonce does not open to the dormant account now (%, %)', v_open.user_id, v_open.issued_at;
  END IF;
  IF position(replace(v_dormant::text, '-', '') IN v_nonce) > 0 OR position(v_dormant::text IN v_nonce) > 0 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the nonce shows the old account''s id';
  END IF;
  IF (SELECT last_active_at FROM public.account_activity WHERE user_id = v_dormant) > now() - interval '199 days' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a refused sign-in recorded activity';
  END IF;
  -- The same device's refresh passes (spec §7.1) and is activity.
  v := pg_temp.hook(pg_temp.event(v_dormant, 'token_refresh'));
  IF v IS DISTINCT FROM pg_temp.event(v_dormant, 'token_refresh') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a dormant account''s refresh was refused: %', v;
  END IF;
  UPDATE public.account_activity SET last_active_at = now() - interval '200 days' WHERE user_id = v_dormant;
  -- dormancy_rule off: no challenge (holds still apply).
  PERFORM pg_temp.flag('dormancy_rule', false);
  v := pg_temp.hook(pg_temp.event(v_dormant, 'otp'));
  PERFORM pg_temp.flag('dormancy_rule', true);
  IF v ? 'error' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: dormancy_rule off still challenged: %', v;
  END IF;
  UPDATE public.account_activity SET last_active_at = now() - interval '200 days' WHERE user_id = v_dormant;

  -- Every hold kind, every way in.
  IF pg_temp.hook(pg_temp.event(v_age, 'otp')) IS DISTINCT FROM
       '{"error": {"http_code": 403, "message": "account_review:age"}}'::jsonb
     OR pg_temp.hook(pg_temp.event(v_age, 'token_refresh')) IS DISTINCT FROM
       '{"error": {"http_code": 403, "message": "account_review:age"}}'::jsonb THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an age hold was not refused with account_review:age';
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.hook(pg_temp.event(v_safety, 'otp')) IS DISTINCT FROM
       '{"error": {"http_code": 403, "message": "account_review"}}'::jsonb THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a safety hold was not refused with account_review';
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.hook(pg_temp.event(v_parked, 'token_refresh')) IS DISTINCT FROM
       '{"error": {"http_code": 403, "message": "account_review"}}'::jsonb THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a recovery hold''s refresh was not refused';
  END IF;
  -- A resolved hold no longer refuses.
  UPDATE public.account_holds SET resolved_at = now() WHERE user_id = v_safety;
  IF pg_temp.hook(pg_temp.event(v_safety, 'otp')) ? 'error' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a resolved hold still refused';
  END IF;

  -- A brand-new sign-up (no users row yet): passes, records nothing.
  v := pg_temp.hook(pg_temp.event(v_new, 'otp'));
  IF v IS DISTINCT FROM pg_temp.event(v_new, 'otp')
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.account_activity WHERE user_id = v_new) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a new sign-up was changed or recorded';
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.failures() <> v_failures THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a deliberate decision was counted as a fail-open';
  END IF;

  -- Malformed events: unchanged, one failure row each (spec §7.1, S-8).
  v_metric := coalesce((SELECT count FROM public.metric_counters
                         WHERE key = 'auth_hook_fail_open' AND day = (now() AT TIME ZONE 'utc')::date), 0);
  IF pg_temp.hook_safe('{}'::jsonb) IS DISTINCT FROM '{}'::jsonb
     OR pg_temp.hook_safe('{"user_id": "not-a-uuid", "claims": {}}'::jsonb) IS DISTINCT FROM '{"user_id": "not-a-uuid", "claims": {}}'::jsonb
     OR pg_temp.hook_safe('[1, 2]'::jsonb) IS DISTINCT FROM '[1, 2]'::jsonb
     OR pg_temp.hook_safe(jsonb_build_object('user_id', v_recent)) IS DISTINCT FROM jsonb_build_object('user_id', v_recent) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a malformed event did not come back unchanged';
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.failures() <> v_failures + 4 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: malformed events wrote % failure rows, not 4', pg_temp.failures() - v_failures;
  END IF;
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM private.auth_hook_failures WHERE id > 0 AND sqlstate IS NULL AND at = clock_timestamp()) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a failure row has no sqlstate';
  END IF;
  IF coalesce((SELECT count FROM public.metric_counters
                WHERE key = 'auth_hook_fail_open' AND day = (now() AT TIME ZONE 'utc')::date), 0) <> v_metric + 4 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: auth_hook_fail_open did not count each fail-open';
  END IF;

  -- No key configured: the dormant sign-in fails open, counted (never a lock-out).
  PERFORM set_config('app.settings.fresh_start_key', '', true);
  v := pg_temp.hook_safe(pg_temp.event(v_dormant, 'otp'));
  PERFORM set_config('app.settings.fresh_start_key', 'w6a-contract-key-0123456789abcdefghijkl', true);
  IF v IS DISTINCT FROM pg_temp.event(v_dormant, 'otp') OR pg_temp.failures() <> v_failures + 5 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a missing fresh_start_key must fail open and be counted, got %', v;
  END IF;
  IF (SELECT sqlstate FROM private.auth_hook_failures ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1) IS DISTINCT FROM 'P0001' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the fail-open row does not carry the error''s sqlstate';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- (W6a Task 2 adds sections A2 and D-G above this line.)
-- (W6b adds section H, the date-of-birth correction, above this line.)

DO $$ BEGIN RAISE NOTICE 'dormancy_contracts: all held'; END $$;

ROLLBACK;
```

Create `supabase/migrations/20261103100000_holds_enforced.sql` with exactly:

```sql
-- Spec 1 W6a, migration 11 part 1 of 3 (spec §4.7 #11; plan P-1):
-- holds_enforced.
--   * get_my_home_state() re-created from its live body (20261025100300,
--     spec §4.3 rule, I-1) with one key added: hold_kind, the unresolved
--     hold's kind (age_review first, then safety_review, then
--     recovery_hold), so the phone blocks itself only for an age hold
--     (W3 batch 5 carry, plan P-3). 'hold' stays as it was, for older
--     builds.
--   * authenticated loses DELETE on public.users (W1 deferred M-6, plan
--     P-4): no policy ever let a client delete its row; now no grant does
--     either. Account deletion is the delete-account edge function's
--     (service role).
-- Grants in 20261103100010_holds_enforced_grants.sql.
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_my_home_state()
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_uid constant uuid := auth.uid();
  v_user public.users%ROWTYPE;
  v_has_account boolean;
  v_needs text[] := ARRAY[]::text[];
  v_rel public.relationships%ROWTYPE;
  v_state text := 'none';
  v_partner_name text;
  v_tab text;
BEGIN
  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'home:not_signed_in';
  END IF;
  SELECT * INTO v_user FROM public.users AS u WHERE u.id = v_uid;
  v_has_account := FOUND;

  -- The version is a number of at most three digits, read the way
  -- complete_sign_up reads it: anything else counts as not accepted and
  -- never throws (batch 4 fix round 0).
  IF NOT v_has_account
     OR coalesce(substring(v_user.eula_version FROM '^[0-9]{1,3}$')::integer, 0) < 2 THEN
    v_needs := array_append(v_needs, 'consent');
  END IF;
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.account_birthdates AS b WHERE b.user_id = v_uid) THEN
    v_needs := array_append(v_needs, 'birth_date');
  END IF;
  -- Read the way complete_sign_up stores it (final review M-1): a name of
  -- invisible or bidi characters only is no name.
  IF NOT v_has_account OR private.normalize_display_name(v_user.display_name) ~ '^\+?[0-9 ()-]*$' THEN
    v_needs := array_append(v_needs, 'name');
  END IF;
  IF v_user.path IS NULL THEN
    v_needs := array_append(v_needs, 'path');
  END IF;

  -- An open (active or paused) relationship ranks first: legacy
  -- accept-invite keeps the acceptor's own pending invite until W4a, and a
  -- linked member must never read as pending (batch 4 review #1).
  SELECT * INTO v_rel FROM public.relationships AS r
   WHERE v_uid IN (r.user_a, r.user_b)
   ORDER BY (r.status IN ('active', 'paused')) DESC, r.created_at DESC, r.id DESC
   LIMIT 1;
  IF FOUND THEN
    v_state := CASE
      WHEN v_rel.status = 'pending' THEN 'pending'
      WHEN v_rel.status = 'active' THEN 'linked'
      WHEN v_rel.status = 'paused' THEN 'paused'
      -- The ending window is the 30 days after the end (spec §3.8 R3,
      -- §3.13), whether or not the chat is still readable: sealing it or
      -- an archive by a new chapter inside those days is still the window
      -- (batch 4 review #7).
      WHEN v_rel.status IN ('ended', 'archived') AND v_rel.ended_at > now() - interval '30 days' THEN 'ended_window'
      ELSE 'after_ending'
    END;
    -- The partner's name only while the space or its ending window is
    -- there, as the lifecycle card shows it; never after the window
    -- (batch 4 review #6). A name, never a phone number (the lifecycle
    -- RPC's guard).
    IF v_state IN ('linked', 'paused', 'ended_window') THEN
      -- Invisible and bidi characters dropped first (final review M-1), so
      -- an override can't spoof it and an invisible-only name reads NULL.
      SELECT CASE WHEN split_part(private.normalize_display_name(p.display_name), ' ', 1) ~ '^\+?[0-9 ()-]*$' THEN NULL
                  ELSE split_part(private.normalize_display_name(p.display_name), ' ', 1) END
        INTO v_partner_name
        FROM public.users AS p
       WHERE p.id = CASE WHEN v_rel.user_a = v_uid THEN v_rel.user_b ELSE v_rel.user_a END;
    END IF;
  END IF;

  -- Chat on the Relationship path. A legacy account that has not chosen a
  -- path yet keeps Chat while its latest relationship is pending, linked or
  -- paused, so the v2 shell never moves a couple onto Me before A6.
  -- A linked or paused member is on Relationship by spec (§3.11, §3.12)
  -- even before a path writer catches up (legacy accept-invite until W4a),
  -- so the tab follows the link (batch 4 review #3).
  v_tab := CASE
    WHEN v_user.path = 'relationship' THEN 'chat'
    WHEN v_state IN ('linked', 'paused') THEN 'chat'
    WHEN v_user.path IS NULL AND v_state IN ('pending', 'linked', 'paused') THEN 'chat'
    ELSE 'me'
  END;

  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'has_account', v_has_account,
    'path', v_user.path,
    'tab', v_tab,
    'path_previous', v_user.path_previous,
    'needs', to_jsonb(v_needs),
    'hold', EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.account_holds AS h
                     WHERE h.user_id = v_uid AND h.resolved_at IS NULL),
    -- Spec 1 W6a: which hold, so the phone blocks itself only for an age
    -- hold (age_review first, then safety_review, then recovery_hold).
    'hold_kind', (SELECT h.kind FROM public.account_holds AS h
                   WHERE h.user_id = v_uid AND h.resolved_at IS NULL
                   ORDER BY CASE h.kind WHEN 'age_review' THEN 0 WHEN 'safety_review' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END
                   LIMIT 1),
    'eula_version', v_user.eula_version,
    'dating', private.dating_home_summary(v_uid),
    'link', jsonb_build_object(
      'state', v_state,
      'relationship_id', CASE WHEN v_state = 'none' THEN NULL ELSE v_rel.id END,
      'partner_first_name', v_partner_name,
      'invite_expires_at', CASE WHEN v_state = 'pending' THEN v_rel.invite_expires_at END,
      'window_ends_at', CASE WHEN v_state = 'ended_window' THEN v_rel.ended_at + interval '30 days' END));
END;
$$;

REVOKE DELETE ON public.users FROM authenticated;

COMMIT;
```

Create `supabase/migrations/20261103100010_holds_enforced_grants.sql` with exactly:

```sql
-- Spec 1 W6a: grants for 20261103100000_holds_enforced.sql. The home state
-- stays the app's; no client deletes a users row (M-6).
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.get_my_home_state() FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.get_my_home_state() TO authenticated;
REVOKE DELETE ON public.users FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
COMMIT;
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write migration 11b and its twin**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261103100100_access_token_hook.sql` with exactly:

```sql
-- Spec 1 W6a, migration 11 part 2 of 3 (spec §4.7 #11; plan P-1):
-- access_token_hook. Holds start to bite.
--   * users.parked_phone_hmac (review I-11): written by consume_fresh_start
--     (part 3) from the real number before the number is parked. Never
--     granted to clients.
--   * account_activity: the last time the account got a token, written by
--     the hook at most every 12 hours; seeded here from Auth's own sign-in
--     and session times, so no account in use reads as dormant on the
--     day the hook is switched on (plan P-9).
--   * fresh_start_challenges: one row per challenge taken by
--     consume_fresh_start (part 3); kept 1 hour by fresh-start-retention.
--   * private.auth_hook_failures (review S-8): one row per fail-open (no
--     user id, no claims); kept 30 days; alerted by identity-health-alert.
--   * private.seal_fresh_start / private.open_fresh_start: the dormancy
--     challenge is SEALED, not stored (plan P-2). Supabase Auth runs the
--     hook inside the token's own transaction and rolls it back when the
--     hook refuses, so a row the hook inserted on a refusal would never
--     exist. The nonce is AES-256-CBC(user id, issue time) under the Vault
--     secret fresh_start_key, with a 16-byte HMAC-SHA-256 tag: 128 hex
--     characters that say nothing about the old account.
--   * private.access_token_refusal(event): the decision (spec §4.4): any
--     unresolved hold -> account_review (account_review:age for an age
--     hold, plan P-5); dormancy_rule on, an OTP sign-in, a users row, no
--     activity for 180 days -> account_dormant:<nonce>; otherwise the
--     activity is refreshed (at most every 12 hours) and NULL comes back.
--   * public.attune_access_token_hook(event): Supabase's Custom Access
--     Token Hook. Any unexpected error, a statement timeout included,
--     returns the event unchanged (fail open, R12) and records one
--     auth_hook_failures row and the auth_hook_fail_open metric in its own
--     nested block. Executable by supabase_auth_admin only.
-- Grants in 20261103100110_access_token_hook_grants.sql.
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';

ALTER TABLE public.users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS parked_phone_hmac text;
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.users.parked_phone_hmac IS
  'Spec 1 W6a (review I-11): dating_phone_hmac of the real number, written by consume_fresh_start just before the number is parked on +999. Spec 2''s dating_phone_hmac_for_user prefers it for a parked account. Not granted to clients.';

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.account_activity (
  user_id uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  last_active_at timestamptz NOT NULL
);
ALTER TABLE public.account_activity ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.fresh_start_challenges (
  nonce_hash text PRIMARY KEY
    CONSTRAINT fresh_start_challenges_nonce_hash_check CHECK (nonce_hash ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  used_at timestamptz
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS fresh_start_challenges_created_idx ON public.fresh_start_challenges (created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS fresh_start_challenges_user_idx ON public.fresh_start_challenges (user_id);
ALTER TABLE public.fresh_start_challenges ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS private.auth_hook_failures (
  id bigserial PRIMARY KEY,
  at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT clock_timestamp(),
  sqlstate text,
  alerted_at timestamptz
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS auth_hook_failures_unalerted_idx
  ON private.auth_hook_failures (id) WHERE alerted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS auth_hook_failures_at_idx ON private.auth_hook_failures (at);

-- Seed (plan P-9): the latest of the account's creation, Auth's last
-- sign-in and update, and its sessions' last refresh. Never moves a later
-- value back.
INSERT INTO public.account_activity AS a (user_id, last_active_at)
SELECT u.id,
       greatest(u.created_at, au.last_sign_in_at, au.updated_at,
                (SELECT max(greatest(s.updated_at, s.refreshed_at AT TIME ZONE 'utc'))
                   FROM auth.sessions AS s WHERE s.user_id = u.id))
  FROM public.users AS u
  LEFT JOIN auth.users AS au ON au.id = u.id
ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE
  SET last_active_at = greatest(a.last_active_at, EXCLUDED.last_active_at);

-- The two keys, derived from one Vault secret (fresh_start_key, at least
-- 32 characters). Unset: fresh_start:not_configured.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.fresh_start_key(p_purpose text)
RETURNS bytea
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_secret constant text := public.app_setting('fresh_start_key');
BEGIN
  IF v_secret IS NULL OR char_length(v_secret) < 32 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'fresh_start:not_configured';
  END IF;
  RETURN extensions.digest(p_purpose || ':' || v_secret, 'sha256');
END;
$$;

-- iv (16) || AES-256-CBC(user id (16) || issue time, epoch seconds (8))
-- (32) || HMAC-SHA-256 tag, first 16 bytes; as hex.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.seal_fresh_start(p_user uuid, p_at timestamptz)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE plpgsql
VOLATILE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_iv constant bytea := extensions.gen_random_bytes(16);
  v_plain constant bytea := decode(replace(p_user::text, '-', ''), 'hex')
                            || int8send(floor(extract(epoch FROM p_at))::bigint);
  v_box bytea;
BEGIN
  v_box := v_iv || extensions.encrypt_iv(v_plain, private.fresh_start_key('enc'), v_iv, 'aes-cbc/pad:pkcs');
  RETURN encode(v_box || substring(extensions.hmac(v_box, private.fresh_start_key('mac'), 'sha256') FROM 1 FOR 16), 'hex');
END;
$$;

-- The account and issue time a nonce was sealed for, or (NULL, NULL) for
-- anything that isn't a nonce this project sealed.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.open_fresh_start(p_nonce text, OUT user_id uuid, OUT issued_at timestamptz)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_raw bytea;
  v_plain bytea;
BEGIN
  IF p_nonce IS NULL OR p_nonce !~ '^[0-9a-f]{128}$' THEN
    RETURN;
  END IF;
  v_raw := decode(p_nonce, 'hex');
  IF substring(extensions.hmac(substring(v_raw FROM 1 FOR 48), private.fresh_start_key('mac'), 'sha256') FROM 1 FOR 16)
     IS DISTINCT FROM substring(v_raw FROM 49 FOR 16) THEN
    RETURN;
  END IF;
  v_plain := extensions.decrypt_iv(substring(v_raw FROM 17 FOR 32), private.fresh_start_key('enc'),
                                   substring(v_raw FROM 1 FOR 16), 'aes-cbc/pad:pkcs');
  IF length(v_plain) <> 24 THEN
    RETURN;
  END IF;
  user_id := encode(substring(v_plain FROM 1 FOR 16), 'hex')::uuid;
  issued_at := to_timestamp(('x' || encode(substring(v_plain FROM 17 FOR 8), 'hex'))::bit(64)::bigint);
END;
$$;

-- Spec §4.4: NULL lets the token through; otherwise the hook's error.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.access_token_refusal(p_event jsonb)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
VOLATILE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_uid uuid;
  v_kind text;
  v_created timestamptz;
  v_last timestamptz;
BEGIN
  IF p_event IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(p_event) IS DISTINCT FROM 'object'
     OR jsonb_typeof(p_event -> 'claims') IS DISTINCT FROM 'object' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'hook:malformed_event';
  END IF;
  v_uid := (p_event ->> 'user_id')::uuid;
  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'hook:malformed_event';
  END IF;

  -- (1) Any unresolved hold: no token, whatever the way in (a refresh
  -- included, so a parked account's old sessions stop within the hour).
  SELECT h.kind INTO v_kind
    FROM public.account_holds AS h
   WHERE h.user_id = v_uid AND h.resolved_at IS NULL
   ORDER BY CASE h.kind WHEN 'age_review' THEN 0 WHEN 'safety_review' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END
   LIMIT 1;
  IF v_kind IS NOT NULL THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('error', jsonb_build_object(
      'http_code', 403,
      'message', CASE WHEN v_kind = 'age_review' THEN 'account_review:age' ELSE 'account_review' END));
  END IF;

  -- A brand-new sign-up has no users row yet: nothing to check or record.
  SELECT u.created_at INTO v_created FROM public.users AS u WHERE u.id = v_uid;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN NULL;
  END IF;
  SELECT a.last_active_at INTO v_last FROM public.account_activity AS a WHERE a.user_id = v_uid;

  -- (2) A recycled number: a code sign-in to an account unused for 180
  -- days. A refresh (the same device) passes.
  IF p_event ->> 'authentication_method' = 'otp'
     AND private.spec1_flag_on('dormancy_rule')
     AND coalesce(v_last, v_created) < now() - interval '180 days' THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('error', jsonb_build_object(
      'http_code', 403,
      'message', 'account_dormant:' || private.seal_fresh_start(v_uid, now())));
  END IF;

  -- (3) In use: the activity, at most every 12 hours.
  IF v_last IS NULL OR v_last < now() - interval '12 hours' THEN
    INSERT INTO public.account_activity AS a (user_id, last_active_at)
    VALUES (v_uid, now())
    ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE
      SET last_active_at = EXCLUDED.last_active_at
      WHERE a.last_active_at < EXCLUDED.last_active_at - interval '12 hours';
  END IF;
  RETURN NULL;
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.attune_access_token_hook(event jsonb)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
VOLATILE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_refusal jsonb;
  v_state text;
BEGIN
  BEGIN
    v_refusal := private.access_token_refusal(event);
  EXCEPTION WHEN query_canceled OR OTHERS THEN
    -- Fail open (R12): a bug or a slow database never locks everyone out.
    -- Counted and alerted (S-8); recording can never fail the hook.
    GET STACKED DIAGNOSTICS v_state = RETURNED_SQLSTATE;
    BEGIN
      INSERT INTO private.auth_hook_failures (sqlstate) VALUES (v_state);
      PERFORM private.bump_metric_internal('auth_hook_fail_open');
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
      NULL;
    END;
    RETURN event;
  END;
  RETURN coalesce(v_refusal, event);
END;
$$;

-- Revoked here as well as in the twin (the W1 precedent): the hosted
-- default privileges grant a new function to anon, authenticated and
-- service_role, and a late twin must not leave the hook, a nonce sealer or
-- the decision callable by a client.
REVOKE ALL ON public.account_activity, public.fresh_start_challenges FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON private.auth_hook_failures FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON SEQUENCE private.auth_hook_failures_id_seq FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.fresh_start_key(text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.seal_fresh_start(uuid, timestamptz) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.open_fresh_start(text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.access_token_refusal(jsonb) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.attune_access_token_hook(jsonb) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;

COMMIT;
```

Create `supabase/migrations/20261103100110_access_token_hook_grants.sql` with exactly:

```sql
-- Spec 1 W6a: grants for 20261103100100_access_token_hook.sql. The hook is
-- Supabase Auth's only (supabase_auth_admin); the two new public tables
-- are the service role's; everything else is server-only.
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';
REVOKE ALL ON public.account_activity, public.fresh_start_challenges FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.account_activity, public.fresh_start_challenges TO service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON private.auth_hook_failures FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON SEQUENCE private.auth_hook_failures_id_seq FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.fresh_start_key(text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.seal_fresh_start(uuid, timestamptz) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.open_fresh_start(text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.access_token_refusal(jsonb) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.attune_access_token_hook(jsonb) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA public TO supabase_auth_admin;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.attune_access_token_hook(jsonb) TO supabase_auth_admin;
COMMIT;
```

-- =====================================================================
-- A2. The fresh start's objects, grants and crons (spec §4.3 W6a, §4.6).
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  v_bad text;
BEGIN
  IF to_regclass('private.fresh_start_attempts') IS NULL
     OR to_regprocedure('public.consume_fresh_start(text,text)') IS NULL
     OR to_regprocedure('public.mark_fresh_start_parked(uuid)') IS NULL
     OR to_regprocedure('public.recovery_hold_candidates()') IS NULL
     OR to_regprocedure('public.is_recovery_hold_due(uuid)') IS NULL
     OR to_regprocedure('public.identity_alerts_due()') IS NULL
     OR to_regprocedure('public.mark_identity_alerts_sent(bigint[])') IS NULL
     OR to_regprocedure('private.identity_health_w6a()') IS NULL
     OR to_regprocedure('private.fresh_start_retention()') IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: W6a fresh-start objects are missing';
  END IF;

  SELECT string_agg(f, ', ') INTO v_bad
    FROM unnest(ARRAY['private.identity_health_w6a()', 'private.fresh_start_retention()']) AS f
   WHERE has_function_privilege('authenticated', f, 'EXECUTE') OR has_function_privilege('anon', f, 'EXECUTE')
      OR has_function_privilege('service_role', f, 'EXECUTE') OR has_function_privilege('supabase_auth_admin', f, 'EXECUTE');
  IF v_bad IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a server-only W6a helper is executable by a role: %', v_bad;
  END IF;

  SELECT string_agg(f, ', ') INTO v_bad
    FROM unnest(ARRAY['public.consume_fresh_start(text,text)', 'public.mark_fresh_start_parked(uuid)',
                      'public.recovery_hold_candidates()', 'public.is_recovery_hold_due(uuid)',
                      'public.identity_alerts_due()', 'public.mark_identity_alerts_sent(bigint[])',
                      'public.get_identity_health()']) AS f
   WHERE has_function_privilege('authenticated', f, 'EXECUTE') OR has_function_privilege('anon', f, 'EXECUTE')
      OR NOT has_function_privilege('service_role', f, 'EXECUTE');
  IF v_bad IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a W6a service function has the wrong grants: %', v_bad;
  END IF;

  IF has_table_privilege('authenticated', 'private.fresh_start_attempts', 'SELECT')
     OR has_table_privilege('service_role', 'private.fresh_start_attempts', 'SELECT') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: fresh_start_attempts is readable outside the server';
  END IF;

  SELECT string_agg(j, ', ') INTO v_bad
    FROM (VALUES ('fresh-start-retention', '43 * * * *'), ('purge-recovery-holds', '55 3 * * *'),
                 ('identity-health-alert', '*/15 * * * *')) AS w(j, s)
   WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM cron.job AS c WHERE c.jobname = w.j AND c.schedule = w.s);
  IF v_bad IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a W6a cron is missing or mis-scheduled: %', v_bad;
