```sql
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- D. The fresh start (spec §3.5, §4.3 W6a, §4.5; I-11; plan P-10).
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  v_old constant uuid := pg_temp.person('20', interval '200 days');
  v_back constant uuid := pg_temp.person('21', interval '200 days');
  v_held constant uuid := pg_temp.person('22', interval '200 days');
  v_nonce text;
  v_got text;
  v_ip constant text := encode(extensions.digest('ip:203.0.113.9', 'sha256'), 'hex');
  i int;
BEGIN
  v_nonce := substring(pg_temp.hook(pg_temp.event(v_old, 'otp')) #>> '{error,message}' FROM 17);

  -- Not before the number moves: mark refuses.
  v_got := pg_temp.as_service(format('SELECT public.mark_fresh_start_parked(%L)::text', v_old));
  IF v_got IS DISTINCT FROM 'ERROR: fresh_start:not_parked' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: mark_fresh_start_parked before any hold or park: %', v_got;
  END IF;

  v_got := pg_temp.as_service(format('SELECT public.consume_fresh_start(%L)::text', v_nonce));
  IF v_got IS DISTINCT FROM v_old::text THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a fresh nonce was not taken: %', v_got;
  END IF;
  -- I-11: the REAL number's HMAC, in Spec 2's form, before the park.
  IF (SELECT parked_phone_hmac FROM public.users WHERE id = v_old)
       IS DISTINCT FROM public.dating_phone_hmac('+1555840020') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: parked_phone_hmac is not dating_phone_hmac(''+'' || digits) of the real number';
  END IF;
  IF (SELECT phone FROM auth.users WHERE id = v_old) IS DISTINCT FROM '1555840020' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: consume_fresh_start changed the number itself';
  END IF;
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.account_holds WHERE user_id = v_old AND kind = 'recovery_hold'
                   AND resolved_at IS NULL AND reason_ref = 'fresh_start'
                   AND expires_at BETWEEN now() + interval '179 days' AND now() + interval '181 days') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: no 180-day recovery hold after a fresh start';
  END IF;
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.fresh_start_challenges
                  WHERE user_id = v_old AND used_at IS NOT NULL
                    AND nonce_hash = encode(extensions.digest(v_nonce, 'sha256'), 'hex')) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the taken challenge was not recorded as used';
  END IF;
  -- The park can be retried with the same nonce while it isn't finished.
  IF pg_temp.as_service(format('SELECT public.consume_fresh_start(%L)::text', v_nonce)) IS DISTINCT FROM v_old::text THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an unfinished park could not be retried';
  END IF;
  -- The parked account's old sessions stop at their next refresh.
  IF pg_temp.hook(pg_temp.event(v_old, 'token_refresh')) IS DISTINCT FROM
       '{"error": {"http_code": 403, "message": "account_review"}}'::jsonb THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a parked account''s refresh was not refused';
  END IF;

  -- Auth moves the number to +999; the mirror follows; then mark.
  UPDATE auth.users SET phone = '999123456789012' WHERE id = v_old;
  IF (SELECT phone FROM public.users WHERE id = v_old) IS DISTINCT FROM '999123456789012' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the parked number was not mirrored to public.users';
  END IF;
  v_got := pg_temp.as_service(format('SELECT public.mark_fresh_start_parked(%L)::text', v_old));
  -- (A void comes back as '' through ::text.)
  IF v_got IS DISTINCT FROM '' OR (SELECT phone_released_at FROM public.users WHERE id = v_old) IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: mark_fresh_start_parked did not set phone_released_at: %', v_got;
  END IF;
  -- Single use: after the park, the nonce takes nothing.
  IF pg_temp.as_service(format('SELECT public.consume_fresh_start(%L)::text', v_nonce)) IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a used nonce was taken again after the park';
  END IF;
  IF (SELECT parked_phone_hmac FROM public.users WHERE id = v_old)
       IS DISTINCT FROM public.dating_phone_hmac('+1555840020') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: parked_phone_hmac moved off the real number';
  END IF;

  -- 15-minute expiry, a future issue time, tampering, rubbish.
  IF pg_temp.as_service(format('SELECT public.consume_fresh_start(%L)::text',
                               private.seal_fresh_start(v_back, now() - interval '16 minutes'))) IS NOT NULL
     OR pg_temp.as_service(format('SELECT public.consume_fresh_start(%L)::text',
                                  private.seal_fresh_start(v_back, now() + interval '5 minutes'))) IS NOT NULL
     OR pg_temp.as_service(format('SELECT public.consume_fresh_start(%L)::text',
                                  overlay(private.seal_fresh_start(v_back, now()) placing
                                          CASE WHEN substring(private.seal_fresh_start(v_back, now()) FROM 20 FOR 1) = '0' THEN '1' ELSE '0' END
                                          FROM 20 FOR 1))) IS NOT NULL
     OR pg_temp.as_service('SELECT public.consume_fresh_start(''deadbeef'')::text') IS NOT NULL
     OR pg_temp.as_service('SELECT public.consume_fresh_start(NULL)::text') IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an expired, future, tampered or malformed nonce was taken';
  END IF;
  -- Its owner came back since the challenge: no fresh start.
  v_nonce := private.seal_fresh_start(v_back, now() - interval '1 minute');
  UPDATE public.account_activity SET last_active_at = now() WHERE user_id = v_back;
  IF pg_temp.as_service(format('SELECT public.consume_fresh_start(%L)::text', v_nonce)) IS NOT NULL
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.account_holds WHERE user_id = v_back) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an account in use again was parked';
  END IF;
  -- Held for anything: no fresh start.
  PERFORM public.place_account_hold(v_held, 'safety_review');
  IF pg_temp.as_service(format('SELECT public.consume_fresh_start(%L)::text',
                               private.seal_fresh_start(v_held, now()))) IS NOT NULL
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.account_holds WHERE user_id = v_held AND kind = 'recovery_hold') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a held account was parked';
  END IF;

  -- Per IP, 5 an hour; an invalid try still counts (P-10).
  FOR i IN 1..5 LOOP
    IF pg_temp.as_service(format('SELECT public.consume_fresh_start(''deadbeef'', %L)::text', v_ip)) IS NOT NULL THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: rubbish was taken';
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM private.fresh_start_attempts WHERE ip_hash = v_ip) <> 5 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: invalid tries were not counted against the IP';
  END IF;
  v_got := pg_temp.as_service(format('SELECT public.consume_fresh_start(%L, %L)::text',
                                     private.seal_fresh_start(v_held, now()), v_ip));
  IF v_got IS DISTINCT FROM 'ERROR: fresh_start:try_later' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the 6th try in an hour from one IP was not refused: %', v_got;
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- E. The purge list (spec §4.3 W6a, §4.5 purge-recovery-holds).
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  v_due constant uuid := pg_temp.person('30', interval '400 days');
  v_waiting constant uuid := pg_temp.person('31', interval '400 days');
  v_resolved constant uuid := pg_temp.person('32', interval '400 days');
  v_ids uuid[];
BEGIN
  PERFORM public.place_account_hold(v_due, 'recovery_hold');
  PERFORM public.place_account_hold(v_waiting, 'recovery_hold');
  PERFORM public.place_account_hold(v_resolved, 'recovery_hold');
  UPDATE public.account_holds SET expires_at = now() - interval '1 day' WHERE user_id IN (v_due, v_resolved);
  UPDATE public.account_holds SET resolved_at = now() WHERE user_id = v_resolved;
  EXECUTE 'SET LOCAL ROLE service_role';
  SELECT array_agg(c) INTO v_ids FROM public.recovery_hold_candidates() AS c;
  RESET ROLE;
  IF NOT (v_due = ANY (v_ids)) OR v_waiting = ANY (v_ids) OR v_resolved = ANY (v_ids) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: recovery_hold_candidates listed the wrong accounts';
  END IF;
  IF NOT public.is_recovery_hold_due(v_due) OR public.is_recovery_hold_due(v_waiting)
     OR public.is_recovery_hold_due(v_resolved) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: is_recovery_hold_due answered wrongly';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- F. Alerts and identity health (S-8; spec §4.3 W6a; plan P-11).
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  v jsonb;
  v_ids bigint[];
BEGIN
  EXECUTE 'SET LOCAL ROLE service_role';
  v := public.identity_alerts_due();
  RESET ROLE;
  SELECT array_agg(x::bigint) INTO v_ids FROM jsonb_array_elements_text(v -> 'ids') AS x;
  IF (v ->> 'count')::int < 5 OR (v ->> 'count')::int <> cardinality(v_ids)
     OR v ->> 'first_at' IS NULL OR v ->> 'last_at' IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: identity_alerts_due did not list the fail-opens: %', v;
  END IF;
  IF (SELECT array_agg(k ORDER BY k) FROM jsonb_object_keys(v) AS k) <> ARRAY['count', 'first_at', 'ids', 'last_at'] THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an alert carries more than counts, ids and times';
  END IF;
  EXECUTE 'SET LOCAL ROLE service_role';
  PERFORM public.mark_identity_alerts_sent(v_ids);
  v := public.identity_alerts_due();
  RESET ROLE;
  IF (v ->> 'count')::int <> 0 OR (v -> 'ids') <> '[]'::jsonb THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: sent alerts are due again: %', v;
  END IF;

  EXECUTE 'SET LOCAL ROLE service_role';
  v := public.get_identity_health();
  RESET ROLE;
  IF NOT (v ?& ARRAY['accounts_without_birth_date', 'accounts_without_path', 'holds',
                     'unfinished_signups_awaiting_purge', 'fresh_starts_7d', 'parked_accounts',
                     'parked_without_hmac', 'hook_fail_opens_24h', 'hook_fail_opens_unalerted',
                     'fresh_start_key_present']) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: identity health lost a W1 count or lacks a W6a one: %', v;
  END IF;
  IF (v ->> 'fresh_starts_7d')::int < 1 OR (v ->> 'parked_accounts')::int < 1
     OR (v ->> 'hook_fail_opens_24h')::int < 5 OR (v ->> 'hook_fail_opens_unalerted')::int <> 0
     OR (v -> 'fresh_start_key_present') IS DISTINCT FROM 'true'::jsonb THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: identity health W6a counts are wrong: %', v;
  END IF;
  IF (SELECT prosrc FROM pg_proc WHERE oid = 'public.get_identity_health()'::regprocedure)
       ~* '(account_activity|fresh_start_challenges|auth_hook_failures|parked_phone_hmac)' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: get_identity_health names a W6 table itself (keep them in private.identity_health_w6a)';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- G. Retention (spec §4.6, §4.8).
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  v_uid constant uuid := pg_temp.person('40', interval '1 day');
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.fresh_start_challenges (nonce_hash, user_id, created_at, used_at)
  VALUES (repeat('a', 64), v_uid, now() - interval '61 minutes', now() - interval '60 minutes'),
         (repeat('b', 64), v_uid, now() - interval '59 minutes', now() - interval '58 minutes');
  INSERT INTO private.auth_hook_failures (at, sqlstate)
  VALUES (now() - interval '31 days', 'XX000'), (now() - interval '29 days', 'XX000');
  INSERT INTO private.fresh_start_attempts (ip_hash, created_at)
  VALUES (repeat('c', 64), now() - interval '61 minutes'), (repeat('d', 64), now() - interval '59 minutes');
  PERFORM private.fresh_start_retention();
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.fresh_start_challenges WHERE nonce_hash = repeat('a', 64))
     OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.fresh_start_challenges WHERE nonce_hash = repeat('b', 64))
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM private.auth_hook_failures WHERE at < now() - interval '30 days')
     OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM private.auth_hook_failures WHERE at BETWEEN now() - interval '30 days' AND now() - interval '28 days')
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM private.fresh_start_attempts WHERE ip_hash = repeat('c', 64))
     OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM private.fresh_start_attempts WHERE ip_hash = repeat('d', 64)) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: fresh-start-retention kept or removed the wrong rows';
  END IF;
END $$;
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write migration 11c and its twin**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261103100200_fresh_start.sql` with exactly:

```sql
-- Spec 1 W6a, migration 11 part 3 of 3 (spec §4.7 #11; plan P-1):
-- fresh_start. A recycled number gets a fresh start; the old account is
-- parked for 180 days of support-only recovery, then deleted.
--   * private.fresh_start_attempts: start-fresh-account's per-IP limit (5
--     an hour, spec §4.5), as an HMAC of the address; kept 1 hour.
--   * consume_fresh_start(p_nonce, p_ip_hash) -> uuid (service role): a
--     nonce the hook sealed in the last 15 minutes, for an account still
--     dormant and with no hold, taken once: the challenge is recorded as
--     used, users.parked_phone_hmac is written from the REAL auth.users
--     phone (review I-11) before anything changes the number, a
--     recovery_hold is placed (expires in 180 days) and the account id
--     comes back. The same nonce again returns the same id only while the
--     park isn't finished (phone_released_at unset), so a failed park can
--     be retried; anything else returns NULL (plan P-10: a NULL, not a
--     raise, so the IP attempt it counted is kept). Past the IP limit:
--     fresh_start:try_later.
--   * mark_fresh_start_parked(p_user) (service role): after the Auth number
--     moved to +999, sets users.phone_released_at.
--   * recovery_hold_candidates() / is_recovery_hold_due(p_user) (service
--     role): recovery holds past their 180 days, for purge-recovery-holds.
--   * identity_alerts_due() / mark_identity_alerts_sent(p_ids) (service
--     role): hook fail-opens not yet posted (review S-8).
--   * private.identity_health_w6a() and get_identity_health() re-created
--     from its live body (20261025100500) with one change: the W1 object
--     || private.identity_health_w6a(), so the W1 body still names no W6
--     table (identity contract I4; plan P-11).
--   * crons: fresh-start-retention (43 * * * *), purge-recovery-holds
--     (55 3 * * *), identity-health-alert (*/15 * * * *).
-- Grants in 20261103100210_fresh_start_grants.sql.
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS private.fresh_start_attempts (
  ip_hash text NOT NULL CONSTRAINT fresh_start_attempts_ip_hash_check CHECK (ip_hash ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT clock_timestamp()
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS fresh_start_attempts_key_idx ON private.fresh_start_attempts (ip_hash, created_at);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.consume_fresh_start(p_nonce text, p_ip_hash text DEFAULT NULL)
RETURNS uuid
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_now constant timestamptz := clock_timestamp();
  v_user uuid;
  v_issued timestamptz;
  v_hash text;
  v_taken public.fresh_start_challenges%ROWTYPE;
  v_created timestamptz;
  v_released timestamptz;
  v_digits text;
BEGIN
  IF p_ip_hash IS NOT NULL THEN
    IF p_ip_hash !~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$' THEN
      RETURN NULL;
    END IF;
    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('fresh-start-ip:' || p_ip_hash, 0));
    IF (SELECT count(*) FROM (SELECT 1 FROM private.fresh_start_attempts AS a
                               WHERE a.ip_hash = p_ip_hash AND a.created_at > v_now - interval '1 hour'
                               LIMIT 5) AS recent) >= 5 THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'fresh_start:try_later';
    END IF;
    INSERT INTO private.fresh_start_attempts (ip_hash, created_at) VALUES (p_ip_hash, v_now);
  END IF;

  SELECT o.user_id, o.issued_at INTO v_user, v_issued FROM private.open_fresh_start(p_nonce) AS o;
  IF v_user IS NULL
     OR v_issued > v_now + interval '1 minute'
     OR v_issued < v_now - interval '15 minutes' THEN
    RETURN NULL;
  END IF;

  -- The users row first (the order set_user_path_internal and the accept
  -- use), so two takes of one nonce serialise here.
  SELECT u.created_at, u.phone_released_at INTO v_created, v_released
    FROM public.users AS u WHERE u.id = v_user FOR UPDATE;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RETURN NULL;
  END IF;

  v_hash := encode(extensions.digest(p_nonce, 'sha256'), 'hex');
  SELECT * INTO v_taken FROM public.fresh_start_challenges AS c WHERE c.nonce_hash = v_hash;
  IF FOUND THEN
    -- Taken before: only to finish a park that didn't complete.
    IF v_taken.user_id = v_user AND v_released IS NULL
       AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.account_holds AS h
                    WHERE h.user_id = v_user AND h.kind = 'recovery_hold' AND h.resolved_at IS NULL) THEN
      RETURN v_user;
    END IF;
    RETURN NULL;
  END IF;

  -- Used again since the challenge (its owner is back), already parked, or
  -- held for any reason: no fresh start.
  IF coalesce((SELECT a.last_active_at FROM public.account_activity AS a WHERE a.user_id = v_user), v_created)
       >= now() - interval '180 days'
     OR v_released IS NOT NULL
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.account_holds AS h WHERE h.user_id = v_user AND h.resolved_at IS NULL) THEN
    RETURN NULL;
  END IF;

  INSERT INTO public.fresh_start_challenges (nonce_hash, user_id, created_at, used_at)
  VALUES (v_hash, v_user, v_issued, v_now);

  -- I-11: the real number's HMAC, in the form Spec 2's helper reads Auth's
  -- phone ('+' || digits), before start-fresh-account parks it.
  v_digits := nullif(regexp_replace(coalesce((SELECT a.phone FROM auth.users AS a WHERE a.id = v_user), ''), '\D', '', 'g'), '');
  UPDATE public.users AS u
     SET parked_phone_hmac = CASE WHEN v_digits IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE public.dating_phone_hmac('+' || v_digits) END
   WHERE u.id = v_user;
  PERFORM public.place_account_hold(v_user, 'recovery_hold', 'fresh_start');
  PERFORM private.bump_metric_internal('dormancy_fresh_start');
  RETURN v_user;
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.mark_fresh_start_parked(p_user uuid)
RETURNS void
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
BEGIN
  UPDATE public.users AS u
     SET phone_released_at = coalesce(u.phone_released_at, now())
   WHERE u.id = p_user
     AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.account_holds AS h
                  WHERE h.user_id = p_user AND h.kind = 'recovery_hold' AND h.resolved_at IS NULL)
     AND (SELECT a.phone FROM auth.users AS a WHERE a.id = p_user) ~ '^\+?999[0-9]{12}$';
  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'fresh_start:not_parked';
  END IF;
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.recovery_hold_candidates()
RETURNS SETOF uuid
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT h.user_id
    FROM public.account_holds AS h
   WHERE h.kind = 'recovery_hold' AND h.resolved_at IS NULL AND h.expires_at < now()
   ORDER BY h.expires_at, h.user_id
   LIMIT 200;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_recovery_hold_due(p_user uuid)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.account_holds AS h
                  WHERE h.user_id = p_user AND h.kind = 'recovery_hold'
                    AND h.resolved_at IS NULL AND h.expires_at < now());
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.identity_alerts_due()
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT jsonb_build_object(
    'ids', coalesce(jsonb_agg(f.id ORDER BY f.id), '[]'::jsonb),
    'count', count(*),
    'first_at', min(f.at),
    'last_at', max(f.at))
    FROM (SELECT x.id, x.at FROM private.auth_hook_failures AS x
           WHERE x.alerted_at IS NULL ORDER BY x.id LIMIT 500) AS f;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.mark_identity_alerts_sent(p_ids bigint[])
RETURNS void
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  UPDATE private.auth_hook_failures AS f
     SET alerted_at = now()
   WHERE f.id = ANY (coalesce(p_ids, ARRAY[]::bigint[])) AND f.alerted_at IS NULL;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.identity_health_w6a()
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT jsonb_build_object(
    -- Taken challenges (the hook's refusals can't be counted: Auth rolls
    -- the refused token's transaction back; plan P-2).
    'fresh_starts_7d', (SELECT coalesce(sum(m.count), 0) FROM public.metric_counters AS m
                         WHERE m.key = 'dormancy_fresh_start' AND m.day > (now() AT TIME ZONE 'utc')::date - 7),
    'parked_accounts', (SELECT count(*) FROM public.account_holds AS h
                         WHERE h.kind = 'recovery_hold' AND h.resolved_at IS NULL),
    'parked_without_hmac', (SELECT count(*) FROM public.account_holds AS h
                              JOIN public.users AS u ON u.id = h.user_id
                             WHERE h.kind = 'recovery_hold' AND h.resolved_at IS NULL
                               AND u.parked_phone_hmac IS NULL),
    'hook_fail_opens_24h', (SELECT count(*) FROM private.auth_hook_failures AS f
                             WHERE f.at > now() - interval '24 hours'),
    'hook_fail_opens_unalerted', (SELECT count(*) FROM private.auth_hook_failures AS f
                                   WHERE f.alerted_at IS NULL),
    'fresh_start_key_present', public.app_setting('fresh_start_key') IS NOT NULL);
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_identity_health()
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT jsonb_build_object(
    'accounts_without_birth_date', (
      SELECT count(*) FROM public.users AS u
       WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.account_birthdates AS b WHERE b.user_id = u.id)),
    'accounts_without_path', (SELECT count(*) FROM public.users AS u WHERE u.path IS NULL),
    'holds', jsonb_build_object(
      'age_review', (SELECT count(*) FROM public.account_holds AS h
                      WHERE h.kind = 'age_review' AND h.resolved_at IS NULL),
      'recovery_hold', (SELECT count(*) FROM public.account_holds AS h
                         WHERE h.kind = 'recovery_hold' AND h.resolved_at IS NULL),
      'safety_review', (SELECT count(*) FROM public.account_holds AS h
                         WHERE h.kind = 'safety_review' AND h.resolved_at IS NULL)),
    'unfinished_signups_awaiting_purge', (SELECT count(*) FROM private.unfinished_signups AS s))
    || private.identity_health_w6a();
$$;

-- Retention (spec §4.6, §4.8): challenges and IP attempts 1 hour, hook
-- fail-opens 30 days. Names only W6a tables.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.fresh_start_retention()
RETURNS void
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  DELETE FROM public.fresh_start_challenges WHERE created_at < clock_timestamp() - interval '1 hour';
  DELETE FROM private.fresh_start_attempts WHERE created_at < clock_timestamp() - interval '1 hour';
  DELETE FROM private.auth_hook_failures WHERE at < clock_timestamp() - interval '30 days';
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON private.fresh_start_attempts FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.consume_fresh_start(text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.mark_fresh_start_parked(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.recovery_hold_candidates() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.is_recovery_hold_due(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.identity_alerts_due() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.mark_identity_alerts_sent(bigint[]) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.identity_health_w6a() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.get_identity_health() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.fresh_start_retention() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;

DO $$
BEGIN
  PERFORM cron.unschedule(jobid) FROM cron.job
   WHERE jobname IN ('fresh-start-retention', 'purge-recovery-holds', 'identity-health-alert');
END;
$$;
SELECT cron.schedule('fresh-start-retention', '43 * * * *', $cron$SELECT private.fresh_start_retention()$cron$);
SELECT cron.schedule('purge-recovery-holds', '55 3 * * *',
                     $cron$SELECT public.invoke_edge_function('purge-recovery-holds')$cron$);
SELECT cron.schedule('identity-health-alert', '*/15 * * * *',
                     $cron$SELECT public.invoke_edge_function('identity-health-alert')$cron$);

COMMIT;
```

```sql
-- Spec 1 W6a: grants for 20261103100200_fresh_start.sql. The fresh start,
-- the purge list and the alerts are the edge functions' (service role);
-- the retention and the health helper are server-only.
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';
REVOKE ALL ON private.fresh_start_attempts FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.consume_fresh_start(text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.mark_fresh_start_parked(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.recovery_hold_candidates() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.is_recovery_hold_due(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.identity_alerts_due() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.mark_identity_alerts_sent(bigint[]) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.identity_health_w6a() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.get_identity_health() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.fresh_start_retention() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.consume_fresh_start(text, text) TO service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.mark_fresh_start_parked(uuid) TO service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.recovery_hold_candidates() TO service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.is_recovery_hold_due(uuid) TO service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.identity_alerts_due() TO service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.mark_identity_alerts_sent(bigint[]) TO service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.get_identity_health() TO service_role;
COMMIT;
```

```bash
  'park_while_active': ("       >= now() - interval '180 days'\n     OR v_released", "       >= now() + interval '1 day'\n     OR v_released"),
  'health_inline': ("    || private.identity_health_w6a();", "    || jsonb_build_object('x', (SELECT count(*) FROM private.auth_hook_failures)) || private.identity_health_w6a();"),
}
for name, (a, b) in muts.items():
    assert m.count(a) == 1, name
    (d / f"mut_{name}.sql").write_text(m.replace(a, b))
print('mutants written')
EOF
for m in hmac_form reuse_after_park expiry_20m invalid_raises park_while_active health_inline; do

done

rm $D/mut_*.sql
```

Create `supabase/functions/start-fresh-account/handler.test.ts` with exactly:

```ts
// Spec 1 W6a (§3.5, §4.5): start-fresh-account against a fake client.
import { type FreshStartClient, handleFreshStart, MAX_PARK_TRIES, parkedPhone } from "./handler.ts";

const OLD = "5f6a0000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000aa";
const NONCE = "ab".repeat(64);

interface Script {
  consume?: { data: unknown; error: { message: string } | null };
  phone?: string;
  takenTries?: number;
  updateError?: { message: string; status?: number; code?: string };
  markError?: { message: string };
}

function rig(script: Script = {}) {
  const calls: string[] = [];
  const logs: string[] = [];
  let taken = script.takenTries ?? 0;
  const client: FreshStartClient = {
    rpc(fn, args) {
      calls.push(`${fn}:${JSON.stringify(args)}`);
      if (fn === "consume_fresh_start") {
        return Promise.resolve(script.consume ?? { data: OLD, error: null });
      }
      return Promise.resolve({ data: null, error: script.markError ?? null });
    },
    auth: {
      admin: {
        getUserById(id) {
          calls.push(`get:${id}`);
          return Promise.resolve({ data: { user: { phone: script.phone ?? "233244123456" } }, error: null });
        },
        updateUserById(id, attributes) {
          calls.push(`update:${id}:${attributes.phone}:${attributes.phone_confirm}`);
          if (script.updateError) return Promise.resolve({ error: script.updateError });
          if (taken > 0) {
            taken--;
            return Promise.resolve({
              error: { message: "A user with this phone number has already been registered", status: 422, code: "phone_exists" },
            });
          }
          return Promise.resolve({ error: null });
        },
      },
    },
  };
  return { client, calls, logs };
}

function post(body: unknown, headers: Record<string, string> = { "cf-connecting-ip": "203.0.113.9" }) {
  return new Request("https://x/functions/v1/start-fresh-account", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...headers },
    body: typeof body === "string" ? body : JSON.stringify(body),
  });
}

async function run(script: Script, body: unknown = { nonce: NONCE }, ipKey: string | null = "ip-key") {
  const r = rig(script);
  const res = await handleFreshStart(post(body), {
    client: r.client,
    ipKey: ipKey ?? undefined,
    log: (c) => r.logs.push(c),
  });
  return { ...r, status: res.status, body: await res.json() };
}

function assert(condition: unknown, message: string): asserts condition {
  if (!condition) throw new Error(message);
}

Deno.test("a usable nonce parks the old number on +999 and marks it, in that order", async () => {
  const r = await run({});
  assert(r.status === 200 && r.body.ok === true, `got ${r.status} ${JSON.stringify(r.body)}`);
  const parked = await parkedPhone(OLD, 1);
  assert(/^\+999\d{12}$/.test(parked), `parked ${parked}`);
  const order = r.calls.map((c) => c.split(":")[0]);
  assert(
    JSON.stringify(order) === JSON.stringify(["consume_fresh_start", "get", "update", "mark_fresh_start_parked"]),
    `order ${order.join(",")}`,
  );
  assert(r.calls[2] === `update:${OLD}:${parked}:true`, `update ${r.calls[2]}`);
  assert(r.calls[3] === `mark_fresh_start_parked:{"p_user":"${OLD}"}`, `mark ${r.calls[3]}`);
});

Deno.test("a taken +999 number is retried with the next one; too many collisions are a 503", async () => {
  const two = await run({ takenTries: 2 });
  assert(two.status === 200, `two collisions: ${two.status}`);
  assert(two.calls.filter((c) => c.startsWith("update:")).length === 3, "three tries");
  assert(two.calls[4] === `update:${OLD}:${await parkedPhone(OLD, 3)}:true`, "the third number");
  const many = await run({ takenTries: MAX_PARK_TRIES });
  assert(many.status === 503 && many.body.error === "fresh_start:unavailable", `many: ${many.status}`);
  assert(!many.calls.some((c) => c.startsWith("mark_")), "never marked without a park");
});

Deno.test("an unfinished park retried: already on +999, so only the mark runs", async () => {
  const r = await run({ phone: "999123456789012" });
  assert(r.status === 200, `status ${r.status}`);
  assert(!r.calls.some((c) => c.startsWith("update:")), "no second park");
  assert(r.calls.some((c) => c.startsWith("mark_fresh_start_parked")), "marked");
});

Deno.test("an unusable nonce is one plain 400; rubbish is sent as '' so the IP still pays", async () => {
  const invalid = await run({ consume: { data: null, error: null } });
  assert(invalid.status === 400 && invalid.body.error === "fresh_start:invalid", `got ${invalid.status}`);
  assert(!invalid.calls.some((c) => c.startsWith("get:") || c.startsWith("update:")), "nothing parked");
  const rubbish = await run({ consume: { data: null, error: null } }, { nonce: "<script>" });
  assert(rubbish.calls[0].includes('"p_nonce":""'), `sent ${rubbish.calls[0]}`);
  const notJson = await run({ consume: { data: null, error: null } }, "not json");
  assert(notJson.status === 400, `not json: ${notJson.status}`);
});

Deno.test("the IP limit is a 429; a missing key or a failing call a 503", async () => {
  const limited = await run({ consume: { data: null, error: { message: "fresh_start:try_later" } } });
  assert(limited.status === 429 && limited.body.error === "fresh_start:try_later", `limited ${limited.status}`);
  const noKey = await run({}, { nonce: NONCE }, null);
  assert(noKey.status === 503 && noKey.calls.length === 0, `no key ${noKey.status}`);
  const down = await run({ consume: { data: null, error: { message: "boom" } } });
  assert(down.status === 503, `down ${down.status}`);
  const auth = await run({ updateError: { message: "Database error", status: 500 } });
  assert(auth.status === 503 && !auth.calls.some((c) => c.startsWith("mark_")), `auth ${auth.status}`);
  const mark = await run({ markError: { message: "fresh_start:not_parked" } });
  assert(mark.status === 503, `mark ${mark.status}`);
});

Deno.test("the IP is hashed with the key, never sent or logged; nothing identifying is logged", async () => {
  const r = await run({});
  assert(!r.calls[0].includes("203.0.113.9"), "raw IP sent");
  assert(/"p_ip_hash":"[0-9a-f]{64}"/.test(r.calls[0]), `ip hash ${r.calls[0]}`);
  const logged = r.logs.join(" ");
  assert(!logged.includes(OLD) && !logged.includes(NONCE) && !logged.includes("203.0.113.9"), `logged ${logged}`);
  assert(r.logs.at(-1) === "fresh_start:parked", `last log ${r.logs.at(-1)}`);
});
```

Create `supabase/functions/purge-recovery-holds/handler.test.ts` with exactly:

```ts
// Spec 1 W6a (§4.5): the recovery-hold purge, against a fake client.
import { candidateIds, purgeRecoveryHolds, type RecoveryPurgeClient } from "./handler.ts";

const A = "5f6a0000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000a1";
const B = "5f6a0000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b2";
const C = "5f6a0000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c3";

function rig(rows: unknown, o: { notDue?: string[]; prepareFails?: string[]; deleteFails?: string[]; listFails?: boolean } = {}) {
  const calls: string[] = [];
  const client: RecoveryPurgeClient = {
    rpc(fn, args) {
      const id = String((args as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.p_user ?? (args as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.p_user_id ?? "");
      calls.push(`${fn}:${id}`);
      if (fn === "recovery_hold_candidates") {
        return Promise.resolve(o.listFails ? { data: null, error: { message: "boom" } } : { data: rows, error: null });
      }
      if (fn === "is_recovery_hold_due") return Promise.resolve({ data: !(o.notDue ?? []).includes(id), error: null });
      return Promise.resolve({ data: null, error: (o.prepareFails ?? []).includes(id) ? { message: "nope" } : null });
    },
    auth: {
      admin: {
        deleteUser(id) {
          calls.push(`delete:${id}`);
          return Promise.resolve({ error: (o.deleteFails ?? []).includes(id) ? { message: "nope" } : null });
        },
      },
    },
  };
  return { client, calls };
}

function assert(condition: unknown, message: string): asserts condition {
  if (!condition) throw new Error(message);
}

Deno.test("each due account: re-checked, prepared, then deleted, in that order", async () => {
  const r = rig([A, { recovery_hold_candidates: B }]);
  const result = await purgeRecoveryHolds(r.client);
  assert(JSON.stringify(result) === JSON.stringify({ candidates: 2, deleted: 2, failed: 0, skipped: 0, remaining: 0 }), JSON.stringify(result));
  assert(
    r.calls.join(" ") ===
      `recovery_hold_candidates: is_recovery_hold_due:${A} prepare_account_deletion:${A} delete:${A} ` +
        `is_recovery_hold_due:${B} prepare_account_deletion:${B} delete:${B}`,
    r.calls.join(" "),
  );
});

Deno.test("a hold resolved since the list was read is skipped; a failed prepare never deletes", async () => {
  const r = rig([A, B, C], { notDue: [A], prepareFails: [B], deleteFails: [C] });
  const result = await purgeRecoveryHolds(r.client);
  assert(JSON.stringify(result) === JSON.stringify({ candidates: 3, deleted: 0, failed: 2, skipped: 1, remaining: 0 }), JSON.stringify(result));
  assert(!r.calls.includes(`delete:${A}`) && !r.calls.includes(`delete:${B}`), r.calls.join(" "));
});

Deno.test("a failed list read throws before anything is deleted; the budget leaves the rest", async () => {
  let message = "";
  try {
    await purgeRecoveryHolds(rig([A], { listFails: true }).client);
  } catch (error) {
    message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
  }
  assert(message === "recovery_hold_candidates_failed", message);
  let t = 0;
  const r = rig([A, B, C]);
  const result = await purgeRecoveryHolds(r.client, { now: () => (t += 40), budgetMs: 60 });
  assert(result.deleted === 1 && result.remaining === 2, JSON.stringify(result));
  assert(candidateIds(null).length === 0 && candidateIds([42, "x"]).length === 0, "non-ids read as nothing");
});
```

Create `supabase/functions/identity-health-alert/handler.test.ts` with exactly:

```ts
// Spec 1 W6a (§4.4 S-8, §4.5): fail-open alerts, against a fake client.
import { type AlertClient, sendAlerts } from "./handler.ts";

function assert(condition: unknown, message: string): asserts condition {
  if (!condition) throw new Error(message);
}

function rig(due: unknown, o: { readFails?: boolean; markFails?: boolean } = {}) {
  const calls: string[] = [];
  const client: AlertClient = {
    rpc(fn, args) {
      calls.push(`${fn}:${JSON.stringify(args ?? {})}`);
      if (fn === "identity_alerts_due") {
        return Promise.resolve(o.readFails ? { data: null, error: { message: "boom" } } : { data: due, error: null });
      }
      return Promise.resolve({ data: null, error: o.markFails ? { message: "boom" } : null });
    },
  };
  return { client, calls };
}

const DUE = { ids: [7, 8, 9], count: 3, first_at: "2026-11-03T10:00:00Z", last_at: "2026-11-03T10:05:00Z" };

Deno.test("due fail-opens: one message, counts and times only, then marked", async () => {
  const r = rig(DUE);
  const posts: string[] = [];
  const result = await sendAlerts(r.client, "https://hooks.example/x", async (_url, body) => {
    posts.push(body);
    return { ok: true, status: 200 };
  });
  assert(result.hook_fail_open === "sent", JSON.stringify(result));
  assert(posts.length === 1, `posts ${posts.length}`);
  const text = JSON.parse(posts[0]).text as string;
  assert(text.includes("3 time(s)") && text.includes("2026-11-03T10:00:00Z") && text.includes("incident"), text);
  assert(Object.keys(JSON.parse(posts[0])).join(",") === "text", posts[0]);
  assert(r.calls[1] === 'mark_identity_alerts_sent:{"p_ids":[7,8,9]}', r.calls.join(" "));
});

Deno.test("nothing due posts nothing; a failed post or no webhook marks nothing", async () => {
  let posted = 0;
  const post = async () => {
    posted++;
    return { ok: false, status: 500 };
  };
  const none = rig({ ids: [], count: 0, first_at: null, last_at: null });
  assert((await sendAlerts(none.client, "https://hooks.example/x", post)).hook_fail_open === "none", "none");
  assert(posted === 0, "posted for nothing");
  const failed = rig(DUE);
  assert((await sendAlerts(failed.client, "https://hooks.example/x", post)).hook_fail_open === "post_failed", "failed");
  assert(!failed.calls.some((c) => c.startsWith("mark_")), "marked after a failed post");
  const thrown = rig(DUE);
  const result = await sendAlerts(thrown.client, "https://hooks.example/x", () => Promise.reject(new Error("dns")));
  assert(result.hook_fail_open === "post_failed" && !thrown.calls.some((c) => c.startsWith("mark_")), "thrown");
  const unset = rig(DUE);
  assert((await sendAlerts(unset.client, undefined, post)).hook_fail_open === "not_configured", "unset");
  assert(!unset.calls.some((c) => c.startsWith("mark_")), "marked with no webhook");
});

Deno.test("a failed read or mark is reported, never thrown", async () => {
  const ok = async () => ({ ok: true, status: 200 });
  assert((await sendAlerts(rig(DUE, { readFails: true }).client, "https://x", ok)).hook_fail_open === "read_failed", "read");
  assert((await sendAlerts(rig(DUE, { markFails: true }).client, "https://x", ok)).hook_fail_open === "mark_failed", "mark");
});
```

Create `supabase/functions/start-fresh-account/handler.ts` with exactly:

```ts
// supabase/functions/start-fresh-account/handler.ts
//
// Spec 1 W6a (§3.5 DM1, §4.5): "I'm new to Attune" on a recycled number.
// The access-token hook refused the code sign-in with
// account_dormant:<nonce>; the app sends that nonce here (no session:
// verify_jwt = false). consume_fresh_start takes it (once, within 15
// minutes, 5 tries an hour per IP), records the real number's HMAC and
// places a 180-day recovery_hold; then the old account's Auth number is
// parked on +999 and 12 digits from its id, retried on a collision, and
// phone_released_at is set. The old account's sessions need nothing: the
// hook refuses every token for a held account. The app then sends a new
// code, which makes a new account.
//
// Answers: 200 {ok:true}; 400 {error:'fresh_start:invalid'} (any nonce
// that isn't usable, never why); 429 {error:'fresh_start:try_later'};
// 503 {error:'fresh_start:unavailable'}. Logs categories only: never the
// nonce, the number, the account or the IP.
import { clientIp } from "../_shared/client_ip.ts";
import { hmacHex, sha256Hex } from "../_shared/otp_phone.ts";

type RpcResult = PromiseLike<{ data: unknown; error: { message: string } | null }>;

export interface FreshStartClient {
  rpc(
    fn: "consume_fresh_start" | "mark_fresh_start_parked",
    args: Record<string, unknown>,
  ): RpcResult;
  auth: {
    admin: {
      getUserById(id: string): PromiseLike<{
        data: { user: { phone?: string | null } | null };
        error: { message: string } | null;
      }>;
      updateUserById(
        id: string,
        attributes: { phone: string; phone_confirm: boolean },
      ): PromiseLike<{ error: { message: string; status?: number; code?: string } | null }>;
    };
  };
}

export interface FreshStartDeps {
  client: FreshStartClient;
  /// OTP_HMAC_KEY: the IP is kept only as an HMAC. Unset: 503.
  ipKey: string | undefined;
  log(category: string): void;
}

const UUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
const NONCE = /^[0-9a-f]{128}$/;
const PARKED = /^\+?999[0-9]{12}$/;
export const MAX_PARK_TRIES = 5;

function json(body: Record<string, unknown>, status: number): Response {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
    status,
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  });
}

/// '+999' and 12 digits from SHA-256(`${userId}:${attempt}`): unassigned
/// (ITU), refused by the Send SMS Hook, and the same for a retry.
export async function parkedPhone(userId: string, attempt: number): Promise<string> {
  const hex = await sha256Hex(new TextEncoder().encode(`${userId}:${attempt}`));
  const digits = (BigInt(`0x${hex.slice(0, 16)}`) % 1_000_000_000_000n).toString().padStart(12, "0");
  return `+999${digits}`;
}

function isPhoneTaken(error: { message: string; status?: number; code?: string }): boolean {
  return error.code === "phone_exists" || /already (been )?registered|phone.*exists/i.test(error.message);
}

export async function handleFreshStart(req: Request, deps: FreshStartDeps): Promise<Response> {
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return json({ ok: true }, 200);
  if (req.method !== "POST") return json({ error: "fresh_start:invalid" }, 405);
  if (!deps.ipKey) {
    deps.log("fresh_start:not_configured");
    return json({ error: "fresh_start:unavailable" }, 503);
  }
  let nonce: unknown;
  try {
    nonce = (await req.json())?.nonce;
  } catch {
    nonce = null;
  }
  const ipHash = await hmacHex(deps.ipKey, `fresh-start-ip:${clientIp(req.headers)}`);
  // Rubbish still costs the IP a try: consume_fresh_start counts it.
  const { data, error } = await deps.client.rpc("consume_fresh_start", {
    p_nonce: typeof nonce === "string" && NONCE.test(nonce) ? nonce : "",
    p_ip_hash: ipHash,
  });
  if (error) {
    if (error.message.includes("fresh_start:try_later")) {
      deps.log("fresh_start:try_later");
      return json({ error: "fresh_start:try_later" }, 429);
    }
    deps.log("fresh_start:consume_failed");
    return json({ error: "fresh_start:unavailable" }, 503);
  }
  const userId = typeof data === "string" && UUID.test(data) ? data : null;
  if (!userId) {
    deps.log("fresh_start:invalid");
    return json({ error: "fresh_start:invalid" }, 400);
  }

  // Park the number (a retry of an unfinished park finds it parked already).
  const current = await deps.client.auth.admin.getUserById(userId);
  if (current.error) {
    deps.log("fresh_start:read_failed");
    return json({ error: "fresh_start:unavailable" }, 503);
  }
  let parked = PARKED.test(current.data.user?.phone ?? "");
  for (let attempt = 1; !parked && attempt <= MAX_PARK_TRIES; attempt++) {
    const { error: updateError } = await deps.client.auth.admin.updateUserById(userId, {
      phone: await parkedPhone(userId, attempt),
      phone_confirm: true,
    });
    if (!updateError) {
      parked = true;
    } else if (!isPhoneTaken(updateError)) {
      deps.log("fresh_start:park_failed");
      return json({ error: "fresh_start:unavailable" }, 503);
    }
  }
  if (!parked) {
    deps.log("fresh_start:park_collisions");
    return json({ error: "fresh_start:unavailable" }, 503);
  }
  const { error: markError } = await deps.client.rpc("mark_fresh_start_parked", { p_user: userId });
  if (markError) {
    deps.log("fresh_start:mark_failed");
    return json({ error: "fresh_start:unavailable" }, 503);
  }
  deps.log("fresh_start:parked");
  return json({ ok: true }, 200);
}
```

Create `supabase/functions/purge-recovery-holds/handler.ts` with exactly:

```ts
// supabase/functions/purge-recovery-holds/handler.ts
//
// Spec 1 W6a (§4.5, §4.8): an account parked for a fresh start is kept for
// 180 days of support-only recovery, then deleted through the existing
// deletion path: prepare_account_deletion, then auth.admin.deleteUser
// (delete-account's order; its cascade reaches everything below
// public.users). Spec 2's deletion steps read parked_phone_hmac, so carry-
// over follows the real number, not +999. Each id is re-checked with
// is_recovery_hold_due just before its delete (support may have resolved
// the hold since the list was read), and no new delete starts after
// RUN_BUDGET_MS. Logs and returns counts only.
type RpcResult = PromiseLike<{ data: unknown; error: { message: string } | null }>;

export interface RecoveryPurgeClient {
  rpc(
    fn: "recovery_hold_candidates" | "is_recovery_hold_due" | "prepare_account_deletion",
    args?: Record<string, unknown>,
  ): RpcResult;
  auth: {
    admin: {
      deleteUser(id: string): PromiseLike<{ error: { message: string } | null }>;
    };
  };
}

export interface RecoveryPurgeResult {
  candidates: number;
  deleted: number;
  failed: number;
  skipped: number;
  remaining: number;
}

export const RUN_BUDGET_MS = 60_000;

const UUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;

/// Bare values or one-key rows, whichever PostgREST sends; anything else is
/// skipped.
export function candidateIds(data: unknown): string[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(data)) return [];
  const ids: string[] = [];
  for (const row of data) {
    const value = typeof row === "string"
      ? row
      : row !== null && typeof row === "object"
      ? Object.values(row as Record<string, unknown>)[0]
      : null;
    if (typeof value === "string" && UUID.test(value)) ids.push(value);
  }
  return ids;
}

export async function purgeRecoveryHolds(
  client: RecoveryPurgeClient,
  options: { now?: () => number; budgetMs?: number } = {},
): Promise<RecoveryPurgeResult> {
  const now = options.now ?? Date.now;
  const budgetMs = options.budgetMs ?? RUN_BUDGET_MS;
  const startedAt = now();
  const { data, error } = await client.rpc("recovery_hold_candidates");
  if (error) throw new Error("recovery_hold_candidates_failed");
  const ids = candidateIds(data);
  let deleted = 0;
  let failed = 0;
  let skipped = 0;
  let remaining = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
    if (now() - startedAt >= budgetMs) {
      remaining = ids.length - i;
      break;
    }
    const id = ids[i];
    try {
      const { data: due, error: recheckError } = await client.rpc("is_recovery_hold_due", { p_user: id });
      if (recheckError) {
        failed++;
        continue;
      }
      if (due !== true) {
        skipped++;
        continue;
      }
      const { error: prepareError } = await client.rpc("prepare_account_deletion", { p_user_id: id });
      if (prepareError) {
        failed++;
        continue;
      }
      const { error: deleteError } = await client.auth.admin.deleteUser(id);
      if (deleteError) failed++;
      else deleted++;
    } catch {
      failed++;
    }
  }
  return { candidates: ids.length, deleted, failed, skipped, remaining };
}
```

Create `supabase/functions/purge-recovery-holds/index.ts` with exactly:

```ts
// supabase/functions/purge-recovery-holds/index.ts — see handler.ts.
// Run daily by the purge-recovery-holds cron (20261103100200) with the
// service-role key; any other caller gets 403. Logs counts only.
import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.177.0/http/server.ts";
import { HttpError, jsonResponse, requireServiceRole, serviceRoleClient } from "../_shared/attune_auth.ts";
import { purgeRecoveryHolds, type RecoveryPurgeClient } from "./handler.ts";

serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return jsonResponse({ ok: true });
  try {
    requireServiceRole(req);
    const result = await purgeRecoveryHolds(serviceRoleClient() as unknown as RecoveryPurgeClient);
    console.log("purge-recovery-holds", JSON.stringify(result));
    return jsonResponse({ success: true, ...result });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof HttpError) return jsonResponse({ error: error.message }, error.status);
    console.error("purge-recovery-holds failed:", error instanceof Error ? error.name : typeof error);
    return jsonResponse({ error: "purge_failed" }, 500);
  }
});
```

Create `supabase/functions/identity-health-alert/handler.ts` with exactly:

```ts
// supabase/functions/identity-health-alert/handler.ts
//
// Spec 1 W6a (§4.4 review S-8, §4.5): every access-token hook fail-open is
// an incident (a held account may have had a token). Every 15 minutes the
// cron runs this: identity_alerts_due() lists the fail-opens not posted
// yet; one message with their count and time span goes to
// SAFETY_ALERT_WEBHOOK (a destination the owner configures); only after
// the webhook accepted it are they marked sent, so a failed post is tried
// again next run. The message carries counts and times only: no user, no
// claims, no sqlstate.
type RpcResult = PromiseLike<{ data: unknown; error: { message: string } | null }>;

export interface AlertClient {
  rpc(fn: string, args?: Record<string, unknown>): RpcResult;
}

export type PostFn = (url: string, body: string) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; status: number }>;

/// One kind of alert: where its due rows come from, how they are marked,
/// and the line that says what happened. W6c adds child-safety reports.
export interface AlertSource {
  name: string;
  due: string;
  mark: string;
  line(count: number, firstAt: string, lastAt: string): string;
}

export const HOOK_FAIL_OPENS: AlertSource = {
  name: "hook_fail_open",
  due: "identity_alerts_due",
  mark: "mark_identity_alerts_sent",
  line: (count, firstAt, lastAt) =>
    `Attune: the access-token hook failed open ${count} time(s) between ${firstAt} and ${lastAt}. ` +
    "A held account may have received a token: treat this as an incident.",
};

export const ALERT_SOURCES: AlertSource[] = [HOOK_FAIL_OPENS];

export interface AlertResult {
  [source: string]: "none" | "sent" | "post_failed" | "read_failed" | "mark_failed" | "not_configured";
}

const UUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;

interface Due {
  /// bigint ids (hook fail-opens) or uuids (W6c's child-safety reports).
  ids: (number | string)[];
  count: number;
  firstAt: string;
  lastAt: string;
}

function readDue(data: unknown): Due | null {
  if (data === null || typeof data !== "object") return null;
  const row = data as Record<string, unknown>;
  const ids = Array.isArray(row.ids)
    ? row.ids.filter((x): x is number | string => Number.isInteger(x) || (typeof x === "string" && UUID.test(x)))
    : [];
  return {
    ids,
    count: typeof row.count === "number" ? row.count : ids.length,
    firstAt: String(row.first_at ?? ""),
    lastAt: String(row.last_at ?? ""),
  };
}

export async function sendAlerts(
  client: AlertClient,
  webhook: string | undefined,
  post: PostFn,
  sources: AlertSource[] = ALERT_SOURCES,
): Promise<AlertResult> {
  const result: AlertResult = {};
  for (const source of sources) {
    const { data, error } = await client.rpc(source.due);
    const due = error ? null : readDue(data);
    if (!due) {
      result[source.name] = "read_failed";
      continue;
    }
    if (due.ids.length === 0) {
      result[source.name] = "none";
      continue;
    }
    if (!webhook) {
      // Never marked: they stay due until a destination exists.
      result[source.name] = "not_configured";
      continue;
    }
    let sent = false;
    try {
      const response = await post(webhook, JSON.stringify({ text: source.line(due.count, due.firstAt, due.lastAt) }));
      sent = response.ok;
    } catch {
      sent = false;
    }
    if (!sent) {
      result[source.name] = "post_failed";
      continue;
    }
    const { error: markError } = await client.rpc(source.mark, { p_ids: due.ids });
    result[source.name] = markError ? "mark_failed" : "sent";
  }
  return result;
}
```

Create `supabase/functions/identity-health-alert/index.ts` with exactly:

```ts
// supabase/functions/identity-health-alert/index.ts — see handler.ts.
// Run every 15 minutes by the identity-health-alert cron (20261103100200)
// with the service-role key; any other caller gets 403.
import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.177.0/http/server.ts";
import { HttpError, jsonResponse, requireServiceRole, serviceRoleClient } from "../_shared/attune_auth.ts";
import { type AlertClient, sendAlerts } from "./handler.ts";

serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return jsonResponse({ ok: true });
  try {
    requireServiceRole(req);
    const result = await sendAlerts(
      serviceRoleClient() as unknown as AlertClient,
      Deno.env.get("SAFETY_ALERT_WEBHOOK"),
      async (url, body) => {
        const response = await fetch(url, {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body,
          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000),
        });
        return { ok: response.ok, status: response.status };
      },
    );
    console.log("identity-health-alert", JSON.stringify(result));
    return jsonResponse({ success: true, ...result });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof HttpError) return jsonResponse({ error: error.message }, error.status);
    console.error("identity-health-alert failed:", error instanceof Error ? error.name : typeof error);
    return jsonResponse({ error: "alert_failed" }, 500);
  }
});
```

### Task 4: The phone's hold kind, and the hook's refusals told apart (W3 batch 5 carry; W2 P-29; §3.15, §5.2; P-3, P-5, P-17, P-19)

**Files:**
- Modify: `lib/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart`, `lib/features/onboarding/data/home_state_store.dart`, `lib/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart`, `lib/features/onboarding/presentation/screens/onboarding_gate_v2.dart`, `lib/features/auth/data/passwordless_auth_service.dart`

- Create: `test/features/onboarding/w6/hold_kind_test.dart`, `test/features/auth/w6/passwordless_hook_refusal_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `hold_kind` in `get_my_home_state`; the hook's messages `account_review`, `account_review:age`, `account_dormant:<128 hex>`.
- Produces: `enum HoldKind { ageReview, safetyReview, recoveryHold, unknown }` with `wire` and `static HoldKind? fromWire(Object?)`; `bool holdBlocksDevice(HoldKind? kind)`; `HomeState.holdKind`, `CachedHomeState.holdKind`, `HomeSnapshot.holdKind` (all `HoldKind?`, optional constructor parameters); the cache's optional `hold_kind` key; in `passwordless_auth_service.dart`: `sealed class HookRefusal` with `static HookRefusal? of(Object error)`, `class AccountDormant(String nonce)`, `class AccountUnderReview({required bool age})`; `userSafeMessage` maps both. Test helper: `homeJson(..., String? holdKind)` (`hold: true` reads `age_review` unless named).

Create `test/features/onboarding/w6/hold_kind_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W6a (W3 batch 5 carry): the home state names the hold's kind; the
// phone keeps it beside the hold; only an age hold, or one whose kind isn't
// known, blocks the phone.
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/home_state_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

import '../../../helpers/fake_home_state.dart';

void main() {
  test('the kind is read from the wire; a name this build lacks is unknown', () {
    expect(HoldKind.fromWire(null), isNull);
    expect(HoldKind.fromWire('age_review'), HoldKind.ageReview);
    expect(HoldKind.fromWire('safety_review'), HoldKind.safetyReview);
    expect(HoldKind.fromWire('recovery_hold'), HoldKind.recoveryHold);
    expect(HoldKind.fromWire('from_a_later_spec'), HoldKind.unknown);
    expect(HoldKind.fromWire(7), HoldKind.unknown);
  });

  test('only a safety or recovery hold leaves the phone unblocked', () {
    expect(holdBlocksDevice(HoldKind.ageReview), isTrue);
    expect(holdBlocksDevice(HoldKind.unknown), isTrue);
    expect(holdBlocksDevice(null), isTrue, reason: 'as before W6a');
    expect(holdBlocksDevice(HoldKind.safetyReview), isFalse);
    expect(holdBlocksDevice(HoldKind.recoveryHold), isFalse);
  });

  test('get_my_home_state: hold_kind read; a server from before W6a reads null', () {
    final held = HomeState.fromJson(homeJson(hold: true, holdKind: 'safety_review'));
    expect(held.hold, isTrue);
    expect(held.holdKind, HoldKind.safetyReview);
    final old = homeJson(hold: true)..remove('hold_kind');
    expect(HomeState.fromJson(old).holdKind, isNull);
    expect(HomeState.fromJson(homeJson()).holdKind, isNull);
  });

  test('the cache keeps the kind beside the hold, and nothing about Dating', () async {
    SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues({});
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final store = HomeStateStore(prefs, userId: 'u1');
    await store.write(HomeState.fromJson(homeJson(hold: true, holdKind: 'recovery_hold')));
    expect(store.read()!.holdKind, HoldKind.recoveryHold);
    expect(prefs.getString(HomeStateStore.keyFor('u1')), isNot(contains('dating')));
    await store.write(HomeState.fromJson(homeJson()));
    expect(store.read()!.holdKind, isNull);
    expect(prefs.getString(HomeStateStore.keyFor('u1')), isNot(contains('hold_kind')));
    // A cache written before W6a.
    await prefs.setString(
      HomeStateStore.keyFor('u1'),
      '{"tab":"me","link":"none","needs":[],"hold":true,"at":"2026-11-01T00:00:00.000Z"}',
    );
    expect(store.read()!.hold, isTrue);
    expect(store.read()!.holdKind, isNull);
  });

  test('the snapshot carries the kind from the server, the cache and a queued write', () {
    final server = HomeSnapshot.fromServer(

    expect(cached.isComplete, isFalse);
```

Create `test/features/auth/w6/passwordless_hook_refusal_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W6a (W2 P-29, §3.5, §3.15, §4.4): the access-token hook's refusals
// are told apart from every other Auth error, and the legacy sign-in screen
// (flow_signin_v2 off) says H1's and DM1's words, never the hook's.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/features/auth/data/passwordless_auth_service.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

final _nonce = 'ab' * 64;

AuthException _hook(String message) => AuthException(message, statusCode: '403');

void main() {
  test('account_dormant:<nonce> is DM1, with the nonce', () {
    final refusal = HookRefusal.of(_hook('account_dormant:$_nonce'));
    expect(refusal, isA<AccountDormant>());
    expect((refusal as AccountDormant).nonce, _nonce);
  });

  test('account_review is H1; account_review:age is H1 for an age hold', () {
    final plain = HookRefusal.of(_hook('account_review'));
    expect(plain, isA<AccountUnderReview>());
    expect((plain as AccountUnderReview).age, isFalse);
    final age = HookRefusal.of(_hook('account_review:age'));
    expect((age as AccountUnderReview).age, isTrue);
  });

  test('anything else is not a refusal', () {
    expect(HookRefusal.of(_hook('account_reviewer')), isNull);
    expect(HookRefusal.of(_hook('account_dormant:abc')), isNull);
    expect(
      HookRefusal.of(
        const AuthException('Token has expired or is invalid', code: 'otp_expired', statusCode: '403'),
      ),
      isNull,
    );
    expect(HookRefusal.of(Exception('account_review')), isNull);
    expect(HookRefusal.of(TimeoutException('slow')), isNull);
  });

  test("the legacy screen's words: H1, DM1, and nothing of the hook's", () {
    final service = PasswordlessAuthService();
    final held = service.userSafeMessage(_hook('account_review:age'));
    expect(held, startsWith('We need to check your account.'));
    expect(held, isNot(contains('account_review')));
    final dormant = service.userSafeMessage(_hook('account_dormant:$_nonce'));
    expect(dormant, startsWith('Welcome to Attune.'));
    expect(dormant, isNot(contains(_nonce)));
    expect(
      service.userSafeMessage(TimeoutException('slow')),
      'That took too long. Check your connection and try again.',
    );
    expect(
      service.userSafeMessage(const AuthException('x', code: 'otp_expired')),
      'That code has expired. Request a new one.',
    );
  });
}
```

`lib/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart`, edit 1 of 4. Replace exactly:

```dart
@immutable
class HomeLink {
```

with:

```dart
/// Which hold an account is under (Spec 1 §4.2 `account_holds`, W6a), as
/// get_my_home_state() names it in `hold_kind`.
enum HoldKind {
  ageReview('age_review'),
  safetyReview('safety_review'),
  recoveryHold('recovery_hold'),

  /// A kind this build doesn't know (a later spec added it).
  unknown('unknown');

  const HoldKind(this.wire);

  final String wire;

  /// Null when the server named no kind (no hold, or a server or cache
  /// from before W6a); a name this build doesn't know is [unknown].
  static HoldKind? fromWire(Object? value) {
    if (value == null) return null;
    for (final kind in values) {
      if (kind != unknown && kind.wire == value) return kind;
    }
    return unknown;
  }
}

/// Whether a hold also blocks this phone (X1's 30-day block; rulings N-2,
/// F-8; W3 batch 5 carry): an age hold does, and so does a hold whose kind
/// isn't known (an older server or cache, a failed read, a kind this build
/// doesn't know), as every hold did before W6a. A safety or recovery hold
/// shows H1 and never blocks the phone: the next person on it may be
/// someone else entirely.
bool holdBlocksDevice(HoldKind? kind) =>
    kind != HoldKind.safetyReview && kind != HoldKind.recoveryHold;
```

`lib/features/auth/data/passwordless_auth_service.dart`, edit 1 of 2. Replace exactly:

```dart
class PasswordlessAuthResult {
```

with:

```dart
/// Why Supabase Auth's access-token hook refused a sign-in (Spec 1 W6a,
/// §3.5, §3.15, §4.4). The hook's own message reaches the app unchanged in
/// the AuthException (403): `account_dormant:<nonce>` for a recycled number
/// (DM1), `account_review` or `account_review:age` for a held account (H1).
sealed class HookRefusal {
  const HookRefusal();

  static final _dormant = RegExp(r'account_dormant:([0-9a-f]{128})');
  static final _review = RegExp(r'account_review(:age)?(?![a-z_])');

  /// The refusal in [error], or null when it is anything else.
  static HookRefusal? of(Object error) {
    if (error is! AuthException) return null;
    final dormant = _dormant.firstMatch(error.message);
    if (dormant != null) return AccountDormant(dormant.group(1)!);
    final review = _review.firstMatch(error.message);
    if (review != null) return AccountUnderReview(age: review.group(1) != null);
    return null;
  }
}

/// DM1: the number's account has had no activity for 180 days. [nonce] is
/// the one-use challenge start-fresh-account takes; held in memory only.
class AccountDormant extends HookRefusal {
  const AccountDormant(this.nonce);

  final String nonce;
}

/// H1: the account is held. [age]: an age hold, which also blocks this
/// phone (X1's block).
class AccountUnderReview extends HookRefusal {
  const AccountUnderReview({required this.age});

  final bool age;
}

class PasswordlessAuthResult {
```

Create `test/features/auth/sign_up/w6/sign_up_dormancy_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W6a (§3.5, §3.15; W3 carry C-2): a code sign-in the access-token
// hook refused. A recycled number: DM1 keeps the challenge in memory, the
// fresh start parks the old account and a new code makes a new account
// (A1/A2 kept, never N1). A held account: H1, the draft gone, the phone
// blocked for an age hold only.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/core/metrics/metric_counter.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/account_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/age_gate_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/fresh_start_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/sign_up_coordinator.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/sign_up_draft_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/domain/otp_failure.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/domain/sign_up_draft.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/onboarding_store.dart';
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

import '../../../../helpers/fake_account_gateway.dart';
import '../../../../helpers/fake_sign_up.dart';

final _now = DateTime.utc(2026, 11, 3, 12);
const _phone = '+233244123456';
final _nonce = 'cd' * 64;

class FakeFreshStart implements FreshStartGateway {
  final results = <FreshStartResult>[];
  final nonces = <String>[];

  @override
  Future<FreshStartResult> start(String nonce) async {
    nonces.add(nonce);
    return results.isEmpty ? FreshStartResult.done : results.removeAt(0);
  }
}

class _Rig {
  _Rig(this.prefs)
    : auth = FakeSignUpAuth(),
      gateway = FakeAccountGateway(
        homeState: FakeAccountGateway.account(hasAccount: false),
      );

  final SharedPreferences prefs;
  final sender = FakeCodeSender();
  final FakeSignUpAuth auth;
  final FakeAccountGateway gateway;
  final fresh = FakeFreshStart();

  SignUpDraftStore get drafts => SignUpDraftStore(prefs, now: () => _now);

  SignUpCoordinator coordinator() => SignUpCoordinator(
    drafts: drafts,
    sender: sender,
    auth: auth,
    accounts: AccountService(gateway),
    ageGate: AgeGateStore(prefs, now: () => _now),
    onboardingStoreFor: (uid) => OnboardingStore(prefs, scope: 'user.$uid'),
    metrics: MetricCounter((key) async {}),
    now: () => _now,
    freshStart: fresh,
  );

  /// The hook refuses the next verify with [message] (a 403, as Auth sends it).
  void refuse(String message) =>
      auth.failVerifyWith = AuthException(message, statusCode: '403');
}

void main() {
  TestWidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
  late SharedPreferences prefs;

  setUp(() async {
    SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues({});
    FlutterSecureStorage.setMockInitialValues({});
    prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  });

  Future<SignUpCoordinator> atCode(_Rig rig, SignUpEntry entry) async {
    final c = rig.coordinator();
    c.start(entry);
    if (entry == SignUpEntry.newAccount) {
      await c.saveName('Esi');
      expect(await c.saveBirthDate(DateTime.utc(1996, 3, 27)), isTrue);
    }
    expect(await c.sendCode(phoneE164: _phone, countryIso: 'GH'), isNull);
    return c;
  }

  test('a dormant number: DM1, then a fresh start and a new code make a new '
      'account with the name and date already typed', () async {
    final rig = _Rig(prefs);
    final c = await atCode(rig, SignUpEntry.newAccount);
    rig.refuse('account_dormant:$_nonce');
    expect(await c.verify('123456'), isA<VerifiedDormant>());
    expect(rig.auth.signOuts, 0);
    expect(
      prefs.getKeys().map((k) => '${prefs.get(k)}').join(' '),
      isNot(contains(_nonce)),
      reason: 'the challenge never reaches the phone\'s storage',
    );

    expect(await c.startFresh(), FreshStartOutcome.done);
    expect(rig.fresh.nonces, [_nonce]);
    expect(c.draft.entry, SignUpEntry.newAccount);
    expect(c.draft.lastSentAt, isNull, reason: 'DM2 sends a new code');
    expect(c.draft.phoneE164, _phone);

    expect(await c.sendCode(), isNull);
    expect(rig.sender.sent.last.$1, _phone);
    rig.auth
      ..failVerifyWith = null
      ..verifiesAs = 'u-new';
    final outcome = await c.verify('123456');
    expect(outcome, isA<VerifiedNew>());
    expect((outcome as VerifiedNew).next, SignUpStepId.consent);
  });

  test('"I already have an account" on a dormant number: after the fresh '
      'start, A1 and A2, never N1', () async {
    final rig = _Rig(prefs);
    final c = await atCode(rig, SignUpEntry.returning);
    rig.refuse('account_dormant:$_nonce');
    expect(await c.verify('123456'), isA<VerifiedDormant>());
    expect(await c.startFresh(), FreshStartOutcome.done);
    expect(await c.sendCode(), isNull);
    rig.auth.failVerifyWith = null;
    final outcome = await c.verify('123456');
    expect((outcome as VerifiedNew).next, SignUpStepId.name);
  });

  test('a used or expired challenge: A3 with the number open; nothing is kept', () async {
    final rig = _Rig(prefs);
    final c = await atCode(rig, SignUpEntry.newAccount);
    rig.refuse('account_dormant:$_nonce');
    await c.verify('123456');
    rig.fresh.results.add(FreshStartResult.invalid);
    expect(await c.startFresh(), FreshStartOutcome.expired);
    expect(c.phoneForA3, _phone);
    expect(c.draft.phoneE164, isNull);
    expect(await c.startFresh(), FreshStartOutcome.expired, reason: 'the challenge is gone');
    expect(rig.fresh.nonces, hasLength(1));
  });

  test('offline or too many tries: DM1 stays, and the same challenge is tried again', () async {
    final rig = _Rig(prefs);
    final c = await atCode(rig, SignUpEntry.newAccount);
    rig.refuse('account_dormant:$_nonce');
    await c.verify('123456');
    rig.fresh.results.addAll([FreshStartResult.unavailable, FreshStartResult.tryLater]);
    expect(await c.startFresh(), FreshStartOutcome.failed);
    expect(await c.startFresh(), FreshStartOutcome.tryLater);
    expect(await c.startFresh(), FreshStartOutcome.done);
    expect(rig.fresh.nonces, [_nonce, _nonce, _nonce]);
  });

  test('an age hold: H1, the draft gone and the phone blocked (W3 carry C-2)', () async {
    final rig = _Rig(prefs);
    final c = await atCode(rig, SignUpEntry.newAccount);
    rig.refuse('account_review:age');
    final outcome = await c.verify('123456');
    expect(outcome, isA<VerifiedHeld>());
    expect((outcome as VerifiedHeld).age, isTrue);
    expect(AgeGateStore.readBlocked(prefs, _now), isTrue);
    expect(rig.drafts.read(), isNull);
  });

  test('a safety or recovery hold: H1 and the draft gone; the phone is not blocked', () async {
    final rig = _Rig(prefs);
    final c = await atCode(rig, SignUpEntry.returning);
    rig.refuse('account_review');
    final outcome = await c.verify('123456');
    expect((outcome as VerifiedHeld).age, isFalse);
    expect(AgeGateStore.readBlocked(prefs, _now), isFalse);
    expect(rig.drafts.read(), isNull);
  });

  test('a wrong code is still a wrong code (the hook\'s 403s are told apart)', () async {
    final rig = _Rig(prefs);
    final c = await atCode(rig, SignUpEntry.newAccount);
    final outcome = await c.verify('000000');
    expect((outcome as VerifyFailed).failure, OtpVerifyFailure.wrongCode);
  });
}
```

Create `test/features/auth/sign_up/w6/fresh_start_service_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W6a (§4.5): start-fresh-account's answers, as DM1 reads them.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/fresh_start_service.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

class _Functions extends Fake implements FunctionsClient {
  _Functions(this.answer);

  final Future<FunctionResponse> Function() answer;
  final calls = <(String, Object?)>[];

  @override
  dynamic noSuchMethod(Invocation invocation) {
    if (invocation.memberName == #invoke) {
      calls.add((invocation.positionalArguments.first as String, invocation.namedArguments[#body]));
      return answer();
    }
    return super.noSuchMethod(invocation);
  }
}

Future<FreshStartResult> _start(Future<FunctionResponse> Function() answer, [List<(String, Object?)>? seen]) {
  final functions = _Functions(answer);
  return SupabaseFreshStartGateway(() => functions).start('ab' * 64).whenComplete(() => seen?.addAll(functions.calls));
}

void main() {
  test('200 is done; the nonce goes in the body to start-fresh-account', () async {
    final seen = <(String, Object?)>[];
    expect(await _start(() async => FunctionResponse(status: 200, data: {'ok': true}), seen), FreshStartResult.done);
    expect(seen.single.$1, 'start-fresh-account');
    expect(seen.single.$2, {'nonce': 'ab' * 64});
  });

  test('400 invalid, 429 try later, anything else unavailable', () async {
    Future<FunctionResponse> fail(int status) =>
        Future.error(FunctionException(status: status, details: {'error': 'x'}));
    expect(await _start(() => fail(400)), FreshStartResult.invalid);
    expect(await _start(() => fail(429)), FreshStartResult.tryLater);
    expect(await _start(() => fail(503)), FreshStartResult.unavailable);
    expect(await _start(() => Future.error(Exception('offline'))), FreshStartResult.unavailable);
  });
}
```

```dart
// Spec 1 W6a (§3.5 DM1-DM2, §3.15 H1, §2.2, §2.3; W3 carry C-2): /signup
// when the access-token hook refused the code. DM1 -> the fresh start ->
// DM2 -> a new code -> A5; H1 with the phone blocked for an age hold only;
// none of it animated; goldens at 1.0, 1.3, 2.0 and right to left.
//   flutter test --update-goldens test/features/auth/sign_up/w6/sign_up_dormancy_flow_test.dart
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:attune/core/platform/sim_country.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/ui/motion/shared_axis_step.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/account_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/age_gate_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/fresh_start_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/sign_up_draft_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/sign_up_gateways.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/sms_code_listener.dart';
```
