```dart
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/screens/sign_up_flow.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/steps/dormant_step.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/steps/fresh_start_step.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

import '../../../../helpers/fake_account_gateway.dart';
import '../../../../helpers/fake_sign_up.dart';
import '../../../../helpers/flow_harness.dart';
import 'sign_up_dormancy_test.dart' show FakeFreshStart;

final _now = DateTime.utc(2026, 11, 3, 12);
final _nonce = 'cd' * 64;

SignUpDraft _atCode({SignUpEntry entry = SignUpEntry.newAccount}) =>
    SignUpDraft.start(entry, now: _now).copyWith(
      name: entry == SignUpEntry.newAccount ? 'Ama' : null,
      birthDate: entry == SignUpEntry.newAccount ? DateTime.utc(1990, 1, 1) : null,
      phoneE164: '+233244123456',
      countryIso: 'GH',
      lastSentAt: _now,
      sendCount: 1,
    );

class _Rig {
  final sender = FakeCodeSender();
  final auth = FakeSignUpAuth();
  final gateway = FakeAccountGateway(
    homeState: FakeAccountGateway.account(hasAccount: false),
  );
  final fresh = FakeFreshStart();

  List<Override> get overrides => [
    codeSenderProvider.overrideWithValue(sender),
    signUpAuthProvider.overrideWithValue(auth),
    accountServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(AccountService(gateway)),
    signUpClockProvider.overrideWithValue(() => _now),
    deviceCountryProvider.overrideWith((ref) async => (sim: 'GH', region: null)),
    smsCodeListenerProvider.overrideWithValue(FakeSmsCodeListener()),
    freshStartGatewayProvider.overrideWithValue(fresh),
  ];
}

Future<FlowHarness> _pumpAtCode(
  WidgetTester tester,
  _Rig rig, {
  SignUpEntry entry = SignUpEntry.newAccount,
}) => pumpFlowScreen(
  tester,
  () => SignUpFlow(entry: entry),
  prefsValues: {
    SignUpDraftStore.key: const JsonEncoder().convert(_atCode(entry: entry).toJson()),
  },
  overrides: rig.overrides,
);

Future<void> _enterCode(WidgetTester tester) async {
  await tester.enterText(find.byType(TextField), '123456');
  await tester.pump();
  await tester.pumpAndSettle();
}

void main() {
  testWidgets('DM1 -> "I\'m new to Attune" -> DM2 -> a new code -> A5', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final rig = _Rig();
    rig.auth.failVerifyWith = AuthException('account_dormant:$_nonce', statusCode: '403');
    await _pumpAtCode(tester, rig);
    await _enterCode(tester);

    expect(find.text('Welcome to Attune'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text("Let's get you set up."), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Used Attune before on this number? Contact support'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.textContaining('Ama'), findsNothing, reason: 'nothing about any account is shown');
    expect(find.byType(SharedAxisStep), findsNothing, reason: 'DM1 is never animated');

    await tester.tap(find.text("I'm new to Attune"));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(rig.fresh.nonces, [_nonce]);
    expect(find.text('One more code to finish'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text("We'll send a new code to +233 24 412 3456."), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.byType(SharedAxisStep), findsNothing, reason: 'DM2 is never animated');

    await tester.tap(find.text('Send code'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(rig.sender.sent.last.$1, '+233244123456');
    expect(find.text('Enter the code'), findsOneWidget);

    rig.auth
      ..failVerifyWith = null
      ..verifiesAs = 'u-new';
    await tester.enterText(find.byType(TextField), '123456');
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 500));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Before you start'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('DM1 offline: said, and the same challenge is tried again', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final rig = _Rig()..fresh.results.add(FreshStartResult.unavailable);
    rig.auth.failVerifyWith = AuthException('account_dormant:$_nonce', statusCode: '403');
    await _pumpAtCode(tester, rig);
    await _enterCode(tester);
    await tester.tap(find.text("I'm new to Attune"));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(
      find.text("We couldn't reach Attune. Check your connection and try again."),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    expect(find.text('Welcome to Attune'), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.text("I'm new to Attune"));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(rig.fresh.nonces, [_nonce, _nonce]);
    expect(find.text('One more code to finish'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('DM1 with a spent challenge: back to A3, said', (tester) async {
    final rig = _Rig()..fresh.results.add(FreshStartResult.invalid);
    rig.auth.failVerifyWith = AuthException('account_dormant:$_nonce', statusCode: '403');
    await _pumpAtCode(tester, rig);
    await _enterCode(tester);
    await tester.tap(find.text("I'm new to Attune"));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text("What's your phone number?"), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('That took too long. Enter your number again.'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('an age hold at sign-in: H1 and this phone blocked (W3 carry C-2)', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final rig = _Rig();
    rig.auth.failVerifyWith = const AuthException('account_review:age', statusCode: '403');
    final h = await _pumpAtCode(tester, rig, entry: SignUpEntry.returning);
    await _enterCode(tester);
    expect(find.text('We need to check your account'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Contact support'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.byType(SharedAxisStep), findsNothing, reason: 'H1 is never animated');
    expect(AgeGateStore.readBlocked(h.prefs, DateTime.now()), isTrue);
    expect(h.routing.underAgeBlocked, isTrue);
    expect(SignUpDraftStore.readFrom(h.prefs, _now), isNull);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Sign out'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(h.location, '/welcome');
  });

  testWidgets('a safety hold at sign-in: H1, and this phone is not blocked', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final rig = _Rig();
    rig.auth.failVerifyWith = const AuthException('account_review', statusCode: '403');
    final h = await _pumpAtCode(tester, rig, entry: SignUpEntry.returning);
    await _enterCode(tester);
    expect(find.text('We need to check your account'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(AgeGateStore.readBlocked(h.prefs, DateTime.now()), isFalse);
    expect(h.routing.underAgeBlocked, isFalse);
    expect(find.text("That code didn't work. Check it and try again."), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('DM1 and DM2 meet the guidelines', (tester) async {
    await pumpFlowScreen(tester, () => DormantStep(onNew: () {}));
    await expectFlowGuidelines(tester);
    await pumpFlowScreen(
      tester,
      () => FreshStartStep(phoneE164: '+233244123456', onSend: () {}),
    );
    await expectFlowGuidelines(tester);
  });

  testWidgets('DM1 goldens', (tester) async {
    await expectFlowGoldens(tester, 'step_dormant', () => DormantStep(onNew: () {}));
  });

  testWidgets('DM1 with its error, goldens', (tester) async {
    await expectFlowGoldens(
      tester,
      'step_dormant_error',
      () => DormantStep(
        onNew: () {},
        errorText: "We couldn't reach Attune. Check your connection and try again.",
      ),
    );
  });

  testWidgets('DM2 goldens', (tester) async {
    await expectFlowGoldens(
      tester,
      'step_fresh_start',
      () => FreshStartStep(phoneE164: '+233244123456', onSend: () {}),
    );
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The fresh-start gateway and the two steps**

Create `lib/features/auth/sign_up/data/fresh_start_service.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

/// What start-fresh-account answered (Spec 1 W6a, §3.5 DM1, §4.5).
enum FreshStartResult {
  /// The old account is parked; a new code makes a new account.
  done,

  /// The nonce can't be used (used, older than 15 minutes, not ours).
  invalid,

  /// Five tries an hour from this network.
  tryLater,

  /// Offline, a timeout, or the server couldn't finish.
  unavailable,
}

/// The one call DM1's "I'm new to Attune" makes, with no session.
abstract class FreshStartGateway {
  Future<FreshStartResult> start(String nonce);
}

class SupabaseFreshStartGateway implements FreshStartGateway {
  SupabaseFreshStartGateway(this._functions);

  final FunctionsClient Function() _functions;

  static const timeout = Duration(seconds: 20);

  @override
  Future<FreshStartResult> start(String nonce) async {
    try {
      await _functions()
          .invoke('start-fresh-account', body: {'nonce': nonce})
          .timeout(timeout);
      return FreshStartResult.done;
    } on FunctionException catch (error) {
      return switch (error.status) {
        400 => FreshStartResult.invalid,
        429 => FreshStartResult.tryLater,
        _ => FreshStartResult.unavailable,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      // Never the nonce or the number (spec §4.1 rule 5).
      debugPrint('[fresh-start] failed: ${error.runtimeType}');
      return FreshStartResult.unavailable;
    }
  }
}

final freshStartGatewayProvider = Provider<FreshStartGateway>(
  (ref) => SupabaseFreshStartGateway(() => Supabase.instance.client.functions),
);
```

Create `lib/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/steps/dormant_step.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/core/utils/constants.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/utils/url_launcher_utils.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_buttons.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_frame.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_text_field.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/submit_once.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/semantics.dart';

/// DM1 · Welcome (Spec 1 §3.5, W6a): the number's account has had no
/// activity for 180 days. Nothing about that account is shown: no name, no
/// path, no partner, no dates. Never animated (§2.2). "Used Attune before
/// on this number?" goes to support, which never confirms or denies an
/// account.
class DormantStep extends StatefulWidget {
  const DormantStep({
    super.key,
    required this.onNew,
    this.errorText,
    this.errorCount = 0,
  });

  /// "I'm new to Attune"; the step stays busy until it has finished.
  final FutureOr<void> Function() onNew;

  /// The fresh start couldn't run; raise [errorCount] to read it again.
  final String? errorText;
  final int errorCount;

  @override
  State<DormantStep> createState() => _DormantStepState();
}

class _DormantStepState extends State<DormantStep> with SubmitOnce {
  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant DormantStep oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    final error = widget.errorText;
    if (error != null &&
        (error != oldWidget.errorText ||
            widget.errorCount != oldWidget.errorCount)) {
      SemanticsService.announce(error, Directionality.of(context));
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final errorText = widget.errorText;
    return FlowFrame(
      title: loc.flow_dormantTitle,
      body: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Text(
            loc.flow_dormantBody,
            style: theme.textTheme.bodyLarge?.copyWith(
              color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
            ),
          ),
          if (errorText != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            FlowErrorLine(
              text: errorText,
              style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium,
              liveRegion: true,
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
      actions: [
        FlowPrimaryButton(
          label: loc.flow_dormantNew,
          busy: submitting,
          onPressed: submitting ? null : () => submitOnce(widget.onNew),
        ),
        FlowTextLink(
          label: loc.flow_dormantUsedBefore,
          onPressed:
              () => UrlLauncherUtils.launchEmail(
                context: context,
                email: AppConstants.supportEmail,
              ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
```

Create `lib/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/steps/fresh_start_step.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/domain/phone_entry.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_buttons.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_frame.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_text_field.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/submit_once.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/semantics.dart';

/// DM2 · One more code to finish (Spec 1 §3.5, W6a): after "I'm new to
/// Attune" parked the old account, a new code to the same number makes a
/// new account. Never animated (§2.2).
class FreshStartStep extends StatefulWidget {
  const FreshStartStep({
    super.key,
    required this.phoneE164,
    required this.onSend,
    this.errorText,
    this.errorCount = 0,
  });

  final String phoneE164;

  /// "Send code"; the step stays busy until it has finished.
  final FutureOr<void> Function() onSend;

  /// The code couldn't be sent; raise [errorCount] to read it again.
  final String? errorText;
  final int errorCount;

  @override
  State<FreshStartStep> createState() => _FreshStartStepState();
}

class _FreshStartStepState extends State<FreshStartStep> with SubmitOnce {
  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant FreshStartStep oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    final error = widget.errorText;
    if (error != null &&
        (error != oldWidget.errorText ||
            widget.errorCount != oldWidget.errorCount)) {
      SemanticsService.announce(error, Directionality.of(context));
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final errorText = widget.errorText;
    return FlowFrame(
```

```dart
  Future<VerifyOutcome> _refused(HookRefusal refusal) async {
    switch (refusal) {
      case AccountDormant(:final nonce):
        _dormantNonce = nonce;
        return const VerifiedDormant();

  Future<FreshStartOutcome> startFresh() async {

      result = FreshStartResult.unavailable;

    switch (result) {

        _dormantNonce = null;
        _draft = _draft.withoutCode().copyWith(entry: SignUpEntry.newAccount);

        return FreshStartOutcome.done;
      case FreshStartResult.invalid:
        _dormantNonce = null;
        _setNumberOpen();
        await _save();
        return FreshStartOutcome.expired;
      case FreshStartResult.tryLater:
        return FreshStartOutcome.tryLater;
      case FreshStartResult.unavailable:
        return FreshStartOutcome.failed;
```

# W6b · Age signals and date-of-birth correction (Tasks 6-8)

### Task 6: Migration 12: the one date-of-birth correction (§3.15, §4.3 W6b, §4.7 #12, §7.1; P-21, P-22)

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261103110000_birth_date_correction.sql`
- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261103110010_birth_date_correction_grants.sql`
- Modify: `supabase/tests/dormancy_contracts.sql` (section H)
- Modify: `scripts/local_pg_grants.sql` (one `\i` block; never run)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `public.account_birthdates`, `private.age_on_utc_today`, `public.place_account_hold`, `public.dating_enrollments`; Task 1's hook (the contract checks a corrected minor gets no token).
- Produces: `private.birth_date_correction_open(p_user uuid) → boolean` (server-only); `public.correct_my_birth_date(p_birth_date date) → jsonb` (authenticated): `{status:'ok', age, changed}` | `{status:'not_eligible'}` (age hold placed, committed) | raises `birth_date:not_signed_in|invalid|not_set|correction_closed`; `public.get_my_birth_date_status() → jsonb {age int|null, can_correct bool, correct_until timestamptz|null}` (authenticated; never the date).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing section**

In `supabase/tests/dormancy_contracts.sql`, edit 1 of 1. Replace exactly:

```sql
-- (W6b adds section H, the date-of-birth correction, above this line.)
```

with:

```sql
-- =====================================================================
-- H. W6b: the date-of-birth correction (§3.15, §4.3 W6b; plan P-21,
--    P-22). Once, within 7 days of the declaration, never while Dating is
--    active; the same date spends nothing; under 18 places an age hold
--    that commits; the date itself is never returned.
-- =====================================================================
CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.dob(p_n text, p_birth date, p_declared interval) RETURNS uuid LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE
  v_uid constant uuid := pg_temp.person(p_n, interval '1 hour');
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.account_birthdates (user_id, birth_date, declared_at)
  VALUES (v_uid, p_birth, now() - p_declared);
  RETURN v_uid;
END $$;

CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.correct(p_user uuid, p_date text) RETURNS text LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE
  v text;
BEGIN
  PERFORM set_config('request.jwt.claims', json_build_object('sub', p_user, 'role', 'authenticated')::text, true);
  PERFORM set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub', coalesce(p_user::text, ''), true);
  EXECUTE 'SET LOCAL ROLE authenticated';
  BEGIN
    EXECUTE format('SELECT public.correct_my_birth_date(%L::date)::text', p_date) INTO v;
  EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    v := 'ERROR: ' || SQLERRM;
  END;
  RESET ROLE;
  RETURN v;
END $$;

-- [p_text] as jsonb, or NULL when it is a refusal.
CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.as_json(p_text text) RETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE sql AS $$
  SELECT CASE WHEN p_text LIKE '{%' THEN p_text::jsonb END;
$$;

CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.dob_status(p_user uuid) RETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE
  v jsonb;
BEGIN
  PERFORM set_config('request.jwt.claims', json_build_object('sub', p_user, 'role', 'authenticated')::text, true);
  PERFORM set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub', coalesce(p_user::text, ''), true);
  EXECUTE 'SET LOCAL ROLE authenticated';
  v := public.get_my_birth_date_status();
  RESET ROLE;
  RETURN v;
END $$;

DO $$
DECLARE
  v_fresh constant uuid := pg_temp.dob('50', DATE '1990-05-01', interval '1 day');
  v_late constant uuid := pg_temp.dob('51', DATE '1990-05-01', interval '8 days');
  v_dating constant uuid := pg_temp.dob('52', DATE '1990-05-01', interval '1 day');
  v_minor constant uuid := pg_temp.dob('53', DATE '1990-05-01', interval '1 day');
  v_none constant uuid := pg_temp.person('54', interval '1 hour');
  v_kid constant text := ((now() AT TIME ZONE 'utc')::date - interval '15 years')::date::text;
  v jsonb;
  v_got text;
BEGIN
  IF to_regprocedure('public.correct_my_birth_date(date)') IS NULL
     OR to_regprocedure('public.get_my_birth_date_status()') IS NULL
     OR to_regprocedure('private.birth_date_correction_open(uuid)') IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: W6b objects are missing';
  END IF;
  IF NOT has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'public.correct_my_birth_date(date)', 'EXECUTE')
     OR NOT has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'public.get_my_birth_date_status()', 'EXECUTE')
     OR has_function_privilege('anon', 'public.correct_my_birth_date(date)', 'EXECUTE')
     OR has_function_privilege('anon', 'public.get_my_birth_date_status()', 'EXECUTE')
     OR has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'private.birth_date_correction_open(uuid)', 'EXECUTE') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: W6b grants are wrong';
  END IF;

  v := pg_temp.dob_status(v_fresh);
  IF (v ->> 'can_correct')::boolean IS NOT TRUE OR (v ->> 'age')::int < 30
     OR (v ->> 'correct_until')::timestamptz NOT BETWEEN now() + interval '5 days 23 hours' AND now() + interval '6 days 1 hour'
     OR v::text ~ '1990' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the status for a new declaration is wrong (or shows the date): %', v;
  END IF;

  -- Once.
  v_got := pg_temp.correct(v_fresh, '1991-06-02');
  IF (pg_temp.as_json(v_got) ->> 'status') IS DISTINCT FROM 'ok' OR (pg_temp.as_json(v_got) ->> 'changed')::boolean IS NOT TRUE
     OR v_got ~ '1991' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a first correction inside 7 days was refused (or echoed the date): %', v_got;
  END IF;
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.account_birthdates WHERE user_id = v_fresh AND birth_date = DATE '1991-06-02'
                   AND self_corrections = 1 AND corrected_at IS NOT NULL AND source = 'self_declared') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the correction was not recorded as one self-correction';
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.correct(v_fresh, '1992-07-03') IS DISTINCT FROM 'ERROR: birth_date:correction_closed' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a second correction was allowed';
  END IF;
  -- The same date again: nothing changes, nothing is spent, no refusal.
  IF (pg_temp.as_json(pg_temp.correct(v_fresh, '1991-06-02')) ->> 'changed')::boolean IS NOT FALSE THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the same date again was refused or counted';
  END IF;
  IF (pg_temp.dob_status(v_fresh) ->> 'can_correct')::boolean IS NOT FALSE THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the status still offers a correction after one';
  END IF;

  -- After 7 days; while Dating is active.
  IF pg_temp.correct(v_late, '1991-06-02') IS DISTINCT FROM 'ERROR: birth_date:correction_closed'
     OR (pg_temp.dob_status(v_late) ->> 'can_correct')::boolean IS NOT FALSE THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a correction 8 days after the declaration was allowed';
  END IF;
  INSERT INTO public.dating_enrollments (user_id, state) VALUES (v_dating, 'active');
  IF pg_temp.correct(v_dating, '1991-06-02') IS DISTINCT FROM 'ERROR: birth_date:correction_closed' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a correction while Dating is active was allowed';
  END IF;
  UPDATE public.dating_enrollments SET state = 'paused' WHERE user_id = v_dating;
  IF (pg_temp.as_json(pg_temp.correct(v_dating, '1991-06-02')) ->> 'status') IS DISTINCT FROM 'ok' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a paused Dating enrollment closed the correction';
  END IF;

  -- Under 18: an age hold that commits, and no raise.
  v_got := pg_temp.correct(v_minor, v_kid);
  IF pg_temp.as_json(v_got) IS DISTINCT FROM '{"status": "not_eligible"}'::jsonb THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an under-18 correction answered %', v_got;
  END IF;
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.account_holds WHERE user_id = v_minor AND kind = 'age_review'
                   AND resolved_at IS NULL AND reason_ref = 'self_corrected_birth_date')
     OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.account_birthdates WHERE user_id = v_minor AND birth_date = v_kid::date) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an under-18 correction did not keep the date and an age hold';
  END IF;
  -- The hook now refuses the account (W6a).
  IF pg_temp.hook(pg_temp.event(v_minor, 'token_refresh')) #>> '{error,message}' IS DISTINCT FROM 'account_review:age' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an account corrected to under 18 still gets tokens';
  END IF;

  -- Rubbish and no date.
  IF pg_temp.correct(v_none, '1990-01-01') IS DISTINCT FROM 'ERROR: birth_date:not_set'
     OR pg_temp.correct(v_late, ((now() AT TIME ZONE 'utc')::date + 2)::text) IS DISTINCT FROM 'ERROR: birth_date:invalid'
     OR pg_temp.correct(v_late, '1899-12-31') IS DISTINCT FROM 'ERROR: birth_date:invalid'
     OR pg_temp.correct(NULL, '1990-01-01') IS DISTINCT FROM 'ERROR: birth_date:not_signed_in' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a bad correction was not refused by its message';
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.dob_status(v_none) IS DISTINCT FROM '{"age": null, "can_correct": false, "correct_until": null}'::jsonb THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the status without a date is %', pg_temp.dob_status(v_none);
  END IF;
END $$;
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write migration 12 and its twin**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261103110000_birth_date_correction.sql` with exactly:

```sql
-- Spec 1 W6b, migration 12 (spec §4.7 #12): birth_date_correction.
--   * private.birth_date_correction_open(p_user): the one rule for "Fix a
--     mistake" (§3.15, §4.3 W6b): a declared date of birth, never
--     self-corrected, declared under 7 days ago, and no Dating enrollment
--     in 'active'. Spec 2 re-creates it from its live body if Dating's
--     state moves elsewhere (plan P-21).
--   * correct_my_birth_date(p_birth_date) -> jsonb (authenticated): once;
--     outside the rule birth_date:correction_closed. The same date again
--     changes and spends nothing. Under 18 -> an age_review hold and
--     {status:'not_eligible'} WITHOUT raising, so the hold and the date
--     commit (set_my_birth_date's pattern). The date is never returned.
--   * get_my_birth_date_status() -> jsonb (authenticated): {age,
--     can_correct, correct_until} for Settings -> Date of birth; never the
--     date itself (plan P-22).
-- Grants in 20261103110010_birth_date_correction_grants.sql.
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION private.birth_date_correction_open(p_user uuid)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.account_birthdates AS b
                  WHERE b.user_id = p_user
                    AND b.self_corrections = 0
                    AND b.declared_at > now() - interval '7 days')
     AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.dating_enrollments AS e
                      WHERE e.user_id = p_user AND e.state = 'active');
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.correct_my_birth_date(p_birth_date date)
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_uid constant uuid := auth.uid();
  v_stored date;
  v_age integer;
BEGIN
  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'birth_date:not_signed_in';
  END IF;
  IF p_birth_date IS NULL OR p_birth_date < DATE '1900-01-01'
     OR p_birth_date > (now() AT TIME ZONE 'utc')::date THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'birth_date:invalid';
  END IF;
  -- The users row first, then the date (the order the path writer and the
  -- accept use), so a correction and a Dating activation serialise.
  PERFORM 1 FROM public.users AS u WHERE u.id = v_uid FOR UPDATE;
  SELECT b.birth_date INTO v_stored
    FROM public.account_birthdates AS b WHERE b.user_id = v_uid FOR UPDATE;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'birth_date:not_set';
  END IF;
  IF v_stored = p_birth_date THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'ok', 'age', private.age_on_utc_today(v_stored), 'changed', false);
  END IF;
  IF NOT private.birth_date_correction_open(v_uid) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'birth_date:correction_closed';
  END IF;

  UPDATE public.account_birthdates AS b
     SET birth_date = p_birth_date,
         self_corrections = b.self_corrections + 1,
         corrected_at = now()
   WHERE b.user_id = v_uid;

  v_age := private.age_on_utc_today(p_birth_date);
  IF v_age < 18 THEN
    PERFORM public.place_account_hold(v_uid, 'age_review', 'self_corrected_birth_date');
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'not_eligible');
  END IF;
  RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'ok', 'age', v_age, 'changed', true);
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_my_birth_date_status()
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT coalesce(
    (SELECT jsonb_build_object(
              'age', private.age_on_utc_today(b.birth_date),
              'can_correct', private.birth_date_correction_open(b.user_id),
              'correct_until', CASE WHEN private.birth_date_correction_open(b.user_id)
                                    THEN b.declared_at + interval '7 days' END)
       FROM public.account_birthdates AS b
      WHERE b.user_id = auth.uid()),
    jsonb_build_object('age', NULL, 'can_correct', false, 'correct_until', NULL));
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.birth_date_correction_open(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.correct_my_birth_date(date) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.get_my_birth_date_status() FROM PUBLIC, anon;

COMMIT;
```

Create `supabase/migrations/20261103110010_birth_date_correction_grants.sql` with exactly:

```sql
-- Spec 1 W6b: grants for 20261103110000_birth_date_correction.sql. The two
-- RPCs are the signed-in app's; the rule is server-only.
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION private.birth_date_correction_open(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.correct_my_birth_date(date) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.get_my_birth_date_status() FROM PUBLIC, anon;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.correct_my_birth_date(date) TO authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.get_my_birth_date_status() TO authenticated;
COMMIT;
```

```bash
d = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[2])
muts = {
  'seven_days': ("AND b.declared_at > now() - interval '7 days')", "AND b.declared_at > now() - interval '70 days')"),
  'twice': ("AND b.self_corrections = 0\n", "\n"),
  'dating_ignored': ("WHERE e.user_id = p_user AND e.state = 'active');", "WHERE e.user_id = p_user AND e.state = 'no_such_state');"),
  'minor_raises': ("    RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'not_eligible');", "    RAISE EXCEPTION 'birth_date:not_eligible';"),
  'same_date_spends': ("  IF v_stored = p_birth_date THEN\n", "  IF false THEN\n"),
}
for name, (a, b) in muts.items():
    assert m.count(a) == 1, name
    (d / f"mut_{name}.sql").write_text(m.replace(a, b))
(d / 'mut_anon_grant.sql').write_text("GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.correct_my_birth_date(date) TO anon;\n")
print('mutants written')
EOF
for m in seven_days twice dating_ignored minor_raises same_date_spends anon_grant; do
  psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f $D/mut_$m.sql > /dev/null 2>&1
  echo "$m: $(psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f $C 2>&1 | grep ERROR | sed -E 's/^psql:[^ ]* //' | cut -c1-130)"
  psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f $M -f $G > /dev/null 2>&1
done
psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f $C 2>&1 | tail -1
rm $D/mut_*.sql
```

Create `test/core/platform/age_signals_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W6b (§3.3 A2, §5.1): the platform age signal through its channel,
// with fakes. Only a status comes back; a failure or a slow answer is
// unavailable; nothing is asked off iOS and Android.
import 'package:attune/core/platform/age_signals.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

void main() {
  TestWidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
  final messenger =
      TestDefaultBinaryMessengerBinding.instance.defaultBinaryMessenger;
  final calls = <MethodCall>[];

  void answer(Future<Object?> Function(MethodCall call) handler) {
    messenger.setMockMethodCallHandler(AgeSignals.channel, (call) {
      calls.add(call);
      return handler(call);
    });
  }

  setUp(calls.clear);
  tearDown(() => messenger.setMockMethodCallHandler(AgeSignals.channel, null));

  test('each status the native side sends is read', () async {
    for (final status in AgeSignalStatus.values) {
      answer((_) async => {'status': status.name});
      final signal = await AgeSignals.read(const ['USA'], platform: TargetPlatform.android);
      expect(signal.status, status);
      expect(signal.source, 'play_age_signals');
    }
  });

  test('iOS asks with the storefronts; its source is Declared Age Range', () async {
    answer((_) async => {'status': 'adult'});
    final signal = await AgeSignals.read(const ['USA', 'BRA'], platform: TargetPlatform.iOS);
    expect(signal.source, 'apple_declared_age_range');
    expect(calls.single.method, 'read');
    expect(calls.single.arguments, {'storefronts': ['USA', 'BRA']});
  });

  test('a minor or supervised answer stops sign-up; nothing else does', () {
    bool stops(AgeSignalStatus s) => AgeSignal('x', s).stopsSignUp;
    expect(stops(AgeSignalStatus.minor), isTrue);
    expect(stops(AgeSignalStatus.supervised), isTrue);
    expect(stops(AgeSignalStatus.adult), isFalse);
    expect(stops(AgeSignalStatus.unknown), isFalse);
    expect(stops(AgeSignalStatus.unavailable), isFalse);
  });

  test('rubbish is unknown; an error, a missing channel or a slow answer is unavailable', () async {
    answer((_) async => {'status': 'teen'});
    expect((await AgeSignals.read(const [], platform: TargetPlatform.android)).status, AgeSignalStatus.unknown);
    answer((_) async => 'adult');
    expect((await AgeSignals.read(const [], platform: TargetPlatform.android)).status, AgeSignalStatus.unknown);
    answer((_) async => throw PlatformException(code: 'boom'));
    expect((await AgeSignals.read(const [], platform: TargetPlatform.android)).status, AgeSignalStatus.unavailable);
    messenger.setMockMethodCallHandler(AgeSignals.channel, null);
    expect((await AgeSignals.read(const [], platform: TargetPlatform.android)).status, AgeSignalStatus.unavailable);
  });

  test('another platform is never asked', () async {
    answer((_) async => {'status': 'minor'});
    final signal = await AgeSignals.read(const ['USA'], platform: TargetPlatform.macOS);
    expect(signal.status, AgeSignalStatus.unavailable);
    expect(calls, isEmpty);
  });
}
```

Create `test/features/auth/sign_up/w6/sign_up_age_signal_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W6b (§3.3 A2, platform_age_signals): A2 reads the platform's age
// signal once. Minor or supervised: X1 without asking for the date; an
// adult date with a minor signal: X1 (the stricter wins); unknown or
// unavailable: the date decides; off: never asked. Nothing is sent.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/core/platform/age_signals.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/platform/sim_country.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/account_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/age_gate_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/sign_up_draft_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/sign_up_gateways.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/sms_code_listener.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/domain/sign_up_draft.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/screens/sign_up_flow.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

import '../../../../helpers/fake_account_gateway.dart';
import '../../../../helpers/fake_sign_up.dart';
import '../../../../helpers/flow_harness.dart';

final _now = DateTime.utc(2026, 11, 3, 12);

class _Rig {
  _Rig({this.on = true, Future<AgeSignal> Function()? answer})
    : _answer = answer ?? (() async => const AgeSignal('play_age_signals', AgeSignalStatus.unknown));

  final bool on;
  final Future<AgeSignal> Function() _answer;
  final sender = FakeCodeSender();
  final gateway = FakeAccountGateway();
  final asked = <List<String>>[];

  List<Override> get overrides => [
    codeSenderProvider.overrideWithValue(sender),
    signUpAuthProvider.overrideWithValue(FakeSignUpAuth()),
    accountServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(AccountService(gateway)),
    signUpClockProvider.overrideWithValue(() => _now),
    deviceCountryProvider.overrideWith((ref) async => (sim: 'GH', region: null)),
    smsCodeListenerProvider.overrideWithValue(FakeSmsCodeListener()),
    ageSignalSettingsProvider.overrideWithValue((on: on, storefronts: const ['USA'])),
    ageSignalReaderProvider.overrideWithValue((storefronts) {
      asked.add(storefronts);
      return _answer();
    }),
  ];
}

Future<FlowHarness> _toA2(WidgetTester tester, _Rig rig) async {
  final h = await pumpFlowScreen(
    tester,
    () => const SignUpFlow(entry: SignUpEntry.newAccount),
    overrides: rig.overrides,
  );
  await tester.enterText(find.widgetWithText(TextField, 'Your name'), 'Esi');
  await tester.pump();
  await tester.tap(find.text('Continue'));
  await tester.pumpAndSettle();
  return h;
}

Future<void> _typeDate(WidgetTester tester, String year) async {
  await tester.enterText(find.widgetWithText(TextField, 'Day'), '27');
  await tester.enterText(find.widgetWithText(TextField, 'Month'), '3');
  await tester.enterText(find.widgetWithText(TextField, 'Year'), year);
  await tester.pump();
  await tester.tap(find.text('Continue'));
  await tester.pumpAndSettle();
}

void _expectStopped(FlowHarness h, _Rig rig) {
  expect(h.location, '/age-stop');
  expect(h.routing.underAgeBlocked, isTrue);
  expect(AgeGateStore.readBlocked(h.prefs, DateTime.now()), isTrue);
  expect(SignUpDraftStore.readFrom(h.prefs, _now), isNull);
  expect(rig.sender.sent, isEmpty);
  expect(rig.gateway.calls, isEmpty);
}

void main() {
  for (final status in [AgeSignalStatus.minor, AgeSignalStatus.supervised]) {
    testWidgets('a $status signal at A2: X1 before any date is typed', (tester) async {
      final rig = _Rig(answer: () async => AgeSignal('play_age_signals', status));
      final h = await _toA2(tester, rig);
      expect(rig.asked, [const ['USA']]);
      _expectStopped(h, rig);
    });
  }

  testWidgets('an adult date with a minor signal still out: X1 (the stricter wins)', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final slow = Completer<AgeSignal>();
    final rig = _Rig(answer: () => slow.future);
    final h = await _toA2(tester, rig);
    expect(find.text("What's your date of birth?"), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.enterText(find.widgetWithText(TextField, 'Day'), '27');
    await tester.enterText(find.widgetWithText(TextField, 'Month'), '3');
    await tester.enterText(find.widgetWithText(TextField, 'Year'), '1996');
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Continue'));
    await tester.pump();
    slow.complete(const AgeSignal('apple_declared_age_range', AgeSignalStatus.minor));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    _expectStopped(h, rig);
  });

  testWidgets('unknown or unavailable: the date decides; asked once', (tester) async {
    final rig = _Rig(answer: () async => const AgeSignal('play_age_signals', AgeSignalStatus.unavailable));
    await _toA2(tester, rig);
    await _typeDate(tester, '1996');
    expect(find.text("What's your phone number?"), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.byTooltip('Back'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text("What's your date of birth?"), findsOneWidget);
    expect(rig.asked, hasLength(1), reason: 'read once per sign-up, never again');
  });

  testWidgets('an adult signal never lets an under-18 date through', (tester) async {
    final rig = _Rig(answer: () async => const AgeSignal('play_age_signals', AgeSignalStatus.adult));
    final h = await _toA2(tester, rig);
    await _typeDate(tester, '2012');
    _expectStopped(h, rig);
  });

  testWidgets('platform_age_signals off: never asked', (tester) async {
    final rig = _Rig(on: false, answer: () async => const AgeSignal('x', AgeSignalStatus.minor));
    await _toA2(tester, rig);
    await _typeDate(tester, '1996');
    expect(rig.asked, isEmpty);
    expect(find.text("What's your phone number?"), findsOneWidget);
  });
}
```

Create `lib/core/platform/age_signals.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/core/flags/public_app_config.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

/// What the platform says about the person's age (Spec 1 §3.3 A2, §5.1,
/// W6b). Never stored, never sent: A2 reads it once and forgets it.
enum AgeSignalStatus { adult, minor, supervised, unknown, unavailable }

@immutable
class AgeSignal {
  const AgeSignal(this.source, this.status);

  static const none = AgeSignal('none', AgeSignalStatus.unavailable);

  /// `apple_declared_age_range`, `play_age_signals` or `none`.
  final String source;
  final AgeSignalStatus status;

  /// A minor or supervised result opens X1 (the stricter of the signal and
  /// the typed date wins); unknown and unavailable leave it to the date.
  bool get stopsSignUp =>
      status == AgeSignalStatus.minor || status == AgeSignalStatus.supervised;

  static AgeSignalStatus statusFrom(Object? answer) {
    final value = answer is Map ? answer['status'] : null;
    for (final status in AgeSignalStatus.values) {
      if (status.name == value) return status;
    }
    return AgeSignalStatus.unknown;
  }
}

/// iOS: Apple's Declared Age Range (iOS 26; weak-linked, `unavailable`
/// below it and outside [storefronts]), through AppDelegate's
/// `attune/age_signals` channel. Android: Play Age Signals through
/// MainActivity's. Anything else, or any failure: `unavailable`.
class AgeSignals {
  const AgeSignals._();

  static const channel = MethodChannel('attune/age_signals');

  /// Apple's sheet waits for the person; Play answers on its own.
  static const iosWait = Duration(seconds: 60);
  static const androidWait = Duration(seconds: 10);

  static Future<AgeSignal> read(
    List<String> storefronts, {
    TargetPlatform? platform,
  }) async {
    final os = platform ?? defaultTargetPlatform;
    final source = switch (os) {
      TargetPlatform.iOS => 'apple_declared_age_range',
      TargetPlatform.android => 'play_age_signals',
      _ => null,
    };
    if (source == null) return AgeSignal.none;
    try {
      final answer = await channel
          .invokeMethod<Object?>('read', {'storefronts': storefronts})
          .timeout(os == TargetPlatform.iOS ? iosWait : androidWait);
      return AgeSignal(source, AgeSignal.statusFrom(answer));
    } catch (error) {
      debugPrint('[age-signals] unavailable: ${error.runtimeType}');
      return AgeSignal(source, AgeSignalStatus.unavailable);
    }
  }
}

/// platform_age_signals (effective) and the App Store storefronts, from the
/// anon config: live, else the cached copy; off until either is known.
final ageSignalSettingsProvider =
    Provider<({bool on, List<String> storefronts})>((ref) {
      final config =
          ref.watch(
            publicAppConfigProvider.select((config) => config.valueOrNull),
          ) ??
          ref.watch(cachedPublicAppConfigProvider);
      return (
        on: config?.platformAgeSignals ?? false,
        storefronts: config?.ageSignalStorefronts ?? const [],
      );
    });

/// The read itself, replaceable in tests.
final ageSignalReaderProvider =
    Provider<Future<AgeSignal> Function(List<String> storefronts)>(
      (ref) => AgeSignals.read,
    );
```

```dart
    step == SignUpStepId.code ? _listenForCode() : _stopListening();
```

```dart
  /// W6b: asks the platform once, when A2 first shows. A minor or
  /// supervised answer while A2 is still up stops the flow at X1 before
  /// any date is typed; otherwise A2's Continue weighs it (the stricter
  /// wins). Off, unknown or unavailable: the date decides.
  void _readAgeSignalOnce() {
    if (_ageSignal != null) return;
    final settings = ref.read(ageSignalSettingsProvider);
    if (!settings.on) return;
    final read = ref.read(ageSignalReaderProvider);
    final signal = Future<AgeSignal>.sync(
      () => read(settings.storefronts),
    ).catchError((Object _) => AgeSignal.none);
    _ageSignal = signal;
    unawaited(
      signal.then((answer) async {
        if (!mounted || !answer.stopsSignUp) return;
        if (_step != SignUpStepId.birthDate || _busy) return;
        _busy = true;
        await _c.stopForAgeSignal();
        if (!mounted) return;
        ref.read(routingNotifierProvider).setUnderAgeBlocked(true);
        context.go(RouteNames.ageStop);
      }),
    );
  }

  /// The signal for A2's Continue: null when none was asked for; a signal
  /// still out after [_signalWait] counts as unavailable.
  Future<AgeSignal?> _settledAgeSignal() async {
    final signal = _ageSignal;
    if (signal == null) return null;
    return signal.timeout(_signalWait, onTimeout: () => AgeSignal.none);
  }
```

`android/app/src/main/kotlin/com/barsopus/attune/MainActivity.kt`, edit 3 of 3. Replace exactly:

```kotlin
    private fun startScreenshotDetection() {
```

with:

```kotlin
    // 18 and over is adult; a band that ends under 18 is supervised when a
    // parent set it (TIER_B), otherwise minor; not shared is unknown; any
    // failure (no Play Store, an old one, not installed from Play) is
    // unavailable, and the date of birth decides.
    private fun readAgeSignal(result: MethodChannel.Result) {
        try {
            AgeSignalsManagerFactory.create(applicationContext)
                .checkAgeSignals(AgeSignalsRequest.builder().build())
                .addOnSuccessListener { signal ->
                    val lower = signal.ageLower()
                    val upper = signal.ageUpper()
                    val status = when {
                        lower != null && lower >= 18 -> "adult"
                        upper != null && upper < 18 ->
                            if (signal.ageRangeSource() == AgeRangeSource.TIER_B) "supervised" else "minor"
                        else -> "unknown"
                    }
                    result.success(mapOf("status" to status))
                }
                .addOnFailureListener { result.success(mapOf("status" to "unavailable")) }
        } catch (_: Throwable) {
            result.success(mapOf("status" to "unavailable"))
        }
    }

    private fun startScreenshotDetection() {
```

```swift
    // Spec 1 W6b (§3.3 A2): Apple's Declared Age Range, once, at A2, only
    // in the listed App Store storefronts. Only a status goes back; nothing
    // is stored or sent. DeclaredAgeRange is weak-linked (OTHER_LDFLAGS
    // -weak_framework) and used only on iOS 26 and later.
    let ageChannel = FlutterMethodChannel(
      name: "attune/age_signals",
      binaryMessenger: controller.binaryMessenger
    )
    ageSignalsChannel = ageChannel
    ageChannel.setMethodCallHandler { [weak self] call, result in
      guard call.method == "read" else {
        result(FlutterMethodNotImplemented)
        return
      }
      let storefronts = (call.arguments as? [String: Any])?["storefronts"] as? [String] ?? []
      Task { @MainActor in
        result(await AgeSignalReader.read(
          storefronts: storefronts,
          controller: self?.window?.rootViewController
```

```swift
  private func hideSwitcherCover() {
```

```swift
/// Spec 1 W6b: Declared Age Range's answer as A2 reads it. 18 and over is
/// adult; a range under 18 is supervised when a guardian declared it,
/// otherwise minor; declined sharing is unknown; below iOS 26, outside the
/// storefronts, or on any error: unavailable (the date of birth decides).
enum AgeSignalReader {
  @MainActor
  static func read(storefronts: [String], controller: UIViewController?) async -> [String: String] {
    guard #available(iOS 26.0, *) else { return ["status": "unavailable"] }
    guard let country = await Storefront.current?.countryCode, storefronts.contains(country),
          let controller else {
      return ["status": "unavailable"]
    }
    do {
      let response = try await AgeRangeService.shared.requestAgeRange(ageGates: 18, in: controller)
      switch response {
      case .declinedSharing:
        return ["status": "unknown"]
      case .sharing(let range):
        if let lower = range.lowerBound, lower >= 18 { return ["status": "adult"] }
        if let upper = range.upperBound, upper < 18 {
          return ["status": range.ageRangeDeclaration == .guardianDeclared ? "supervised" : "minor"]
        }
        return ["status": "unknown"]
      @unknown default:
        return ["status": "unknown"]
      }
    } catch {
      return ["status": "unavailable"]
    }
  }
}
```

Then weak-link `DeclaredAgeRange` in the Runner target's three configurations (Debug, Release, Profile) by script. Create `.superpowers/sdd/2026-11-03-spec1-w6/pbx_weak.py` with exactly:

```python
"""Adds -weak_framework DeclaredAgeRange to the Runner target's three build
configurations (Debug, Release, Profile), once each. Usage:
python3 -I pbx_weak.py ios/Runner.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj"""
import pathlib, sys
p = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1])
s = p.read_text()
anchor = "\t\t\t\tINFOPLIST_FILE = Runner/Info.plist;\n"
add = ("\t\t\t\tOTHER_LDFLAGS = (\n"
       "\t\t\t\t\t\"$(inherited)\",\n"
       "\t\t\t\t\t\"-weak_framework\",\n"
       "\t\t\t\t\tDeclaredAgeRange,\n"
       "\t\t\t\t);\n")
if "DeclaredAgeRange" in s:
    raise SystemExit("already weak-linked")
assert s.count(anchor) == 3, s.count(anchor)
p.write_text(s.replace(anchor, anchor + add))
print("weak-linked DeclaredAgeRange in 3 Runner configurations")
```

```dart
    'correct_until': '2026-11-08T10:00:00Z',
  };
  Object? correction = {'status': 'ok', 'age': 31, 'changed': true};
  final calls = <(String, Map<String, dynamic>?)>[];
  int signOuts = 0;

  Future<Object?> rpc(String fn, Map<String, dynamic>? params) async {
    calls.add((fn, params));
    if (fn == 'get_my_birth_date_status') return status;
    final answer = correction;
    if (answer is Exception) throw answer;
    return answer;
  }

  List<Override> get overrides => [
    birthDateSettingsServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(BirthDateSettingsService(rpc)),
    catchUpSignOutProvider.overrideWithValue(() async => signOuts++),
  ];
}

Future<FlowHarness> _pump(WidgetTester tester, _Server server) => pumpFlowScreen(
  tester,
  () => const BirthDateSettingsScreen(),
  overrides: server.overrides,
);

Future<void> _fix(WidgetTester tester, String year) async {
  await tester.tap(find.text('Fix a mistake'));
  await tester.pumpAndSettle();
  expect(find.text('Fix your date of birth'), findsOneWidget);
  await tester.enterText(find.bySemanticsLabel('Day of birth'), '2');
  await tester.enterText(find.bySemanticsLabel('Month of birth'), '6');
  await tester.enterText(find.bySemanticsLabel('Year of birth'), year);
  await tester.pump();
  await tester.tap(find.text('Continue'));
  await tester.pumpAndSettle();
}

void main() {
  testWidgets('the age, never the date, and the one fix while it is open', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server();
    await _pump(tester, server);
    expect(find.text('Your age: 30'), findsOneWidget);
    final until = MyDateFormat.toDate(DateTime.parse('2026-11-08T10:00:00Z').toLocal());
    expect(find.text("If it's wrong, you can fix it once, until $until."), findsOneWidget);
    server.status = {'age': 31, 'can_correct': false, 'correct_until': null};
    await _fix(tester, '1995');
    expect(server.calls.where((c) => c.$1 == 'correct_my_birth_date').single.$2, {'p_birth_date': '1995-06-02'});
    expect(find.text('Saved.'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Your age: 31'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('To change this, contact support.'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Fix a mistake'), findsNothing);
    expect(find.textContaining('1995'), findsNothing, reason: 'the date is never shown');
  });

  testWidgets('closed: contact support, no fix', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server()..status = {'age': 30, 'can_correct': false, 'correct_until': null};
    await _pump(tester, server);
    expect(find.text('To change this, contact support.'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Contact support'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Fix a mistake'), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('closed since the screen opened: back to the status, which says so', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server()
      ..correction = const PostgrestException(message: 'birth_date:correction_closed', code: 'P0001');
    await _pump(tester, server);
    server.status = {'age': 30, 'can_correct': false, 'correct_until': null};
    await _fix(tester, '1995');
    expect(find.text('To change this, contact support.'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('a correction to under 18: blocked, signed out, X1', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server()..correction = {'status': 'not_eligible'};
    final h = await _pump(tester, server);
    await _fix(tester, '2012');
    expect(AgeGateStore.readBlocked(h.prefs, DateTime.now()), isTrue);
    expect(h.routing.underAgeBlocked, isTrue);
    expect(server.signOuts, 1);
    expect(h.location, '/age-stop?from=account');
  });

  testWidgets('a failed save stays on A2 and says so', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server()..correction = Exception('offline');
    await _pump(tester, server);
    await _fix(tester, '1995');
    expect(find.text('Fix your date of birth'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text("We couldn't save that. Try again."), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('the Settings row only under flow_signin_v2', (tester) async {
    for (final on in [true, false]) {
      await pumpFlowScreen(
        tester,
        () => Consumer(
          builder: (context, ref, _) => Text(birthDateSetting(context, ref)?.title ?? 'no row'),
        ),
        overrides: [flowSignInV2Provider.overrideWithValue(on)],
      );
      expect(find.text(on ? 'Date of birth' : 'no row'), findsOneWidget);
    }
  });

  testWidgets('guidelines, and goldens open and closed', (tester) async {
    await _pump(tester, _Server());
    await expectFlowGuidelines(tester);
    await expectFlowGoldens(
      tester,
      'dob_settings_open',
      () => const BirthDateSettingsScreen(),
      overrides: _Server().overrides,
    );
    final closed = _Server()..status = {'age': 30, 'can_correct': false, 'correct_until': null};
    await expectFlowGoldens(
      tester,
      'dob_settings_closed',
      () => const BirthDateSettingsScreen(),
      overrides: closed.overrides,
    );
  });
}
```

Create `lib/features/settings/data/birth_date_settings_service.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/domain/sign_up_draft.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

/// Settings -> Date of birth (Spec 1 §3.15, W6b): the age, and whether the
/// one correction is still open. Never the date itself.
@immutable
class BirthDateStatus {
  const BirthDateStatus({
    required this.age,
    required this.canCorrect,
    this.correctUntil,
  });

  /// Null when the account has no date of birth yet.
  final int? age;
  final bool canCorrect;
  final DateTime? correctUntil;

  factory BirthDateStatus.fromJson(Object? json) {
    final map = json is Map ? json : const {};
    final until = map['correct_until'];
    return BirthDateStatus(
      age: (map['age'] as num?)?.toInt(),
      canCorrect: map['can_correct'] == true,
      correctUntil: until is String ? DateTime.tryParse(until)?.toLocal() : null,
    );
  }
}

/// correct_my_birth_date's answer.
enum BirthDateCorrection {
  /// Saved (or the same date again: nothing changed).
  saved,

  /// Under 18: the account is on hold; the phone stops at X1.
  notEligible,

  /// `birth_date:correction_closed`: used, too late, or Dating is active.
  closed,

  /// Offline, a timeout or a refusal that can't be acted on.
  failed,
}

typedef SettingsRpc =
    Future<Object?> Function(String fn, Map<String, dynamic>? params);

class BirthDateSettingsService {
  BirthDateSettingsService(this._rpc);

  final SettingsRpc _rpc;

  static const timeout = Duration(seconds: 15);

  Future<BirthDateStatus> status() async => BirthDateStatus.fromJson(
    await _rpc('get_my_birth_date_status', null).timeout(timeout),
  );

  Future<BirthDateCorrection> correct(DateTime birthDate) async {
    try {
      final data = await _rpc('correct_my_birth_date', {
        'p_birth_date': SignUpDraft.dateOnly(birthDate),
      }).timeout(timeout);
      final status = data is Map ? data['status'] : null;
      return switch (status) {
        'not_eligible' => BirthDateCorrection.notEligible,
        'ok' => BirthDateCorrection.saved,
        _ => BirthDateCorrection.failed,
      };
    } on PostgrestException catch (error) {
      return error.message.startsWith('birth_date:correction_closed')
          ? BirthDateCorrection.closed
          : BirthDateCorrection.failed;
    } catch (error) {
      debugPrint('[dob-settings] correction failed: ${error.runtimeType}');
      return BirthDateCorrection.failed;
    }
  }
}

final birthDateSettingsServiceProvider = Provider<BirthDateSettingsService>(
  (ref) => BirthDateSettingsService(
    (fn, params) => Supabase.instance.client.rpc(fn, params: params),
  ),
);

final birthDateStatusProvider = FutureProvider.autoDispose<BirthDateStatus>(
  (ref) => ref.watch(birthDateSettingsServiceProvider).status(),
);
```

Create `lib/features/settings/presentation/screens/birth_date_settings_screen.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/app/routing/app_router.dart' show RouteNames;
import 'package:attune/core/flags/public_app_config.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/providers/routing_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/utils/constants.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/utils/date_formatter.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/utils/url_launcher_utils.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/data/age_gate_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/domain/birth_date_validator.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/steps/birth_date_step.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_buttons.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_frame.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_text_field.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/screens/onboarding_gate_v2.dart'
    show catchUpSignOutProvider;
import 'package:attune/features/settings/data/birth_date_settings_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/settings/models/settings_config.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/semantics.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

/// Settings -> Date of birth (Spec 1 §3.15, W6b), for a signed-in account
/// under flow_signin_v2 (where every account has one).
SettingsConfig? birthDateSetting(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
  if (!ref.watch(flowSignInV2Provider)) return null;
  final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
  return SettingsConfig(
    id: 'date_of_birth',
    title: loc.flow_dobSettingsTitle,
    subtitle: loc.flow_dobSettingsSubtitle,
    icon: Icons.badge_outlined,
    type: SettingsItemType.navigation,
    routeName: RouteNames.birthDateSettings,
    onTap: () => context.push(RouteNames.birthDateSettings),
    iconColor: Colors.indigo,
    order: 5,
  );
}

/// "Your age: {age}" (never the date); "Fix a mistake" once, within 7 days
/// of the declaration and only while Dating isn't active (the server
/// decides: get_my_birth_date_status); otherwise "To change this, contact
/// support." Fixing it is A2 again; under 18 is X1, as at sign-up.
class BirthDateSettingsScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const BirthDateSettingsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<BirthDateSettingsScreen> createState() =>
      _BirthDateSettingsScreenState();
}

class _BirthDateSettingsScreenState
    extends ConsumerState<BirthDateSettingsScreen> {
  bool _fixing = false;
  String? _error;
  int _errorCount = 0;

  /// A notice on the status view ("Saved.", or why it can't be fixed).
  String? _notice;

  AppLocalizations get _loc => AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

  void _back() {
    if (_fixing) {
      setState(() {
        _fixing = false;
        _error = null;
      });
      return;
    }
    if (context.canPop()) {
      context.pop();
    } else {
      context.go(RouteNames.settings);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _save(DateTime date) async {
    // Read before the call: the stop must finish even if the screen goes.
    final ageGate = ref.read(ageGateStoreProvider);
    final routing = ref.read(routingNotifierProvider);
    final signOut = ref.read(catchUpSignOutProvider);
    final router = GoRouter.of(context);
    final outcome = await ref.read(birthDateSettingsServiceProvider).correct(date);
    switch (outcome) {
      case BirthDateCorrection.notEligible:
        // Under 18: the server holds the account. Block, sign out, X1 (the
        // gate's order, rulings F-8 and B3 W2 I-3).
        await ageGate.block().timeout(const Duration(seconds: 1), onTimeout: () {});
        routing.setUnderAgeBlocked(true);
        try {
          await signOut();
        } catch (error) {
          debugPrint('[dob-settings] sign-out failed: ${error.runtimeType}');
        }
        router.go('${RouteNames.ageStop}?from=account');
      case BirthDateCorrection.saved:
        if (!mounted) return;
        ref.invalidate(birthDateStatusProvider);
        setState(() {
          _fixing = false;
          _notice = _loc.flow_dobFixSaved;
        });
        SemanticsService.announce(_loc.flow_dobFixSaved, Directionality.of(context));
      case BirthDateCorrection.closed:
        // Closed since the screen opened: the status, re-read, says
        // "To change this, contact support."
        if (!mounted) return;
        ref.invalidate(birthDateStatusProvider);
        setState(() {
          _fixing = false;
          _notice = null;
        });
      case BirthDateCorrection.failed:
        if (!mounted) return;
        setState(() {
          _error = _loc.flow_saveFailed;
          _errorCount++;
        });
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = _loc;
    if (_fixing) {
      return BirthDateStep(
        order: birthDateOrderFor(WidgetsBinding.instance.platformDispatcher.locale),
        todayUtc: _todayUtc(),
        title: loc.flow_dobFixTitle,
        errorText: _error,
        errorCount: _errorCount,
        onBack: _back,
        onSubmit: _save,
      );
    }
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final status = ref.watch(birthDateStatusProvider);
    final textStyle = theme.textTheme.bodyLarge?.copyWith(
      color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
    );
    return status.when(
      loading:
          () => FlowFrame(
            title: loc.flow_dobSettingsTitle,
            onBack: _back,
            body: Center(
              child: CircularProgressIndicator(semanticsLabel: loc.flow_accountChecking),
            ),
          ),
      error:
          (_, __) => FlowFrame(
            title: loc.flow_dobSettingsTitle,
            onBack: _back,
            body: FlowErrorLine(
              text: loc.flow_dobSettingsLoadFailed,
              style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium,
              liveRegion: true,
            ),
            actions: [
              FlowPrimaryButton(
                label: loc.flow_tryAgain,
                onPressed: () => ref.invalidate(birthDateStatusProvider),
              ),
            ],
          ),
      data: (value) {
        final age = value.age;
        final until = value.correctUntil;
        final notice = _notice;
        return FlowFrame(
          title: loc.flow_dobSettingsTitle,
          onBack: _back,
          body: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              Text(
                age == null ? loc.flow_dobSettingsNoAge : loc.flow_dobSettingsAge(age),
                style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                  color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Text(
                value.canCorrect && until != null
                    ? loc.flow_dobSettingsFixUntil(MyDateFormat.toDate(until))
                    : loc.flow_dobSettingsContact,
                style: textStyle,
              ),
              if (notice != null) ...[
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Semantics(liveRegion: true, child: Text(notice, style: textStyle)),
              ],
            ],
          ),
          actions: [
            if (value.canCorrect)
              FlowPrimaryButton(
                label: loc.flow_dobSettingsFix,
                onPressed:
                    () => setState(() {
                      _fixing = true;
                      _notice = null;
                    }),
              )
            else
              FlowPrimaryButton(
                label: loc.flow_holdContact,
                onPressed:
                    () => UrlLauncherUtils.launchEmail(
                      context: context,
                      email: AppConstants.supportEmail,
                    ),
              ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  static DateTime _todayUtc() {
    final now = DateTime.now().toUtc();
    return DateTime.utc(now.year, now.month, now.day);
  }
}
```

Create `supabase/tests/child_safety_contracts.sql` with exactly:

```sql
-- supabase/tests/child_safety_contracts.sql
-- Spec 1 W6c (§3.15, §4.2, §4.3 W6c, §6, §7.1): "Report a concern about a
-- child". A report is sent through the RPC only, 5 a person a day, read by
-- the service role only; nothing names, finds or tells the reported
-- person; the safety webhook learns a count and a time, never the words.
-- One BEGIN ... ROLLBACK.
-- Run only on attune_spec3a:
--   psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f supabase/tests/child_safety_contracts.sql
-- Fixture ids 5f6c0000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000NN; phones +15558401NN
-- (§7.1's 84xx range), set on auth.users and public.users. Every refusal
-- is checked by its message.
BEGIN;

CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.id(p_n text) RETURNS uuid LANGUAGE sql AS $$
  SELECT ('5f6c0000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000' || p_n)::uuid;
$$;

CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.person(p_n text) RETURNS uuid LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO auth.users (id, phone, phone_confirmed_at) VALUES (pg_temp.id(p_n), '15558401' || p_n, now());
  INSERT INTO public.users (id, phone, display_name, path)
  VALUES (pg_temp.id(p_n), '15558401' || p_n, 'Person ' || p_n, 'just_me');
  RETURN pg_temp.id(p_n);
END $$;

-- [p_user]'s report: NULL, or the refusal.
CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.report(p_user uuid, p_where text, p_details text) RETURNS text LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE
  v text;
BEGIN
  PERFORM set_config('request.jwt.claims', json_build_object('sub', p_user, 'role', 'authenticated')::text, true);
  PERFORM set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub', coalesce(p_user::text, ''), true);
  EXECUTE 'SET LOCAL ROLE authenticated';
  BEGIN
    PERFORM public.report_child_safety_concern(p_where, p_details);
  EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    v := SQLERRM;
  END;
  RESET ROLE;
  RETURN v;
END $$;

CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.client_error(p_role text, p_sql text) RETURNS text LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE
  v text;
BEGIN
  EXECUTE format('SET LOCAL ROLE %I', p_role);
  BEGIN
    EXECUTE p_sql;
  EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
    v := SQLSTATE;
  END;
  RESET ROLE;
  RETURN v;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- A. Objects, grants, no client read or write (spec §4.2, §7.1).
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  v_cols text;
BEGIN
  IF to_regclass('public.child_safety_reports') IS NULL
     OR to_regprocedure('public.report_child_safety_concern(text,text)') IS NULL
     OR to_regprocedure('public.child_safety_alerts_due()') IS NULL
     OR to_regprocedure('public.mark_child_safety_alerts_sent(uuid[])') IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: W6c objects are missing';
  END IF;
  SELECT string_agg(c.column_name, ',' ORDER BY c.column_name) INTO v_cols
    FROM information_schema.columns AS c
   WHERE c.table_schema = 'public' AND c.table_name = 'child_safety_reports';
  IF v_cols IS DISTINCT FROM 'alerted_at,created_at,details,id,reporter_user_id,reviewed_at,status,where_seen' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: child_safety_reports has columns beyond the spec (nothing may name the reported person): %', v_cols;
  END IF;
  IF NOT (SELECT relrowsecurity FROM pg_class WHERE oid = 'public.child_safety_reports'::regclass) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: RLS is off on child_safety_reports';
  END IF;
  IF has_table_privilege('authenticated', 'public.child_safety_reports', 'SELECT')
     OR has_table_privilege('authenticated', 'public.child_safety_reports', 'INSERT')
     OR has_table_privilege('authenticated', 'public.child_safety_reports', 'UPDATE')
     OR has_table_privilege('authenticated', 'public.child_safety_reports', 'DELETE')
     OR has_table_privilege('anon', 'public.child_safety_reports', 'SELECT')
     OR has_table_privilege('anon', 'public.child_safety_reports', 'INSERT')
     OR NOT has_table_privilege('service_role', 'public.child_safety_reports', 'SELECT')
     OR NOT has_table_privilege('service_role', 'public.child_safety_reports', 'UPDATE') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: child_safety_reports grants are wrong (no client read or write)';
  END IF;
  IF NOT has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'public.report_child_safety_concern(text,text)', 'EXECUTE')
     OR has_function_privilege('anon', 'public.report_child_safety_concern(text,text)', 'EXECUTE')
     OR has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'public.child_safety_alerts_due()', 'EXECUTE')
     OR has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'public.mark_child_safety_alerts_sent(uuid[])', 'EXECUTE')
     OR NOT has_function_privilege('service_role', 'public.child_safety_alerts_due()', 'EXECUTE')
     OR NOT has_function_privilege('service_role', 'public.mark_child_safety_alerts_sent(uuid[])', 'EXECUTE') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: W6c function grants are wrong';
  END IF;
  -- Nothing tells anyone: the RPC writes the report and nothing else.
  IF (SELECT prosrc FROM pg_proc WHERE oid = 'public.report_child_safety_concern(text,text)'::regprocedure)
       ~* '(notification|push|enqueue|net\.http|invoke_edge_function|relationships|profiles)' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: report_child_safety_concern reaches beyond the report (nothing may tell or find anyone)';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- B. Sending: through the RPC only, validated, 5 a day, no client read.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  v_ama constant uuid := pg_temp.person('01');
  v_kofi constant uuid := pg_temp.person('02');
  v_got text;
  i int;
BEGIN
  v_got := pg_temp.report(v_ama, 'chat', '  Someone in a group chat asked a 14-year-old for photos.  ');
  IF v_got IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a valid report was refused: %', v_got;
  END IF;
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.child_safety_reports
                  WHERE reporter_user_id = v_ama AND where_seen = 'chat' AND status = 'new'
                    AND details = 'Someone in a group chat asked a 14-year-old for photos.'
                    AND reviewed_at IS NULL AND alerted_at IS NULL) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the report was not stored as sent (trimmed, new)';
  END IF;
  -- Where is optional.
  IF pg_temp.report(v_kofi, NULL, 'Saw it somewhere') IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a report without a place was refused';
  END IF;

  IF pg_temp.report(v_ama, 'chat', '   ') IS DISTINCT FROM 'child_report:invalid'
     OR pg_temp.report(v_ama, 'chat', NULL) IS DISTINCT FROM 'child_report:invalid'
     OR pg_temp.report(v_ama, 'chat', repeat('x', 2001)) IS DISTINCT FROM 'child_report:invalid'
     OR pg_temp.report(v_ama, 'forum', 'words') IS DISTINCT FROM 'child_report:invalid'
     OR pg_temp.report(NULL, 'chat', 'words') IS DISTINCT FROM 'child_report:not_signed_in' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a bad report was not refused by its message';
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.report(v_ama, 'other', repeat('x', 2000)) IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: 2,000 characters were refused';
  END IF;

  -- 5 a person a day (Ama has sent 2).
  FOR i IN 3..5 LOOP
    v_got := pg_temp.report(v_ama, 'stories', 'report ' || i);
    IF v_got IS NOT NULL THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: report % of 5 was refused: %', i, v_got;
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
  IF pg_temp.report(v_ama, 'stories', 'report 6') IS DISTINCT FROM 'child_report:limit' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the 6th report in a day was not refused with child_report:limit';
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.report(v_kofi, 'dating', 'still mine to send') IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: one person''s limit stopped another';
  END IF;
  UPDATE public.child_safety_reports SET created_at = now() - interval '25 hours'
   WHERE reporter_user_id = v_ama AND details = 'report 3';
  IF pg_temp.report(v_ama, 'opinions', 'a day later') IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a report older than a day still counted';
  END IF;

  -- No client reads or writes the table, even its own reports.
  PERFORM set_config('request.jwt.claims', json_build_object('sub', v_ama, 'role', 'authenticated')::text, true);
  PERFORM set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub', v_ama::text, true);
  IF pg_temp.client_error('authenticated', 'SELECT count(*) FROM public.child_safety_reports') IS DISTINCT FROM '42501'
     OR pg_temp.client_error('authenticated', format(
          'INSERT INTO public.child_safety_reports (reporter_user_id, details) VALUES (%L, ''x'')', v_ama)) IS DISTINCT FROM '42501'
     OR pg_temp.client_error('anon', 'SELECT count(*) FROM public.child_safety_reports') IS DISTINCT FROM '42501'
     OR pg_temp.client_error('anon', 'SELECT public.report_child_safety_concern(''chat'', ''x'')') IS DISTINCT FROM '42501' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a client reached child_safety_reports outside the RPC';
  END IF;

  -- The reporter's account goes; the report stays, unattributed.
  DELETE FROM public.users WHERE id = v_kofi;
  DELETE FROM auth.users WHERE id = v_kofi;
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.child_safety_reports
                  WHERE details = 'still mine to send' AND reporter_user_id IS NULL) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a report went with its reporter''s account';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- C. The safety webhook learns a count and a time, never the words
--    (plan P-30).
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  v jsonb;
  v_ids uuid[];
BEGIN
  EXECUTE 'SET LOCAL ROLE service_role';
  v := public.child_safety_alerts_due();
  RESET ROLE;
  IF (SELECT array_agg(k ORDER BY k) FROM jsonb_object_keys(v) AS k) <> ARRAY['count', 'first_at', 'ids', 'last_at']
     OR v::text ~ '(photos|report|mine|xxxx)'
     OR NOT pg_input_is_valid(v ->> 'first_at', 'timestamptz')
     OR NOT pg_input_is_valid(v ->> 'last_at', 'timestamptz') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an alert carries more than ids, a count and times: %', v;
  END IF;
  IF (v ->> 'count')::int < 7 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: new reports were not due for the webhook: %', v;
  END IF;
  SELECT array_agg(x::uuid) INTO v_ids FROM jsonb_array_elements_text(v -> 'ids') AS x;
  EXECUTE 'SET LOCAL ROLE service_role';
  PERFORM public.mark_child_safety_alerts_sent(v_ids);
  v := public.child_safety_alerts_due();
  RESET ROLE;
  IF (v ->> 'count')::int <> 0 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: alerted reports were due again';
  END IF;
END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN RAISE NOTICE 'child_safety_contracts: all held'; END $$;

ROLLBACK;
```

```ts
});
```
