- [ ] **Step 3: Write migration 13, its twin, and the alert source**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261103120000_child_safety_reports.sql` with exactly:

```sql
-- Spec 1 W6c, migration 13 (spec §4.7 #13): child_safety_reports.
--   * public.child_safety_reports (spec §4.2): what a person saw and where,
--     written only by report_child_safety_concern; read only by the named
--     child-safety contact (service role). There is no column for the
--     reported person: nothing can tell them (§3.15). alerted_at (plan
--     P-30) marks a report the safety webhook has been told about (a count
--     and a time, never the words).
--   * report_child_safety_concern(p_where, p_details) (authenticated): 5 a
--     person a day (child_report:limit), the details trimmed and 1-2,000
--     characters (child_report:invalid), where one of chat, stories,
--     opinions, dating, other or none. Sends nothing to anyone.
--   * child_safety_alerts_due() / mark_child_safety_alerts_sent(p_ids)
--     (service role): new reports for identity-health-alert's webhook post
--     (plan P-30).
-- No retention job: how long reports are kept after closing is privacy
-- counsel's call (spec §4.8, §10; plan P-31).
-- Grants in 20261103120010_child_safety_reports_grants.sql.
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.child_safety_reports (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  reporter_user_id uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  where_seen text
    CONSTRAINT child_safety_reports_where_seen_check
    CHECK (where_seen IN ('chat', 'stories', 'opinions', 'dating', 'other')),
  details text NOT NULL
    CONSTRAINT child_safety_reports_details_check CHECK (char_length(details) BETWEEN 1 AND 2000),
  status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'new'
    CONSTRAINT child_safety_reports_status_check CHECK (status IN ('new', 'reviewing', 'actioned', 'closed')),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  reviewed_at timestamptz,
  alerted_at timestamptz
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS child_safety_reports_reporter_idx
  ON public.child_safety_reports (reporter_user_id, created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS child_safety_reports_unalerted_idx
  ON public.child_safety_reports (created_at) WHERE alerted_at IS NULL;
ALTER TABLE public.child_safety_reports ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.report_child_safety_concern(p_where text, p_details text)
RETURNS void
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_uid constant uuid := auth.uid();
  v_details constant text := btrim(coalesce(p_details, ''));
BEGIN
  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'child_report:not_signed_in';
  END IF;
  IF char_length(v_details) NOT BETWEEN 1 AND 2000
     OR (p_where IS NOT NULL AND p_where NOT IN ('chat', 'stories', 'opinions', 'dating', 'other')) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'child_report:invalid';
  END IF;
  -- One count at a time per person, so two quick sends can't both be the 5th.
  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('child-report:' || v_uid::text, 0));
  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.child_safety_reports AS r
       WHERE r.reporter_user_id = v_uid AND r.created_at > now() - interval '1 day') >= 5 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'child_report:limit';
  END IF;
  INSERT INTO public.child_safety_reports (reporter_user_id, where_seen, details)
  VALUES (v_uid, p_where, v_details);
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.child_safety_alerts_due()
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT jsonb_build_object(
    'ids', coalesce(jsonb_agg(r.id ORDER BY r.created_at, r.id), '[]'::jsonb),
    'count', count(*),
    'first_at', min(r.created_at),
    'last_at', max(r.created_at))
    FROM (SELECT x.id, x.created_at FROM public.child_safety_reports AS x
           WHERE x.alerted_at IS NULL ORDER BY x.created_at, x.id LIMIT 500) AS r;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.mark_child_safety_alerts_sent(p_ids uuid[])
RETURNS void
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  UPDATE public.child_safety_reports AS r
     SET alerted_at = now()
   WHERE r.id = ANY (coalesce(p_ids, ARRAY[]::uuid[])) AND r.alerted_at IS NULL;
$$;

REVOKE ALL ON public.child_safety_reports FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.report_child_safety_concern(text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.child_safety_alerts_due() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.mark_child_safety_alerts_sent(uuid[]) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;

COMMIT;
```

Create `supabase/migrations/20261103120010_child_safety_reports_grants.sql` with exactly:

```sql
-- Spec 1 W6c: grants for 20261103120000_child_safety_reports.sql. A report
-- is sent through the RPC only and read by the service role only.
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';
REVOKE ALL ON public.child_safety_reports FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.child_safety_reports TO service_role;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.report_child_safety_concern(text, text) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.child_safety_alerts_due() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.mark_child_safety_alerts_sent(uuid[]) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.report_child_safety_concern(text, text) TO authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.child_safety_alerts_due() TO service_role;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.mark_child_safety_alerts_sent(uuid[]) TO service_role;
COMMIT;
```

Then the handler:

`supabase/functions/identity-health-alert/handler.ts`, edit 1 of 1. Replace exactly:

```ts
export const ALERT_SOURCES: AlertSource[] = [HOOK_FAIL_OPENS];
```

with:

```ts
/// Spec 1 W6c (plan P-30): a new "Report a concern about a child" reaches
/// a person within 15 minutes. The count and the time only: the words stay
/// in child_safety_reports, read by the named child-safety contact.
export const CHILD_SAFETY_REPORTS: AlertSource = {
  name: "child_safety_report",
  due: "child_safety_alerts_due",
  mark: "mark_child_safety_alerts_sent",
  line: (count, firstAt) =>
    `Attune: ${count} new report(s) of a concern about a child, the first at ${firstAt}. ` +
    "A person must read them today (child_safety_reports).",
};

export const ALERT_SOURCES: AlertSource[] = [HOOK_FAIL_OPENS, CHILD_SAFETY_REPORTS];
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Mutation checks (each must turn the contract red with the message quoted)**

```bash
M=supabase/migrations/20261103120000_child_safety_reports.sql
G=supabase/migrations/20261103120010_child_safety_reports_grants.sql
C=supabase/tests/child_safety_contracts.sql
D=.superpowers/sdd/2026-11-03-spec1-w6
python3 -I - "$M" "$D" <<'EOF'
import pathlib, sys
m = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1]).read_text()
d = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[2])
muts = {
  'six_a_day': ("interval '1 day') >= 5 THEN", "interval '1 day') >= 6 THEN"),
  'untrimmed': ("v_details constant text := btrim(coalesce(p_details, ''));", "v_details constant text := coalesce(p_details, '');"),
  'tells_someone': ("  INSERT INTO public.child_safety_reports (reporter_user_id, where_seen, details)\n  VALUES (v_uid, p_where, v_details);",
                    "  PERFORM 1 FROM public.relationships LIMIT 1; -- enqueue a notification\n  INSERT INTO public.child_safety_reports (reporter_user_id, where_seen, details)\n  VALUES (v_uid, p_where, v_details);"),
}
for name, (a, b) in muts.items():
    assert m.count(a) == 1, name
    (d / f"mut_{name}.sql").write_text(m.replace(a, b))
(d / 'mut_client_read.sql').write_text("GRANT SELECT ON public.child_safety_reports TO authenticated;\n")
print('mutants written')
EOF
for m in six_a_day untrimmed tells_someone client_read; do
  psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f $D/mut_$m.sql > /dev/null 2>&1
  echo "$m: $(psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f $C 2>&1 | grep ERROR | sed -E 's/^psql:[^ ]* //' | cut -c1-130)"
  psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f $M -f $G > /dev/null 2>&1
done
# A column naming the reported person, and a cascade that deletes a report with its reporter (both rolled back with the contract):
echo "reported_column: $(psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -c 'BEGIN' -c 'ALTER TABLE public.child_safety_reports ADD COLUMN reported_user_id uuid' -f $C 2>&1 | grep ERROR | sed -E 's/^psql:[^ ]* //' | cut -c1-120)"
echo "cascade: $(psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -c 'BEGIN' -c 'ALTER TABLE public.child_safety_reports DROP CONSTRAINT child_safety_reports_reporter_user_id_fkey, ADD CONSTRAINT child_safety_reports_reporter_user_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (reporter_user_id) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE' -f $C 2>&1 | grep ERROR | sed -E 's/^psql:[^ ]* //' | cut -c1-120)"
psql -X -d attune_spec3a -Atc "select count(*) from information_schema.columns where table_name = 'child_safety_reports' and column_name = 'reported_user_id'"
psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f $C 2>&1 | tail -1
rm $D/mut_*.sql
```

- `tells_someone` (nothing tells the reported person): `ERROR:  CONTRACT VIOLATED: report_child_safety_concern reaches beyond the report (nothing may tell or find anyone)`
- `client_read`: `ERROR:  CONTRACT VIOLATED: child_safety_reports grants are wrong (no client read or write)`
- `reported_column`: `ERROR:  CONTRACT VIOLATED: child_safety_reports has columns beyond the spec (nothing may name the reported person): …`

then `0` and `NOTICE:  child_safety_contracts: all held`.

import 'package:attune/core/utils/constants.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';

import '../../../helpers/home_harness.dart';

class _Server {
  final sent = <Map<String, dynamic>>[];
  Object? fail;

  Future<Object?> rpc(String fn, Map<String, dynamic> params) async {
    expect(fn, 'report_child_safety_concern');
    final error = fail;
    if (error != null) throw error;
    sent.add(params);
    return null;
  }
}

List<Override> _overrides(_Server server, {String path = 'just_me', bool enrolled = false}) => [
  childReportServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(ChildReportService(server.rpc)),
  homeAuthProvider.overrideWithValue(FakeHomeAuth(testUser('u-report'))),
  homeStateProvider.overrideWith(() => _Home(homeJson(path: path, datingEnrolled: enrolled))),
];

class _Home extends HomeStateNotifier {
  _Home(this.json);

  final Map<String, dynamic> json;

  @override
  HomeSnapshot build(String userId) => HomeSnapshot.fromServer(HomeState.fromJson(json));
}

Future<FlowHarness> _pump(WidgetTester tester, _Server server, {String path = 'just_me', bool enrolled = false}) =>
    pumpFlowScreen(
      tester,
      () => const ReportChildConcernScreen(),
      overrides: _overrides(server, path: path, enrolled: enrolled),
    );

void main() {
  testWidgets('the words and the place go to the RPC; then the thanks', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server();
    await _pump(tester, server);
    expect(find.text('Report a concern about a child'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.textContaining('A person on our team reads every report.'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.textContaining('call your local emergency number'), findsOneWidget);
    final send = find.widgetWithText(FilledButton, 'Send report');
    expect(tester.widget<FilledButton>(send).onPressed, isNull, reason: 'nothing to send yet');
    await tester.tap(find.text('Stories'));
    await tester.enterText(find.byType(TextField), '  A grown-up asked a child to message privately.  ');
    await tester.pump();
    expect(find.text('50 of 2000'), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Send report'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(server.sent.single, {
      'p_where': 'stories',
      'p_details': 'A grown-up asked a child to message privately.',
    });
    expect(find.text("Thank you. We've received your report."), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('no place is fine', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server();
    await _pump(tester, server);
    await tester.enterText(find.byType(TextField), 'Saw it');
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Send report'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(server.sent.single['p_where'], isNull);
  });

  testWidgets('no "Dating" on the Relationship path (D20)', (tester) async {
    await _pump(tester, _Server(), path: 'relationship');
    expect(find.text('Dating'), findsNothing);
    expect(find.text('Somewhere else'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('"Dating" on the Dating path', (tester) async {
    await _pump(tester, _Server(), path: 'dating');
    expect(find.text('Dating'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('"Dating" for someone enrolled in it', (tester) async {
    await _pump(tester, _Server(), path: 'just_me', enrolled: true);
    expect(find.text('Dating'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('the daily limit and a failure are said, and the words kept', (tester) async {
    final server = _Server()
      ..fail = const PostgrestException(message: 'child_report:limit', code: 'P0001');
    await _pump(tester, server);
    await tester.enterText(find.byType(TextField), 'More to say');
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Send report'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(
      find.text("You've sent 5 reports today. To tell us more, email ${AppConstants.childSafetyEmail}."),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    expect(find.text('More to say'), findsOneWidget);
    server.fail = Exception('offline');
    await tester.tap(find.text('Send report'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text("We couldn't send this. Check your connection and try again."), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('More to say'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('guidelines and goldens', (tester) async {
    await _pump(tester, _Server());
    await expectFlowGuidelines(tester);
    await expectFlowGoldens(
      tester,
      'report_child_concern',
      () => const ReportChildConcernScreen(),
      overrides: _overrides(_Server()),
    );
  });
}

- [ ] **Step 4: The service, the screen, the rows, the route and the address**

Create `lib/features/safety/data/child_report_service.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

/// Where the person saw it (Spec 1 §3.15), as child_safety_reports stores it.
enum ChildReportPlace {
  chat('chat'),
  stories('stories'),
  opinions('opinions'),
  dating('dating'),
  other('other');

  const ChildReportPlace(this.wire);

  final String wire;
}

/// report_child_safety_concern's answer.
enum ChildReportOutcome {
  sent,

  /// `child_report:limit`: five today already.
  limit,

  /// Offline, a timeout, or anything the server refused otherwise; the
  /// words stay on the screen to send again.
  failed,
}

typedef ChildReportRpc =
    Future<Object?> Function(String fn, Map<String, dynamic> params);

/// "Report a concern about a child" (Spec 1 W6c): one call, nothing else.
/// Nobody is named, and nothing tells anyone but the team.
class ChildReportService {
  ChildReportService(this._rpc);

  final ChildReportRpc _rpc;

  static const timeout = Duration(seconds: 20);

  Future<ChildReportOutcome> send({
    required ChildReportPlace? place,
    required String details,
  }) async {
    try {
      await _rpc('report_child_safety_concern', {
        'p_where': place?.wire,
        'p_details': details.trim(),
      }).timeout(timeout);
      return ChildReportOutcome.sent;
    } on PostgrestException catch (error) {
      return error.message.startsWith('child_report:limit')
          ? ChildReportOutcome.limit
          : ChildReportOutcome.failed;
    } catch (_) {
      // Nothing is logged: the safety features never write to the device
      // log (test/features/safety/safety_privacy_scan_test.dart).
      return ChildReportOutcome.failed;
    }
  }
}

final childReportServiceProvider = Provider<ChildReportService>(
  (ref) => ChildReportService(
    (fn, params) => Supabase.instance.client.rpc(fn, params: params),
  ),
);
```

Create `lib/features/safety/presentation/screens/report_child_concern_screen.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/app/routing/app_router.dart' show RouteNames;
import 'package:attune/core/utils/constants.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_buttons.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_frame.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_text_field.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/safety/data/child_report_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/settings/models/settings_config.dart';
import 'package:attune/home/home_seams.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/semantics.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

/// Settings -> Safety, and Settings -> Support (Spec 1 §3.15, W6c): the
/// same screen from both, so it is found from either.
SettingsConfig childReportSetting(
  BuildContext context, {
  required String id,
  required int order,
}) {
  final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
  return SettingsConfig(
    id: id,
    title: loc.support_childReportTitle,
    subtitle: loc.support_childReportSubtitle,
    icon: Icons.child_care_outlined,
    type: SettingsItemType.navigation,
    routeName: RouteNames.reportChildConcern,
    onTap: () => context.push(RouteNames.reportChildConcern),
    iconColor: Colors.red,
    order: order,
  );
}

/// "Report a concern about a child" (Spec 1 §3.15, W6c). A person on the
/// team reads every report; the reported person is never told, and the
/// form never asks who it is. Never animated (§2.2); the app shell's quick
/// exit sits above it.
class ReportChildConcernScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const ReportChildConcernScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<ReportChildConcernScreen> createState() =>
      _ReportChildConcernScreenState();
}

class _ReportChildConcernScreenState
    extends ConsumerState<ReportChildConcernScreen> {
  static const maxLength = 2000;

  final _details = TextEditingController();
  ChildReportPlace? _place;
  bool _busy = false;
  bool _sent = false;
  String? _error;

  AppLocalizations get _loc => AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _details.addListener(() => setState(() {}));
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _details.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  /// "Dating" only for someone who uses it (D20: nothing about Dating on
  /// the Relationship path, and nothing kept on the phone).
  bool _showsDating() {
    final userId = ref.read(homeAuthProvider).currentUser?.id;
    if (userId == null) return false;
    final home = ref.watch(homeStateProvider(userId));
    return home.path == AppPath.dating || (home.dating?.enrolled ?? false);
  }

  String _placeLabel(ChildReportPlace place) => switch (place) {
    ChildReportPlace.chat => _loc.support_childReportChat,
    ChildReportPlace.stories => _loc.support_childReportStories,
    ChildReportPlace.opinions => _loc.support_childReportOpinions,
    ChildReportPlace.dating => _loc.support_childReportDating,
    ChildReportPlace.other => _loc.support_childReportElsewhere,
  };

  void _leave() {
    if (context.canPop()) {
      context.pop();
    } else {
      context.go(RouteNames.settings);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _send() async {
    if (_busy || _details.text.trim().isEmpty) return;
    setState(() {
      _busy = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    final outcome = await ref
        .read(childReportServiceProvider)
        .send(place: _place, details: _details.text);
    if (!mounted) return;
    final loc = _loc;
    setState(() {
      _busy = false;
      switch (outcome) {
        case ChildReportOutcome.sent:
          _sent = true;
          _details.clear();
          _place = null;
        case ChildReportOutcome.limit:
          _error = loc.support_childReportLimit(AppConstants.childSafetyEmail);
        case ChildReportOutcome.failed:
          _error = loc.support_childReportFailed;
      }
    });
    SemanticsService.announce(
      _sent ? loc.support_childReportThanks : _error!,
      Directionality.of(context),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = _loc;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final textStyle = theme.textTheme.bodyLarge?.copyWith(
      color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
    );
    if (_sent) {
      return FlowFrame(
        title: loc.support_childReportTitle,
        onBack: _leave,
        body: Semantics(
          liveRegion: true,
          child: Text(loc.support_childReportThanks, style: textStyle),
        ),
        actions: [
          FlowPrimaryButton(label: loc.support_childReportDone, onPressed: _leave),
        ],
      );
    }
    final places = [
      for (final place in ChildReportPlace.values)
        if (place != ChildReportPlace.dating || _showsDating()) place,
    ];
    final error = _error;
    return FlowFrame(
      title: loc.support_childReportTitle,
      onBack: _leave,
      body: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Text(loc.support_childReportBody, style: textStyle),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          Semantics(
            header: true,
            child: Text(
              loc.support_childReportWhere,
              style: theme.textTheme.titleSmall?.copyWith(
                color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Wrap(
            spacing: 8,
            runSpacing: 8,
            children: [
              for (final place in places)
                ChoiceChip(
                  label: Text(_placeLabel(place)),
                  selected: _place == place,
                  materialTapTargetSize: MaterialTapTargetSize.padded,
                  onSelected: (selected) {
                    HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
                    setState(() => _place = selected ? place : null);
                  },
                ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          TextField(
            controller: _details,
            minLines: 4,
            maxLines: 8,
            keyboardType: TextInputType.multiline,
            textInputAction: TextInputAction.newline,
            inputFormatters: [LengthLimitingTextInputFormatter(maxLength)],
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              labelText: loc.support_childReportDetails,
              alignLabelWithHint: true,
              border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
            ),
          ),
          if (error != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            // Read out by _send (SemanticsService), once per failure.
            FlowErrorLine(text: error, style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Align(
            alignment: AlignmentDirectional.centerEnd,
            child: Text(
              loc.support_childReportCount(_details.text.characters.length, maxLength),
              style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      actions: [
        FlowPrimaryButton(
          label: loc.support_childReportSend,
          busy: _busy,
          onPressed: _details.text.trim().isEmpty ? null : _send,
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
```

## Owner deploy checklist (the plan never runs any of this)

1. **Order.** W1-W5 are deployed first (their checklists). Push the W6a migrations in order (`20261103100000`, `…100010`, `…100100`, `…100110`, `…100200`, `…100210`), then deploy the three functions, then set the secrets, then (last) enable the hook. W6b's (`20261103110000`, `…110010`) and W6c's (`20261103120000`, `…120010`) migrations go before any build that calls their RPCs reaches a tester; `identity-health-alert` is redeployed with W6c's source.
2. **Server.** `supabase db push` applies the ten files. Afterwards, in the SQL editor:
   - `select has_function_privilege('supabase_auth_admin', 'public.attune_access_token_hook(jsonb)', 'EXECUTE'), has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'public.attune_access_token_hook(jsonb)', 'EXECUTE'), has_function_privilege('service_role', 'public.attune_access_token_hook(jsonb)', 'EXECUTE');` → `t | f | f`
   - `select has_table_privilege('authenticated', 'public.users', 'DELETE'), has_table_privilege('authenticated', 'public.child_safety_reports', 'SELECT'), has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'public.report_child_safety_concern(text,text)', 'EXECUTE'), has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'public.correct_my_birth_date(date)', 'EXECUTE');` → `f | f | t | t`
   - `select count(*) from public.account_activity;` → about the number of `public.users` rows (the P-9 seed).
   - `select jobname, schedule from cron.job where jobname in ('fresh-start-retention', 'purge-recovery-holds', 'identity-health-alert');` → the three, `43 * * * *`, `55 3 * * *`, `*/15 * * * *`.
   - `select public.get_identity_health();` (service role) → the W1 keys plus `fresh_starts_7d`, `parked_accounts`, `parked_without_hmac`, `hook_fail_opens_24h`, `hook_fail_opens_unalerted`, `fresh_starts_unparked`, `parked_kept_for_review`, `recovery_purge_stuck`, `fresh_start_key_present` and `child_safety_reports_unalerted` (reports the webhook hasn't been told about 15 minutes after they came in; it must read `0` once `SAFETY_ALERT_WEBHOOK` is set).
   All were checked on the scratch copy of `attune_spec3a`.
3. **Secrets and functions.**
   - Vault: `select vault.create_secret('<48+ random characters>', 'fresh_start_key');` (P-7; generate with a password manager; never reuse another key). Then `fresh_start_key_present` reads `true`. `dating_exclusion_key` must already be set (W1/Spec 2), or `parked_without_hmac` counts every fresh start.
   - Function secret `SAFETY_ALERT_WEBHOOK` (a destination the owner configures, e.g. a private Slack channel's incoming webhook read by the safety owner). `OTP_HMAC_KEY` exists from W2c and is reused by `start-fresh-account` for the IP hash.
   - `supabase functions deploy start-fresh-account purge-recovery-holds identity-health-alert` (`start-fresh-account` with `verify_jwt = false`, from `config.toml`).
   - Smoke: `curl -s -X POST <project>/functions/v1/start-fresh-account -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"nonce":"x"}'` → `{"error":"fresh_start:invalid"}` (400).
   - **Safety webhook delivers, end to end (final review #3; development project, after W6c's migrations and `identity-health-alert` are deployed):** from a test account send one report (Settings -> Safety -> "Report a concern about a child"), wait one cron tick (`*/15`), confirm the message arrived at `SAFETY_ALERT_WEBHOOK`'s destination (a count and a time only), and that `select public.get_identity_health();` reads `child_safety_reports_unalerted` `0` and `hook_fail_opens_unalerted` `0`; then delete the test row (service role: `delete from public.child_safety_reports where id = '<test id>';`). If nothing arrived, fix the webhook before production: a dead webhook only shows as `post_failed`/`not_configured` in the function's logs.
   - **JWT expiry ≤ 3600 s (final review #3):** Authentication -> Settings (JWT expiry) on both projects reads at most `3600`; "a held or parked account's sessions stop within the hour" depends on it (the hook refuses the next refresh). `config.toml` has no `[auth]` section, so nothing in the repo sets it.
4. **The Custom Access Token Hook (development project first; review F-2).**
   - Confirm in the dashboard that the plan offers both Auth hooks (Send SMS and Custom Access Token).
   - Right before enabling the hook on each project (development, then production), re-run 11b's `account_activity` seed (the `INSERT INTO public.account_activity AS a … ON CONFLICT … greatest(…)` statement from `20261103100100_access_token_hook.sql`, preflight F7). It is idempotent and never moves a value back; it covers accounts that were active only between `db push` and the hook going on.
   - Authentication -> Hooks -> Custom Access Token: Postgres function `public.attune_access_token_hook`. Enable on the **development** project.
   - Check 1 (F-2): place a hold on a test account (`select public.place_account_hold('<id>', 'safety_review');`), sign in by code on a phone: the app shows H1 ("We need to check your account"), not "That code didn't work"; with `age_review` instead, H1 and the phone's under-18 block (Welcome's "Join Attune" opens X1) — the test account must have been used in the last 180 days; an age-held account idle 180 days answers plain `account_review` at a code sign-in (H1, no block; final review I-1). Resolve the hold afterwards (`update public.account_holds set resolved_at = now(), resolved_by = 'test' where user_id = '<id>';`).
   - Check 2 (F-2, the recycled number): `update public.account_activity set last_active_at = now() - interval '200 days' where user_id = '<test id>';`, sign in by code on another phone: DM1 shows; "I'm new to Attune" -> DM2 -> a new code -> A5 -> a new account; the old account is unreadable (`select phone, phone_released_at, parked_phone_hmac is not null from public.users where id = '<old id>';` → `999…`, a time, `t`), and its old phone is signed out within the hour (the refresh is refused). Then (B3 staging check) the old account's Auth identity no longer holds the real number: `select count(*) from auth.identities where provider = 'phone' and user_id = '<old id>' and identity_data->>'phone' = '<real number, digits as Auth stores it>';` → `0`, and the same query with the **new** account's id → `1` (the next code to the real number signed in the new account, never the old one).
   - Check 3: Auth's logs show `authentication_method` `otp` for a code sign-in (the hook only challenges `otp`).
   - Check 4: `select public.identity_alerts_due();` → `count` 0 (no fail-open during the checks). If not 0, read `private.auth_hook_failures`' `sqlstate` and fix before production.
   - Production: the same, only after the W6a gate (below) and the same run passes on the production project; then `select public.record_flag_gate_evidence('dormancy_rule', '<review ref>');` and `update public.feature_flags set enabled = true where key = 'dormancy_rule';` (holds apply from the moment the hook is enabled, whatever the flag).
   - Enable `dormancy_rule` only with `flow_signin_v2` on. The legacy copy can't offer the fresh start. (If `flow_signin_v2` is ever switched off, switch `dormancy_rule` off too; B4 review #3.)
5. **Stores (W6b gate).** **First (review B6 N-3): add the Declared Age Range capability to the App ID (`com.barsopus.attune`) in the Apple Developer portal, regenerate the provisioning profiles, then add the entitlement line back to `ios/Runner/Runner.entitlements` (`<key>com.apple.developer.declared-age-range</key>` `<true/>`, after `aps-environment`).** Until then the line is left out so signed builds (dev included) keep provisioning; the weak-linked, availability-guarded reader answers `unavailable` without it and the date of birth decides. Play Console: content rating answered for an 18+ social and dating app; **Restrict Minor Access** on; Age signals page left on the default ranges (the app reads 18+ only). App Store Connect: age rating 18+; request the **Declared Age Range** entitlement (`com.apple.developer.declared-age-range`; left out of `Runner.entitlements` until the capability is on the App ID, see the first line of this item); keep `platform_age_signals` off in production until the entitlement line is back and a signed release build shows Apple's sheet. Device checks in **release** builds: an iOS 26 phone in a listed storefront (`USA`, `BRA` today, `app_public_config.age_signal_storefronts`) shows Apple's sheet at A2 and a minor answer stops at X1; an iOS 18 phone shows nothing and A2 works; an Android phone installed from Play: A2 works whatever Play answers. Confirm on a Play-installed device with a parent-supervised Google account that Play reports `ageRangeSource` `TIER_B` for a parent-set range (preflight F14: `javap` shows only the constant, not its meaning; the app reads `TIER_B` as "supervised"). Then `select public.record_flag_gate_evidence('platform_age_signals', '<ref>');` and switch it on.
6. **Child safety (W6c gate).**
   - The address is `childsafety@barsopus.com` (owner ruling; built as `AppConstants.childSafetyEmail`, shown in the daily-limit message). A **named** child-safety contact is still needed: name the person (with a deputy) and confirm `childsafety@barsopus.com` and `support@barsopus.com` reach them (P-30); they read `child_safety_reports` (service role, SQL editor) at least daily and on every webhook post, and set `status`/`reviewed_at`.
   - Ask privacy counsel for the retention rule (P-25: "2 years after closing, unless a legal hold"); a purge job follows that ruling.
   - Publish the CSAE standards page on barsopus.com and link it in Play Console (Policy -> App content -> Child safety standards). Draft (§8.1), for the owner's and counsel's edit:
     > **Attune child safety standards.** Attune has zero tolerance for child sexual abuse and exploitation (CSAE). **What is prohibited:** child sexual abuse material (CSAM), grooming, sextortion of minors, and any sexualised content involving a minor, in any part of the app. **How Attune prevents it:** Attune is for adults only (18 and over); everyone gives a date of birth before a phone number and the server refuses anyone under 18; platform age signals (Apple Declared Age Range, Google Play Age Signals) where available; image uploads are matched against known CSAM hash lists; a person reviews every report. **How to report:** in the app, Settings -> Safety -> "Report a concern about a child"; or email childsafety@barsopus.com. **What happens:** a person on our team reviews every report within 24 hours; content is removed and accounts are held or closed; we report to the authorities where the law requires it or a route exists (for example NCMEC's CyberTipline, the IWF, and Ghana's Cyber Security Authority), and preserve evidence as the law requires. **Child-safety contact:** <role>, childsafety@barsopus.com. Attune complies with the child-safety laws of every country where it operates.
7. **Reviews before production (gates).** W6a: the DV specialist reviews DM1, DM2 and H1, with P-29's DM1 lines ("We couldn't reach Attune…", "Too many tries…", "That took too long…"), and answers whether DM1 should show a visible "Not now" (B5 review I-2: today system back leaves DM1 neutrally and nothing on screen says so); record `record_flag_gate_evidence('dormancy_rule', …)`. W6b: the store items above. W6c: the CSAE page and the contact above.
8. **Owner decisions to confirm** (the plan built the recommendation): P-2 (the sealed challenge; health counts fresh starts), P-5 (`account_review:age`; plain `account_review` for a code sign-in to an age-held account idle 180 days, final review I-1), P-24 (the child-safety webhook post), P-26 ("call your local emergency number" until W7a), P-30 (the two addresses).
9. **Support runbook (W6).** Add these lines to the support runbook before the hook is enabled in production:
   - **Recycled number behind an old hold (B1 review M-3; final review I-1).** A number whose old account has an unresolved age or safety hold shows H1 to the number's new owner (holds come before dormancy, so a held account can't be fresh-started away). **The new owner's phone is not blocked:** a code sign-in to an age-held account idle 180 days answers plain `account_review` (no under-18 device block), and a safety or recovery hold never blocks. H1 on a number whose account has been dormant for 180+ days may be a recycled number: the new owner reaches support from H1; support verifies them, checks the old account's hold and resolves it, or moves the number off the held account (or purges the old account).
   - **Fresh-start park alert (preflight F4; `fresh_start_unparked`).** The fresh start consumed the challenge but the old account's Auth number was never moved. Finish the park in this order: move the old account's Auth phone to its `+999…` number first (as `start-fresh-account` does), then call `select public.mark_fresh_start_parked('<old id>');`. Never mark before the move.
   - **Stuck-park alert can mean only the mark failed (B3 review M-4).** Before moving anything, check the old account's Auth phone: if it is already `+999…`, only `mark_fresh_start_parked` is left to run.
   - **Parked account kept for a review / purge stuck** (`parked_kept_for_review`, `recovery_purge_stuck`): resolve the open review, or find the purge failure in `account_deletion_requests` (`source = 'recovery_purge'`).
   - **Child-safety report alert** (`child_safety_report`): the named contact reads `child_safety_reports` in the SQL editor (service role) the same day and sets `status`/`reviewed_at`. The webhook post carries a count and a time only; nothing is sent to the reported person, ever. If `get_identity_health()`'s `child_safety_reports_unalerted` is above `0`, the webhook isn't reaching anyone: read the reports directly and fix `SAFETY_ALERT_WEBHOOK`.
